      <!-- Result -->
      <div id="scanResult" class="hidden" style="margin-top:14px;padding:12px;background:var(--gglow);border:1px solid rgba(15,159,118,.2);border-radius:var(--r2);text-align:center">
        <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:var(--gn1);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;margin-bottom:4px">Scanned</div>
        <div style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)" id="scanResultCode"></div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t2);margin-top:6px" id="scanResultName"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

  const a=new Uint8Array(12); crypto.getRandomValues(a);
  return Array.from(a).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
})();

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   TOAST
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function toast(msg,type='in'){
  const icons={ok:'check_circle',er:'error_outline',in:'info',wa:'warning_amber'};
  const safeMsg=(!msg||msg==='undefined'||msg==='null')?
    (type==='er'?'Something went wrong — check your connection':'Done'):
    String(msg);
  const t=document.createElement('div');
  t.className='toast '+type;
  t.innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round">'+(icons[type]||'info')+'</span><span>'+safeMsg+'</span>';
  $('twrap').appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(()=>{t.style.opacity='0';t.style.transform='translateX(16px)';setTimeout(()=>t.remove(),280)},3200);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   MODAL
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function showModal(title,html,btns=[]){
  $('gmTit').innerText=title;
  $('gmBd').innerHTML=html;
  const f=$('gmFt'); f.innerHTML='';
  btns.forEach(b=>{
    const el=document.createElement('button');
    el.className=`btn ${b.c||'btn-s'}`; el.innerHTML=b.label; el.onclick=b.fn;
    f.appendChild(el);
  });
  openOV('genOV');
}
const closeModal=()=>closeOV('genOV');
function esc(s){
  return String(s==null?'':s)
    .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g,'&#39;');
}
function authWrap(fn){
  return function(r){
    if(r&&r.unauthorized===true){
      toast('Session expired — please sign in again','er');
      _sessionToken=null; user={};
      $('appShell').classList.add('hidden');
      $('loginScreen').classList.remove('hidden');
      $('lgCode').value='';
      return;
    }
    if(fn) fn(r);
  };
}
function openOV(id){
  $(id).classList.add('on');
  // genOV always appears on top of everything
  if(id==='genOV') $(id).style.zIndex='9000';
}
function closeOV(id){$(id).classList.remove('on')}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   ANIMATED COUNTER
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function countUp(el, end, prefix='₦', dur=650){
  const n=parseFloat(String(end).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,''))||0;
  const start=performance.now();
  (function tick(now){
    const p=Math.min((now-start)/dur,1);
    const ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,4);
    const cur=Math.round(n*ease);
    el.textContent=prefix===''?cur:(prefix+cur.toLocaleString());
    if(p<1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    else el.textContent=typeof end==='string'?end:prefix+n.toLocaleString();
  })(start);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   CANVAS SPARKLINE
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function drawSparkline(canvas, data, color='#2254e0'){
  if(!canvas||!data||data.length<2) return;
  const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height;
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
  const min=Math.min(...data),max=Math.max(...data);
  const range=max-min||1;
  const pts=data.map((v,i)=>({x:i*(w/(data.length-1)),y:h-((v-min)/range)*(h*0.8)-h*0.1}));
  // gradient fill
  const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h);
  grad.addColorStop(0,color+'33');
  grad.addColorStop(1,color+'00');
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x,h);
  pts.forEach(p=>ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y));
  ctx.lineTo(pts[pts.length-1].x,h);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle=grad;
  ctx.fill();
  // line
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x,pts[0].y);
  pts.forEach(p=>ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y));
  ctx.strokeStyle=color;
  ctx.lineWidth=1.8;
  ctx.lineJoin='round';
  ctx.stroke();
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   DONUT CHART
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function drawDonut(canvas,data,colors){
  if(!canvas||!data.length) return;
  const size=canvas.width;
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,size,size);
  const total=data.reduce((s,d)=>s+d,0)||1;
  const cx=size/2,cy=size/2,r=size/2-6,inner=r*.58;
  let startAngle=-Math.PI/2;
  data.forEach((v,i)=>{
    const slice=(v/total)*Math.PI*2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx,cy);
    ctx.arc(cx,cy,r,startAngle,startAngle+slice);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle=colors[i%colors.length];
    ctx.fill();
    startAngle+=slice;
  });
  // hole
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx,cy,inner,0,Math.PI*2);
  ctx.fillStyle=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--s0').trim()||'#fff';
  ctx.fill();
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   LOGIN
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function doLogin(){
  const code=$('lgCode').value.trim();
  if(!code) return toast('Enter your access code','er');
  $('lgBtn').disabled=true;
  $('lgBtn').innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:16px">sync</span> Signing in…';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(u=>{
      $('lgBtn').disabled=false;
      $('lgBtn').innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:17px">lock_open</span> Sign In';
      if(!u){
        $('lgErr').innerText='Incorrect PIN — please try again';
        $('lgErr').classList.remove('hidden');
        $('lgCode').value='';
        $('lgCode').focus();
        setTimeout(()=>$('lgErr').classList.add('hidden'),4000);
        return;
      }
      if(u.locked){
        $('lgErr').innerText=u.msg||'Too many failed attempts — please wait 15 minutes';
        $('lgErr').classList.remove('hidden');
        $('lgCode').value='';
        $('lgBtn').disabled=true;
        $('lgBtn').innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:16px">lock</span> Locked';
        setTimeout(()=>{
          $('lgErr').classList.add('hidden');
          $('lgBtn').disabled=false;
          $('lgBtn').innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:17px">lock_open</span> Sign In';
        }, 60000); // re-enable UI after 1 min (user still needs to wait server-side)
        return;
      }
      user=u; $('lgCode').value=''; setupUser();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err=>{
      $('lgBtn').disabled=false;
      $('lgBtn').innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:17px">lock_open</span> Sign In';
      toast('Login failed — check your connection','er');
    })
    .checkCode({code, nonce:_loginNonce});
}

function setupUser(){

  _sessionToken = user.sessionToken || null

  $('loginScreen').classList.add('hidden');
  $('appShell').classList.remove('hidden');
  $('sbAv').innerText=user.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase();
  $('sbName').innerText=user.name;
  $('sbRole').innerText=user.store+' · '+(user.role.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+user.role.slice(1));

  const can=p=>user.permissions&&user.permissions.includes(p);

  // Sidebar nav visibility
  const navMap={
    'nav-dashboard':  can('dashboard_ops')||can('dashboard_finance'),
    'nav-stock':      can('stock'),
    'nav-expense':    can('expenses'),
    'nav-activity':   true,
    'nav-updatePrice':can('prices'),
    'nav-records':    can('settings'),
    'nav-transfer':   user.isAdmin||user.isManager,
    'nav-barcode':    can('barcode'),
    'nav-attendance': can('staff_mgmt')||can('attendance_view'),
    'nav-customers':  can('customers')||user.isManager||user.isAdmin,
    'nav-settings':   can('settings'),
    'newProdBtn':     can('settings'),
  };
  Object.entries(navMap).forEach(([id,show])=>{
    const el=$(id);
    if(el) show?el.classList.remove('hidden'):el.classList.add('hidden');
  });

  // Store selector
  if(user.availableStores&&user.availableStores.length>0){
    const s=$('storeSel'); s.innerHTML='';
    user.availableStores.forEach(st=>{
      const o=new Option(st+(st===user.store?' ✓':''),st);
      if(st===user.store) o.selected=true;
      s.add(o);
    });
    $('sswrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  }

  // Start notifications based on role
  if(can('staff_mgmt')||can('approve_returns')){
    loadPendingReturns();
  }
  if(!can('approve_returns')&&can('returns')){
    pollStaffNotifications();
  }
  // Poll notifications every 5 minutes instead of 15 seconds
  setInterval(pollNotifications, 300000);

  // Load products, expense types, notifications
  loadProds();
  loadExpTypes();
  // #19: load VAT config for EVERY user (staff can't read full hub settings) so VAT applies at checkout
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(v=>{
      if(v && !v.unauthorized){
        try{
          const s=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('hubSettings_'+user.name)||'{}');
          s.vat_enabled=v.vat_enabled; s.vat_rate=v.vat_rate; s.vat_base=v.vat_base;
          sessionStorage.setItem('hubSettings_'+user.name,JSON.stringify(s));
        }catch(e){}
      }
    })
    .getVatSettings(_sessionToken);
  // Preload everything in the background
  _backgroundPreload();
  loadNotifs();
  updateNotifBadge();
  // Pre-load activity so transactions are ready
  if(user){
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
        myTxns=[];
        if(data&&data.transactions&&data.transactions.length){
          data.transactions.forEach(t=>{
            if(!t.time&&t.date) t.time=t.date;
            myTxns.push(t);
          });
        }
      })
      .getSalespersonActivity({soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:null,days:14,_token:_sessionToken});
  }

  // Restore clock state
  const saved=localStorage.getItem('clockIn_'+user.name);
  if(saved){
    _clockIn=new Date(saved);
    $('clockStatus').innerText='Clocked In';
    $('clockStatus').style.color='var(--gn1)';
    $('clockInfo').innerText='Since '+_clockIn.toLocaleTimeString('en-NG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
    $('clockInBtn').classList.add('hidden');
    $('clockOutBtn').classList.remove('hidden');
    _clockTimer=setInterval(()=>{
      const diff=Date.now()-_clockIn.getTime();
      const h=Math.floor(diff/3600000);
      const m=Math.floor((diff%3600000)/60000);
      const s=Math.floor((diff%60000)/1000);
      $('clockTimer').innerText=(h?h+'h ':'')+m+'m '+s+'s';
    },1000);
  }

  // Always start on sales page
  goPage('sales');

  // Login popup only if new permissions were assigned since last login
  setTimeout(()=>showLoginTasksPopup(),800);
}

function showLoginTasksPopup(){
  const can=p=>user.permissions&&user.permissions.includes(p);
  const allPerms=user.permissions||[];

  // Compare with last saved permissions
  const storageKey='lastPerms_'+user.name;
  const lastPerms=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey)||'[]');
  const newPerms=allPerms.filter(p=>!lastPerms.includes(p));

  // Save current permissions for next login
  sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey,JSON.stringify(allPerms));

  // Only show popup if there are newly assigned permissions
  if(!newPerms.length) return;

  const permLabels={
    stock:{icon:'inventory_2',label:'Stock Management',color:'var(--b1)'},
    expenses:{icon:'receipt_long',label:'Expenses',color:'var(--am1)'},
    prices:{icon:'sell',label:'Price Updates',color:'var(--gn1)'},
    approve_returns:{icon:'assignment_return',label:'Pending Returns to Review',color:'var(--rd1)'},
    staff_mgmt:{icon:'group',label:'Staff Management',color:'var(--b1)'},
    barcode:{icon:'qr_code_2',label:'Barcode Generator',color:'var(--t2)'},
    activity:{icon:'timeline',label:'Activity View',color:'var(--gn1)'},
    dashboard_ops:{icon:'dashboard',label:'Dashboard',color:'var(--b1)'},
    dashboard_finance:{icon:'payments',label:'Financial Dashboard',color:'var(--gn1)'},
  };

  const tasks=newPerms.map(p=>permLabels[p]).filter(Boolean);
  if(!tasks.length) return;

  openOV('genOV');
  $('gmTit').innerHTML=`🎉 New Access Granted`;
  $('gmBd').innerHTML=`
    <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t2);margin-bottom:14px">You have been given access to the following new features:</p>
    <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
      ${tasks.map(t=>`
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px 13px;background:var(--s1);border:1px solid var(--bdr);border-radius:var(--r2)">
          <span class="material-icons-round" style="color:${t.color};font-size:20px">${t.icon}</span>
          <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${t.label}</span>
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
  `;
  $('gmFt').innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-p btn-fw" onclick="closeModal()">
      <span class="material-icons-round">check</span>Got it!
    </button>
  `;
}

function toggleSB(){
  sbOpen=!sbOpen;
  $('sidebar').classList.toggle('exp',sbOpen);
  $('sbIco').innerText=sbOpen?'chevron_left':'chevron_right';
}
function toggleTheme(){
  document.body.classList.toggle('dark');
  const dark=document.body.classList.contains('dark');
  $('thIco').innerText=dark?'light_mode':'dark_mode';
  if($('thLbl')) $('thLbl').innerText=dark?'Disable':'Enable';
}

function goPage(id){
  const can=p=>user&&user.permissions&&user.permissions.includes(p);
  const pagePerms={
    dashboard: ()=>can('dashboard_ops')||can('dashboard_finance'),
    stock:     ()=>can('stock'),
    expense:   ()=>can('expenses'),
    activity:  ()=>true,
    updatePrice:()=>can('prices'),
    barcode:   ()=>can('barcode'),
    attendance:()=>can('staff_mgmt')||can('attendance_view'),
    settings:  ()=>can('settings'),
    sales:     ()=>can('sales'),
    returns:   ()=>can('returns'),
    customers: ()=>can('customers'),
  };
  if(pagePerms[id]&&!pagePerms[id]()){
    toast('You do not have permission to access this page','er');
    return;
  }

  ['sales','stock','expense','activity','dashboard','updatePrice','returns','transfer','barcode','attendance','settings','customers'].forEach(p=>{
    $('page-'+p)?.classList.add('hidden');
    $('nav-'+p)?.classList.remove('act');
  });
  $('page-'+id)?.classList.remove('hidden');
  $('nav-'+id)?.classList.add('act');

  const fn={
    sales:initSales,
    stock:initStock,
    expense:()=>{ $('expDate').value=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]; },
    activity:()=>{ if(myTxns.length){ _actPage=1; renderActivity(); } else { loadActivity(); } },
    dashboard:()=>{ populateDashStoreFilter(); if(_dashData){ renderDash(_dashData,$('dashFrom').value||'',$('dashTo').value||''); } else { loadDash(); } },
    updatePrice:initPrice,
    returns:initReturns,
    transfer:initTransfer,
    barcode:initBarcode,
    attendance:initAttendancePage,
    settings:initSettingsPage,
    customers:initCustomers
  };
  fn[id]?.();
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   STORE SWITCH
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function switchStore(){
  const s=$('storeSel').value; if(!s||s===user.store) return;
  user.store=s;
  $('sbRole').innerText=s+(user.isAdmin?' · Admin':'');
  Array.from($('storeSel').options).forEach(o=>o.text=o.value+(o.value===s?' ✓':''));
  cart=[]; myTxns=[]; renderCart();
  initSales();
  if(_allProdsCache && _allProdsCache[s]){
    products=_allProdsCache[s];
    buildCatBar(); buildGrid(products,''); initStock();
    toast('Switched to '+s,'in');
  } else {
    loadProds();
    toast('Switched to '+s,'in');
  }
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   DATA LOADING
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
const isWB=()=>false; // legacy stub — kept to avoid breakage; always false

function _backgroundPreload(){
  // Products for all stores
  (user.availableStores||[user.store]).forEach(storeName=>{
    if(storeName===user.store) return;
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(p=>{ _allProdsCache[storeName]=Array.isArray(p)?p:[]; })
      .getProducts({store:storeName,_token:_sessionToken});
  });

  // Customer data
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(d=>{ _custDataCache=d; })
    .getCustomerData({store:null,_token:_sessionToken});

  // Inventory overview
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(inv=>{ _invOverviewCache=inv; })
    .getInventoryOverview({_token:_sessionToken});

  // Activity / transactions
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      if(data&&data.transactions) data.transactions.forEach(t=>{
        if(!t.time&&t.date) t.time=t.date;
        if(!myTxns.find(x=>x.id===t.id)) myTxns.push(t);
      });
    })
    .getSalespersonActivity({soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:null,days:14,_token:_sessionToken});

  // Inventory movement (last 30 days)
  const _to=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const _from=new Date(Date.now()-30*24*60*60*1000).toISOString().split('T')[0];
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(rows=>{
      _invMvData=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];
      _invMvLoaded=true;
    })
    .getInventoryMovement({from:_from,to:_to,_token:_sessionToken});

  // Archived sales (last 30 days)
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(rows=>{
      _archData=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];
      _archLoaded=true;
    })
    .getArchivedSales({from:_from,to:_to,_token:_sessionToken});

  // Expiry alerts
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(alerts=>{
      if(!alerts||!Array.isArray(alerts)||!alerts.length) return;
      const dismissed=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotifs_'+user.name)||'[]');
      alerts.forEach(a=>{
        const nid='EXPIRY-'+a.productID+'-'+a.store+'-'+a.expiryDate;
        if(dismissed.includes(nid)||_notifs.some(n=>n.id===nid)) return;
        addNotif(
          a.status==='expired'?'🔴 Expired Product':'🟡 Expiring Soon',
          a.name+' ('+a.store+') — '+(a.status==='expired'?'expired on '+a.expiryDate:'expires in '+a.daysToExpiry+' day'+(a.daysToExpiry!==1?'s':'')+' ('+a.expiryDate+')'),
          a.status==='expired'?'er':'wa',
          ()=>goPage('stock')
        );
        _notifs[0].id=nid;
      });
      saveNotifs(); updateNotifBadge();
    })
    .getExpiryAlerts({_token:_sessionToken});

  // Dashboard data (last 30 days)
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(d=>{
      if(d&&!d.unauthorized){
        _dashDataCache=d;
        _dashCacheFrom=_from;
        _dashCacheTo=_to;
      }
    })
    .getDashboardData({from:_from,to:_to,store:null,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function loadProds(){
  if(_allProdsCache && _allProdsCache[user.store]){
    products=_allProdsCache[user.store];
    buildCatBar(); buildGrid(products,''); initStock(); return;
  }
  products=[];
  $('pgrid').innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3">sync</span><p style="margin-top:10px;font-size:13px">Loading products…</p></div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(p=>{
      if(!p||p.unauthorized===true){
        toast('Session expired — please sign in again','er');
        _sessionToken=null; user={};
        $('appShell').classList.add('hidden');
        $('loginScreen').classList.remove('hidden');
        $('lgCode').value='';
        return;
      }
      if(!Array.isArray(p)){
        console.error('getProducts returned unexpected value:',p);
        $('pgrid').innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--rd1);font-size:13px">Unexpected response from server — check console</div>';
        return;
      }
      products=p;
      _allProdsCache[user.store]=p;
      buildCatBar();
      buildGrid(products,'');
      initStock();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{
      console.error('loadProds error:',e);
      $('pgrid').innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--rd1);font-size:13px"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:32px;display:block;margin-bottom:8px">wifi_off</span>'+(e&&e.message?e.message:'No connection — tap refresh to try again')+'</div>';
    })
    .getProducts({ store: user.store, _token: _sessionToken });
}
function refreshProds(){
  products=[];
  $('pgrid').innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3">sync</span><p style="margin-top:10px;font-size:13px">Refreshing products…</p></div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(p=>{
      products=p;
      _allProdsCache[user.store]=p;
      buildCatBar();
      buildGrid(products,'');
      initStock();
      toast('Products refreshed','ok');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(()=>{ $('pgrid').innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--rd1);font-size:13px"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:32px;display:block;margin-bottom:8px">wifi_off</span>No connection — tap refresh to try again</div>';
      toast('No connection — could not refresh','wa');
    })
    .getProducts({ store: user.store, _token: _sessionToken });
}
function loadExpTypes(){
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(t=>{
      expTypes=t;
      const s=$('expType'); s.innerHTML='<option value="">Select type</option>';
      t.forEach(x=>s.add(new Option(x,x)));
    })
    .getExpenseTypes(_sessionToken);
}
/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   SALES INIT
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function initSales(){
  $('elArea').classList.remove('hidden');
  $('undoElBtn').classList.remove('hidden');
  $('salesSub').innerText='Browse products · Add to cart';
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   CATEGORY BAR
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function buildCatBar(){
  if(!Array.isArray(products)||!products.length) return;
  const bar=$('catBar'); bar.innerHTML='';
  const cats=['All',...new Set(products.map(p=>p.category))];
  cats.forEach((c,i)=>{
    const counts=c==='All'?products.length:products.filter(p=>p.category===c).length;
    const chip=document.createElement('div');
    chip.className='chip'+(i===0?' on':'');
    chip.innerHTML=`${c}<span class="chip-cnt">${counts}</span>`;
    chip.onclick=()=>{
      bar.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
      chip.classList.add('on');
      buildGrid(products,c==='All'?'':c);
    };
    bar.appendChild(chip);
  });
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   PRODUCT GRID
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function buildGrid(prods,cat){
  const g=$('pgrid'); g.innerHTML='';
  const list=cat?prods.filter(p=>p.category===cat):prods;
  if(!list.length){
    g.innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3);font-size:13px;">No products found</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.forEach((p,i)=>{
    const oos=p.stock<=0, low=p.stock>0&&p.stock<=(p.reorder||5);
    const _hasExp=p.daysToExpiry!==null&&p.daysToExpiry!==undefined&&p.expiryDate;
    const _expired=_hasExp&&p.daysToExpiry<0;
    const _expCrit=_hasExp&&p.daysToExpiry>=0&&p.daysToExpiry<=7;
    const _expWarn=_hasExp&&p.daysToExpiry>7&&p.daysToExpiry<=14;
    const card=document.createElement('div');
    card.className='pc'+(oos?' oos':'');
    if(oos) card.style.cssText='opacity:0.28;filter:blur(0.2px)';
    if(oos) card.onclick=e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); toast('Out of stock · Cannot add to cart','er'); };
    card.style.animationDelay=`${i*0.03}s`;
    card.innerHTML=`
      ${oos?'<div class="pc-sbdg psb-out">Out of Stock</div>':low?'<div class="pc-sbdg psb-low">Low Stock</div>':''}
      ${_expired?`<div class="pc-sbdg" style="position:absolute;top:30px;right:9px;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:20px;background:var(--rglow);color:var(--rd1)">Expired</div>`:_expCrit?`<div class="pc-sbdg" style="position:absolute;top:30px;right:9px;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:20px;background:var(--rglow);color:var(--rd1)">Exp ${p.daysToExpiry}d</div>`:_expWarn?`<div class="pc-sbdg" style="position:absolute;top:30px;right:9px;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:20px;background:var(--aglow);color:var(--am1)">Exp ${p.daysToExpiry}d</div>`:''}
      <div class="pc-ico"><span class="material-icons-round">inventory_2</span></div>
      <div class="pc-n">${esc(p.name)}</div>
      <div class="pc-s">${esc(p.subCategory||'')}</div>
      <div class="pc-row">
        <div class="pc-price">₦${p.price.toLocaleString()}</div>
        <div class="pc-stk">${p.stock} left</div>
      </div>
      <div class="pc-plus"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:16px">add</span></div>
    `;
    if(!oos) card.onclick=()=>addToCart(p);
    g.appendChild(card);
  });
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   SEARCH HANDLERS
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function searchProd(){
  const q=$('elSearch').value.toLowerCase().trim(), box=$('elSR');
  if(!q){ box.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  const m=products.filter(p=>p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)).slice(0,12);
  box.innerHTML='';
  if(!m.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="sri" style="color:var(--t3)">No results</div>'; }
  m.forEach(p=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='sri';
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="sri-n">${esc(p.name)}</div><div class="sri-m">${esc(p.category)} · Stock: ${p.stock} · ₦${p.price.toLocaleString()}</div>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{ if(p.stock>0){ addToCart(p); toast(p.name+' added','ok'); } else toast('Out of stock','er'); box.classList.remove('on'); $('elSearch').value=''; };
    box.appendChild(d);
  });
  box.classList.add('on');
}
function srchStock(){
  const q=$('stockSearch').value.toLowerCase().trim(), box=$('stockSR');
  if(!q){ box.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  const src=isWB()?(window._wbs||[]):products;
  const m=src.filter(p=>p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)).slice(0,12);
  box.innerHTML='';
  m.forEach(p=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='sri';
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="sri-n">${esc(p.name)}</div><div class="sri-m">Stock: ${p.stock}</div>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{
      $('stockCat').value=p.category; onStockCat();
      setTimeout(()=>{ $('stockSub').value=p.subCategory; onStockSub();
        setTimeout(()=>{ $('stockProd').value=String(p.id); onStockProd(); },60);
      },60);
      box.classList.remove('on'); $('stockSearch').value='';
    };
    box.appendChild(d);
  });
  box.classList.toggle('on',m.length>0);
}
function srchElPrice(){
  const q=$('pSearch').value.toLowerCase().trim(), box=$('pSR');
  if(!q){ box.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  const m=products.filter(p=>p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)).slice(0,10);
  box.innerHTML='';
  m.forEach(p=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='sri';
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="sri-n">${esc(p.name)}</div><div class="sri-m">Cost: ₦${(p.cost||0).toLocaleString()} · Price: ₦${p.price.toLocaleString()}</div>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{ selElPrice(p); box.classList.remove('on'); };
    box.appendChild(d);
  });
  box.classList.toggle('on',m.length>0);
}
function srchNPC(){
  const q=$('npCat').value.toLowerCase().trim(), box=$('npCSR');
  if(!q){ box.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  const m=[...new Set(products.map(p=>p.category))].filter(c=>c.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  box.innerHTML='';
  m.forEach(c=>{ const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='sri'; d.innerHTML=`<div class="sri-n">${c}</div>`; d.onclick=()=>{ $('npCat').value=c; box.classList.remove('on'); }; box.appendChild(d); });
  box.classList.toggle('on',m.length>0);
}
function srchNPS(){
  const q=$('npSub').value.toLowerCase().trim(), box=$('npSSR');
  if(!q){ box.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  const m=[...new Set(products.map(p=>p.subCategory))].filter(c=>c.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  box.innerHTML='';
  m.forEach(c=>{ const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='sri'; d.innerHTML=`<div class="sri-n">${c}</div>`; d.onclick=()=>{ $('npSub').value=c; box.classList.remove('on'); }; box.appendChild(d); });
  box.classList.toggle('on',m.length>0);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   CART
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function addToCart(p){
  if(p.daysToExpiry!==null&&p.daysToExpiry!==undefined&&p.daysToExpiry<0){
    showModal('⚠️ Expired Product',`
      <div style="background:var(--rglow);border:1px solid rgba(224,36,36,.2);border-radius:var(--r2);padding:14px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:12px">
        <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:28px;color:var(--rd1);display:block;margin-bottom:6px">warning</span>
        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--rd1)">${esc(p.name)} expired on ${p.expiryDate}</div>
      </div>
      <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t2)">This product is past its expiry date. Do you still want to add it to the cart?</p>
    `,[
      {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
      {label:'Add Anyway',c:'btn-d',fn:()=>{ closeModal(); _doAddToCart(p); }}
    ]);
    return;
  }
  _doAddToCart(p);
}
function _doAddToCart(p){
  const ex=cart.find(i=>i.id===p.id&&!i.isWB);
  if(ex) ex.qty++; else cart.push({id:p.id,name:p.name,price:p.price,unitPrice:p.price,qty:1,isWB:false,expiryDate:p.expiryDate||''});
  renderCart(); toast(p.name+' added','ok');
}

function removeCI(i){ cart.splice(i,1); renderCart(); }
function chgQty(i,d){ cart[i].qty=Math.max(1,cart[i].qty+d); renderCart(); }
function chgPrice(i,v){ cart[i].unitPrice=Number(v)||cart[i].price; renderCart(); }
function clearCart(){ if(!cart.length) return; cart=[]; renderCart(); }
let discType='fixed';
function setDiscType(type){
  discType=type;
  $('discTypeFixed').style.background=type==='fixed'?'var(--bglow)':'var(--s1)';
  $('discTypeFixed').style.color=type==='fixed'?'var(--b1)':'var(--t3)';
  $('discTypePct').style.background=type==='pct'?'var(--bglow)':'var(--s1)';
  $('discTypePct').style.color=type==='pct'?'var(--b1)':'var(--t3)';
  $('discAmt').placeholder=type==='pct'?'0%':'0';
  updTotals();
}
function setPM(el,m){ payMethod=m; document.querySelectorAll('.pm').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('on')); el.classList.add('on'); }

function renderCart(){
  const bd=$('cartBd');
  const cnt=cart.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.qty,0);
  $('cartCnt').innerText=cnt;
  $('cartBdg').innerText=cnt;
  $('cartBdg').classList.toggle('hidden',cnt===0);
  if(!cart.length){
    bd.innerHTML='<div class="cart-empty"><span class="material-icons-round">shopping_cart_checkout</span><p>Cart is empty</p><small>Click any product to add</small></div>';
    updTotals(); return;
  }
  bd.innerHTML='';
  cart.forEach((item,idx)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='ci';
    d.innerHTML=`
      <div class="ci-top">
        <div class="ci-name">${esc(item.name)}</div>
        <button class="ci-rm" onclick="removeCI(${idx})"><span class="material-icons-round">close</span></button>
      </div>
      <div class="ci-pr">
        <span class="ci-prl">Unit ₦</span>
        <input class="ci-pin" type="number" value="${item.unitPrice}" onchange="chgPrice(${idx},this.value)" title="Edit unit price">
      </div>
      <div class="ci-bot">
        ${item.isWB?`<span style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--t3);font-style:italic">${esc(item.wbNote||'1 blind')}</span>`:`
        <div class="qty">
          <button class="qb" onclick="chgQty(${idx},-1)">−</button>
          <span class="qv">${item.qty}</span>
          <button class="qb" onclick="chgQty(${idx},1)">+</button>
        </div>`}
        <div class="ci-tot">₦${(item.unitPrice*item.qty).toLocaleString()}</div>
      </div>
    `;
    bd.appendChild(d);
  });
  updTotals();
}
function updTotals(){
  const sub=cart.reduce((s,i)=>s+(i.unitPrice*i.qty),0);
  const discVal=Number($('discAmt').value)||0;
  const disc=discType==='pct'?Math.round(sub*(discVal/100)):discVal;
  const _vs=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('hubSettings_'+user.name)||'{}');
  const _ve=_vs.vat_enabled==='true'||_vs.vat_enabled===true;
  const _vr=_ve?((parseFloat(_vs.vat_rate)||7.5)/100):0;
  const _vbase=_vs.vat_base||'after_discount';
  const vatAmt=_ve?Math.round((_vbase==='before_discount'?sub:(sub-disc))*_vr):0;
  const tot=Math.max(0,sub-disc+vatAmt);
  $('ctSub').innerText='₦'+sub.toLocaleString();
  $('ctDisc').innerText=disc>0?'-₦'+disc.toLocaleString()+(discType==='pct'?' ('+discVal+'%)':''):'—';
  $('ctVat').innerText=vatAmt>0?'+₦'+vatAmt.toLocaleString()+(vatAmt?` (${Math.round(_vr*100)}%)`:''):'—';
  $('ctVatRow').style.display=vatAmt>0?'flex':'none';
  $('ctTot').innerText='₦'+tot.toLocaleString();
  window._lastVatAmt=vatAmt;
  window._lastVatRate=Math.round(_vr*100);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   CHECKOUT + SALE PROCESSING
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function checkout(){
  if(!cart.length) return toast('Cart is empty','er');
  const sub=cart.reduce((s,i)=>s+(i.unitPrice*i.qty),0);
  const discVal=Number($('discAmt').value)||0;
  const disc=discType==='pct'?Math.round(sub*(discVal/100)):discVal;
  const tot=Math.max(0,sub-disc);
  showModal('Confirm Sale',`
    <div style="background:var(--bglow2);border:1px solid rgba(34,84,224,.18);border-radius:var(--r4);padding:16px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:12px">
      <div style="font-family:var(--f-d);font-size:27px;font-weight:900;color:var(--b1)">₦${tot.toLocaleString()}</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:3px">${cart.length} item(s) · ${payMethod}</div>
    </div>
    <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t2)">Confirm sale and print receipt?</p>
  `,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:15px">check_circle</span> Confirm & Print',c:'btn-ok',fn:()=>{closeModal();processSale();}}
  ]);
}

function processSale(){
  showLoading('Processing sale…');
  $('chkBtn').disabled=true;
  const date=$('elDate').value, customer=$('custName').value.trim();
  const discVal=Number($('discAmt').value)||0;
  const sub=cart.reduce((s,i)=>s+(i.unitPrice*i.qty),0);
  const discount=discType==='pct'?Math.round(sub*(discVal/100)):discVal;
  let pending=cart.length, errors=[], snapshot=cart.map(x=>({...x}));
  window._lastRealSaleID = null;
  const sharedTxnId='YB-'+new Date().toISOString().slice(2,10).replace(/-/g,'')+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6).toUpperCase();
let itemIndex=0;

  // If offline, save locally and return
  if(!_isOnline){
    const snapshot=cart.map(x=>({...x}));
    const discount=Number($('discAmt').value)||0;
    const sub=snapshot.reduce((s,i)=>s+(i.unitPrice*i.qty),0);
    const tot=Math.max(0,sub-discount);
    window._lastReceiptDisc=discount;
    window._lastReceiptSub=sub;
    const txnId='ST-'+Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase();
    cart.forEach(item=>{
      const _itemDisc=cart.length>1?Math.round(discount*(item.unitPrice*item.qty)/Math.max(sub,1)):discount;
      const payload=item.isWB?
        {fabricID:item.wbData.fabricID,width:item.wbData.width,height:item.wbData.height,windows:item.wbData.windows,accessories:[],finalPrice:item.unitPrice,disc:_itemDisc,payment:payMethod,soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:$('elDate').value}:
        {productID:item.id,qty:item.qty,unitSoldPrice:item.unitPrice,disc:_itemDisc,payment:payMethod,soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:$('elDate').value};
      savePendingSale(payload);
    });
    myTxns.push({id:txnId,time:new Date(),items:snapshot,total:tot,payment:payMethod,customer:$('custName').value.trim(),discount});
    cart=[]; $('discAmt').value=''; $('custName').value='';
    document.querySelectorAll('.pm').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('on'));
    document.querySelector('.pm').classList.add('on');
    payMethod='Cash'; renderCart();
    showReceipt(snapshot,customer||'',payMethod,discount,txnId);
    $('chkBtn').disabled=false;
    return;
  }

  const vatAmt=window._lastVatAmt||0;
  const vatRate=window._lastVatRate||0;
  cart.forEach(item=>{
  const itemNum=++itemIndex;
  const itemVat=cart.length>1?Math.round(vatAmt*(item.unitPrice*item.qty)/Math.max(sub,1)):vatAmt;
  const itemDisc=cart.length>1?Math.round(discount*(item.unitPrice*item.qty)/Math.max(sub,1)):discount;
  if(item.isWB){
    const d=item.wbData;
    const acc=[{name:'Rail',qty:d.width*d.windows},{name:'Miler',qty:d.width*d.windows},{name:'Small Belt',qty:d.width*d.windows},{name:'Big Belt',qty:d.width*d.windows},{name:'Accessories',qty:d.windows}];
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r=>{hideLoading();done(r,item)})
      .withFailureHandler(e=>{hideLoading();errors.push(e.message);done(null,item)})
      .recordSale({fabricID:d.fabricID,width:d.width,height:d.height,windows:d.windows,accessories:acc,finalPrice:item.unitPrice,disc:itemDisc,vat:itemVat,vatRate,payment:payMethod,soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date,saleID:sharedTxnId+'-'+itemNum,customerName:$('custName').value.trim(),customerWA:($('custWA').value||'').trim(),_token:_sessionToken});
  } else {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r=>{hideLoading();done(r,item)})
      .withFailureHandler(e=>{hideLoading();errors.push(e.message);done(null,item)})
      .recordSale({productID:item.id,qty:item.qty,unitSoldPrice:item.unitPrice,disc:itemDisc,vat:itemVat,vatRate,payment:payMethod,soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date,saleID:sharedTxnId+'-'+itemNum,customerName:$('custName').value.trim(),customerWA:($('custWA').value||'').trim(),_token:_sessionToken});
  }
});

  function done(r,item){
    pending--;
    // Inspect backend response: session expired, or a business failure (success returns a saleID)
    if(r&&r.unauthorized===true){
      if(!errors.includes('Session expired — please sign in again')) errors.push('Session expired — please sign in again');
    } else if(r&&!r.saleID&&r.msg){
      errors.push(r.msg);
    }
    if(pending>0) return;
    $('chkBtn').disabled=false;
    if(errors.length){ toast('Errors: '+errors.join('; '),'er'); return; }

    const txnId = sharedTxnId;
    snapshot.forEach((item,i)=>{ item.txnId = sharedTxnId+'-'+(i+1); });
    window._lastRealSaleID = null;
    const tot=snapshot.reduce((s,i)=>s+(i.unitPrice*i.qty),0)-discount;
    const cust=$('custName').value.trim();
    const waNum=($('custWA').value||'').trim().replace(/\D/g,'');
    myTxns.push({
      id:txnId, time:new Date(), items:snapshot,
      total:tot, payment:payMethod, customer:cust, discount, waNum
    });

    // Clear cart immediately — new sale can start right away
    cart=[]; $('discAmt').value=''; $('custName').value=''; $('custWA').value='';
    setDiscType('fixed');
    document.querySelectorAll('.pm').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('on'));
    document.querySelector('.pm').classList.add('on');
    payMethod='Cash';
    renderCart();
    _custDataCache=null; // invalidate so next customer load is fresh
    // Update stock locally — deduct sold qty from products array
    snapshot.forEach(item=>{
      const p=products.find(x=>x.id===item.id);
      if(p) p.stock=Math.max(0,p.stock-item.qty);
    });
    buildCatBar();
    buildGrid(products,'');
    initStock();
    // Show receipt in separate window
    showReceipt(snapshot,cust,payMethod,discount,txnId,waNum,true);
    toast('Sale recorded · Receipt printing…','ok');
  }
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   RECEIPT
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function showReceipt(items,customer,pay,discount,id,waNum,saveNew){
  const sub=items.reduce((s,i)=>s+(i.unitPrice*i.qty),0);
  const _vatSettings=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('hubSettings_'+user.name)||'{}');
  const _vatEnabled=_vatSettings.vat_enabled==='true'||_vatSettings.vat_enabled===true;
  const _vatRate=_vatEnabled?((parseFloat(_vatSettings.vat_rate)||7.5)/100):0;
  const _vatBase=_vatSettings.vat_base||'after_discount';
  const vatAmt=_vatEnabled?Math.round((_vatBase==='before_discount'?sub:(sub-discount))*_vatRate):0;
  const tot=Math.max(0,sub-discount+vatAmt);
  const now=new Date();
  id=id||('ST-'+now.getTime().toString(36).toUpperCase());
  const dateStr=now.toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'});
  const timeStr=now.toLocaleTimeString('en-NG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});

  const receiptHTML=`
    <div style="font-family:'Outfit',sans-serif;color:#0a1628;width:100%;max-width:300px;margin:0 auto;font-size:12px">
      <!-- HEADER -->
      <div style="text-align:center;padding-bottom:10px;border-bottom:1px dashed #c8d3e8;margin-bottom:8px">
        <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:-.5px;color:#1a48cc">Tado Foods POS</div>
        <div style="font-size:9px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:2px;text-transform:uppercase;color:#6b84af;margin-top:1px">${user.store}</div>
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:#6b84af;margin-top:3px">${dateStr} · ${timeStr}</div>
      </div>

      <!-- META -->
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px;font-size:9px">
        <div><span style="color:#6b84af">Receipt: </span><span style="font-family:'Fira Code',monospace;font-weight:700">${id}</span></div>
        <div style="color:#6b84af">${user.name}</div>
      </div>

      ${customer?`<div style="font-size:9px;margin-bottom:8px;padding:4px 8px;background:#f0f4ff;border-radius:4px">👤 <strong>${esc(customer)}</strong></div>`:''}

      <!-- ITEMS -->
      <div style="border-top:1px solid #0a1628;border-bottom:1px solid #0a1628;padding:6px 0;margin-bottom:6px">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:8.5px;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.8px;color:#6b84af;padding-bottom:4px;margin-bottom:4px;border-bottom:1px dashed #e0e7f0">
          <span style="flex:1">Item</span>
          <span style="width:30px;text-align:center">Qty</span>
          <span style="width:70px;text-align:right">Amount</span>
        </div>
        ${items.map((item,idx)=>`
          <div style="padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px dashed ${idx===items.length-1?'transparent':'#f0f4ff'}">
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center">
              <div style="flex:1;padding-right:6px">
                <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#0a1628">${esc(item.name)}</div>
                <div style="font-size:8.5px;color:#a8bcda;font-family:'Fira Code',monospace">@ ₦${item.unitPrice.toLocaleString()}</div>
                ${item.txnId?`<div style="font-size:8px;color:#c8d3e8;font-family:'Fira Code',monospace;margin-top:1px">TXN: ${item.txnId}</div>`:''}
              </div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#3d5278;width:30px;text-align:center">${item.isWB?'1':item.qty}</div>
              <div style="font-family:'Fira Code',monospace;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#0a1628;width:70px;text-align:right">₦${(item.unitPrice*(item.isWB?1:item.qty)).toLocaleString()}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>

      <!-- TOTALS -->
      <div style="margin-bottom:8px">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:10px;color:#6b84af;padding:2px 0">
          <span>Subtotal</span><span style="font-family:'Fira Code',monospace">₦${sub.toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>
        ${discount>0?`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:10px;color:#6b84af;padding:2px 0">
          <span>Discount</span><span style="font-family:'Fira Code',monospace">— ₦${discount.toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>`:''}
        ${vatAmt>0?`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:10px;color:#6b84af;padding:2px 0">
          <span>VAT (${Math.round(_vatRate*100)}%)</span><span style="font-family:'Fira Code',monospace">+ ₦${vatAmt.toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>`:''}
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:6px;padding:8px 10px;background:#0a1628;border-radius:6px">
          <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#fff">TOTAL</span>
          <span style="font-family:'Fira Code',monospace;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:#60a5fa">₦${tot.toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- PAYMENT -->
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:6px 10px;background:#f0f4ff;border-radius:6px;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1a48cc">${pay}</div>
        <div style="border:1.5px solid #0a7c5c;color:#0a7c5c;font-size:9px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:4px;transform:rotate(-2deg);letter-spacing:1px">PAID ✓</div>
      </div>

      <!-- FOOTER -->
      <div style="text-align:center;padding-top:8px;border-top:1px dashed #c8d3e8">
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#0a1628">Thank you for your business!</div>
        <div style="font-size:8.5px;color:#a8bcda;margin-top:2px">Keep receipt for returns</div>
        <div style="font-size:8px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:1.5px;text-transform:uppercase;color:#c8d3e8;margin-top:6px">Tado Foods POS</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  $('rcptCnt').innerHTML=receiptHTML;

  // Save receipt to session
  if(!window._receipts) window._receipts=[];
  const blob=`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8">
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Outfit:wght@400;700;800;900&family=Fira+Code:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>body{margin:0;padding:20px;background:#fff;display:flex;justify-content:center}</style>
  </head><body>${receiptHTML}</body></html>`;
  window._receipts.push({id,html:blob,items,customer:customer||'',payment:pay,total:tot});

  // Auto-save to Google Drive — only for a brand-new sale, never on a reprint (avoids duplicates)
  if(saveNew){
    const driveFileName='Receipt_'+id+'_'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,19).replace(/:/g,'-').replace('T','_')+'.html';
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
        if(r&&r.success) toast('Receipt saved to Drive','ok');
        else toast('Drive save failed: '+((r&&r.error)||'unknown'),'wa');
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e=>toast('Receipt not saved to Drive — no connection','wa'))
      .saveReceiptToDrive({id,html:blob,fileName:driveFileName,_token:_sessionToken});
  }

  window._lastReceiptItems=items;
  window._lastReceiptSub=sub;
  window._lastReceiptDisc=discount;
  const fn='Receipt_'+id+'.pdf';
  const pw=window.open('','_blank','width=400,height=650');
  if(pw){
    const waBtn=waNum
      ?'<button onclick="window.opener.sendWAReceipt(\''+waNum+'\',\''+id+'\',\''+tot+'\',\''+pay+'\')" style="background:#25D366;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 16px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer">💬 WhatsApp</button>'
      :'';
    pw.document.write('<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8">'
      +'<title>'+fn+'</title>'
      +'<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Outfit:wght@400;700;800;900&family=Fira+Code:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">'
      +'<style>'
      +'*{box-sizing:border-box}'
      +'body{margin:0;padding:16px;background:#fff;font-family:Outfit,sans-serif;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center}'
      +'@media print{.nop{display:none!important}.hint{display:none!important}@page{size:80mm auto;margin:4mm}}'
      +'.nop{display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:center;padding:8px;background:#f8fafd;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e7f0;width:100%;margin-bottom:12px}'
      +'.nop button{padding:7px 14px;border:none;border-radius:6px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;font-family:Outfit,sans-serif}'
      +'.pb{background:#1a48cc;color:#fff}.sb{background:#059669;color:#fff}.cb{background:#6b7280;color:#fff}'
      +'</style>'
      +'</head><body>'
      +'<div class="nop">'
      +'<button class="pb" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ Print</button>'
      +'<button class="sb" onclick="window.print()">💾 Save PDF</button>'
      +waBtn
      +'<button class="cb" onclick="window.close()">✕</button>'
      +'</div>'
      +receiptHTML
      +'<script>window.onload=function(){setTimeout(function(){window.print()},800)}<\/script>'
      +'</body></html>');
    pw.document.close();
  } else {
    openOV('rcptOV');
    toast('Allow popups for auto-print','wa');
    const waOvBtn=$('rcptWaBtn');
    if(waOvBtn){
      if(waNum){ waOvBtn.classList.remove('hidden'); waOvBtn.onclick=()=>sendWAReceipt(waNum,id,tot,pay); }
      else waOvBtn.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }
} // END showReceipt

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────

───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function cfmUndoEl(){
  showModal('Undo Last Sale',`<p style="color:var(--t2);font-size:14px">Undo the most recent sale on <strong>${user.store}</strong>?<br><small style="color:var(--t3)">Stock will be restored.</small></p>`,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'Undo Sale',c:'btn-d',fn:()=>{ closeModal(); google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r=>{ toast(r.msg,'in'); loadProds(); }).withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e&&e.message?'Error: '+e.message:'Connection error — please try again','er')).undoLastSale({store:user.store,user:user.name,txnBase:(myTxns.length?String(myTxns[myTxns.length-1].id):null),_token:_sessionToken}); }}
  ]);
}
/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   STOCK
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function initStock(){
  $('stockDate').value=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  if($('stockExpiry')) $('stockExpiry').value='';
  if($('stockAlertWrap')) $('stockAlertWrap').classList.add('hidden');
  [60,30,14,7].forEach(v=>{ const el=$('sAlert'+v); if(el) el.checked=(v===14); });
  $('stockSearch').value=''; $('stockSR').classList.remove('on');
  $('stockInfoBox').classList.add('hidden'); $('stockQty').value='';
  $('stockInfoBox').dataset.cs='';
  if(isWB()){
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(p=>{
      window._wbs=p;
      $('stockCat').innerHTML='<option value="">Select category</option>';
      [...new Set(p.map(x=>x.category))].forEach(c=>$('stockCat').add(new Option(c,c)));
      $('stockSub').disabled=false; $('stockProd').disabled=false;
    }).getWindowBlindProducts(user.store);
  } else {
    $('stockCat').innerHTML='<option value="">Select category</option>';
    [...new Set(products.map(p=>p.category))].forEach(v=>$('stockCat').add(new Option(v,v)));
    $('stockSub').innerHTML='<option value="">Select subcategory</option>';
    $('stockProd').innerHTML='<option value="">Select product</option>';
    $('stockSub').disabled=true; $('stockProd').disabled=true;
  }
}
function onStockCat(){
  const src=isWB()?(window._wbs||[]):products;
  const cat=$('stockCat').value;
  $('stockSub').innerHTML='<option value="">Select subcategory</option>';
  $('stockSub').disabled=false;
  $('stockProd').innerHTML='<option value="">Select product</option>';
  $('stockProd').disabled=true;
  [...new Set(src.filter(p=>p.category===cat).map(p=>p.subCategory))].forEach(v=>$('stockSub').add(new Option(v,v)));
}
function onStockSub(){
  const src=isWB()?(window._wbs||[]):products;
  const sub=$('stockSub').value;
  $('stockProd').innerHTML='<option value="">Select product</option>';
  $('stockProd').disabled=false;
  src.filter(p=>p.subCategory===sub).forEach(p=>$('stockProd').add(new Option(p.name,p.id)));
}
function onStockProd(){
  const id=$('stockProd').value, box=$('stockInfoBox');
  if(!id){ box.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
  const src=isWB()?(window._wbs||[]):products;
  const p=src.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
  if(!p) return;
  box.dataset.cs=p.stock;
  box.innerText='Current Stock: '+p.stock;
  box.classList.remove('hidden');
  prevStock();
}
function prevStock(){
  const box=$('stockInfoBox'), qty=+($('stockQty').value||0), cur=+(box.dataset.cs||0);
  if(qty) box.innerText=`Current: ${cur} → After: ${cur+qty}`;
}
function doStock(){
  if(!$('stockProd').value) return toast('Select a product','er');
  if(!$('stockQty').value||+$('stockQty').value<1) return toast('Enter valid quantity','er');
  showModal('Confirm Stock In',`<p style="color:var(--t2);font-size:14px">Add <strong>${$('stockQty').value} units</strong> to inventory?</p>`,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'Add Stock',c:'btn-p',fn:()=>{
      closeModal();
      showLoading('Updating stock…');
      const _exp=$('stockExpiry').value||null;
      const _alrt=[60,30,14,7].filter(v=>$('sAlert'+v)&&$('sAlert'+v).checked).join(',')||null;
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
          hideLoading();
          toast(r.msg,'ok');
          if(r.oldExpiryNote){
            const n=r.oldExpiryNote;
            const msg=n.daysToExpiry<=0
              ?`${n.productName} had expired stock (${n.oldExpiry}) — check if it has been cleared from shelf`
              :`${n.productName} had stock expiring in ${n.daysToExpiry} day${n.daysToExpiry!==1?'s':''} (${n.oldExpiry}) — check if it has been cleared from shelf`;
            addNotif('⚠️ Old Stock Check',msg,'wa',()=>goPage('stock'));
            saveNotifs(); updateNotifBadge();
            toast('Old expiry stock detected — see notifications','wa');
          }
          initStock(); loadProds();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
        .stockIn({productID:$('stockProd').value,qty:+$('stockQty').value,store:user.store,performedBy:user.name,date:$('stockDate').value,expiryDate:_exp,alertDays:_alrt,_token:_sessionToken});
    }}
  ]);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   EXPENSE
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function doExpense(){
  const type=$('expType').value, amt=+$('expAmt').value;
  if(!type||!amt) return toast('Fill expense type and amount','er');
  showLoading('Saving expense…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(r=>{ hideLoading(); toast(r.msg+(r.id?' · '+r.id:''),'ok'); $('expAmt').value=''; $('expNotes').value=''; })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .recordExpense({expenseType:type,amount:amt,notes:$('expNotes').value,paidBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:$('expDate').value,_token:_sessionToken});
}
function openExpMdl(){ openOV('expOV'); }
function doNewExpType(){
  const name=$('nExpN').value.trim(), amt=+$('nExpA').value;
  if(!name||!amt) return toast('Fill name and amount','er');
  closeOV('expOV');
  showLoading('Creating expense type…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r=>{ hideLoading(); toast(r.msg,'ok'); loadExpTypes(); })
        .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
        .recordExpense({expenseType:name,amount:amt,notes:$('nExpNt').value,paidBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:$('expDate').value,_token:_sessionToken});
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .createExpenseType({name,_token:_sessionToken});
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   MY ACTIVITY
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function toDateStr(val){
  if(!val) return '';
  if(val instanceof Date) return isNaN(val)?'':val.toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const s=String(val);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return s.slice(0,10);
  const d=new Date(s);
  return isNaN(d)?'':d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
}

function loadActivity(){
  if(myTxns.length){
    _actPage=1;
    renderActivity();
    return;
  }
  $('actTxns').innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3">sync</span><p style="margin-top:10px;font-size:13px">Loading your activity…</p></div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      myTxns=[];
      if(data&&data.transactions&&data.transactions.length){
        data.transactions.forEach(t=>{
          if(!t.time&&t.date) t.time=t.date;
          myTxns.push(t);
        });
      }
      _actPage=1;
      renderActivity();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{
      $('actTxns').innerHTML=`<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--rd1);font-size:13px">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;
    })
    .getSalespersonActivity({soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:null,days:14,_token:_sessionToken});
}
function filterAct(){ _actPage=1; renderActivity(); }

function renderActivity(){
  const search=($('actSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const dateF=$('actDate').value;
  let txns=[...myTxns];

  if(dateF) txns=txns.filter(t=>toDateStr(t.time||t.date)===dateF);
  if(search) txns=txns.filter(t=>
    t.id?.toLowerCase().includes(search)||
    t.customer?.toLowerCase().includes(search)||
    t.items?.some(i=>i.name?.toLowerCase().includes(search))
  );
  txns.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.time||b.date||0)-new Date(a.time||a.date||0));

  // KPIs
  const todayStr=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const monthStr=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,7);
  const todayTxns=myTxns.filter(t=>toDateStr(t.time||t.date)===todayStr);
  const monthTxns=myTxns.filter(t=>(toDateStr(t.time||t.date)||'').slice(0,7)===monthStr);
  const monthRev=monthTxns.reduce((s,t)=>s+(t.total||0),0);
  const kd=$('actKpis'); kd.innerHTML='';
  const weekTxns=myTxns.filter(t=>{ const d=new Date(t.time||t.date||0); return !isNaN(d)&&(new Date()-d)/(1000*86400)<=7; });

  // Busiest hour
  const hourCount={};
  myTxns.forEach(t=>{ const h=new Date(t.time||t.date||0).getHours(); if(!isNaN(h)) hourCount[h]=(hourCount[h]||0)+1; });
  const busiestHour=Object.keys(hourCount).sort((a,b)=>hourCount[b]-hourCount[a])[0];
  const busiestHourStr=busiestHour!==undefined?`${busiestHour>12?busiestHour-12:busiestHour||12}${busiestHour>=12?'pm':'am'}`:'—';

  // Returns count
  const returnsCount=myTxns.filter(t=>t.id&&String(t.id).includes('RET')).length;

  [{l:"Today's Sales",v:todayTxns.length,pre:''},{l:"This Week's Sales",v:weekTxns.length,pre:''},{l:"This Month's Sales",v:monthTxns.length,pre:''}]
  .forEach(k=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div'); el.className='act-k';
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="act-k-v">—</div><div class="act-k-l">${k.l}</div>`;
    kd.appendChild(el);
    countUp(el.querySelector('.act-k-v'),k.v,k.pre);
  });

  // Busiest hour card
  const bhEl=document.createElement('div'); bhEl.className='act-k';
  bhEl.innerHTML=`<div class="act-k-v" style="font-size:20px">${busiestHourStr}</div><div class="act-k-l">Busiest Hour</div>`;
  kd.appendChild(bhEl);

  // Returns card
  const retEl=document.createElement('div'); retEl.className='act-k';
  retEl.innerHTML=`<div class="act-k-v">—</div><div class="act-k-l">My Returns</div>`;
  kd.appendChild(retEl);
  countUp(retEl.querySelector('.act-k-v'),returnsCount,'');

  const td=$('actTxns');
  if(!txns.length){
    td.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3);font-size:13px">No transactions found</div>';
    return;
  }

  // Pagination
  const total=txns.length;
  const maxPage=Math.ceil(total/_actPerPage);
  _actPage=Math.max(1,Math.min(_actPage,maxPage));
  const start=(_actPage-1)*_actPerPage;
  const pageItems=txns.slice(start,start+_actPerPage);

  td.innerHTML='';

  // Pagination bar top
  if(maxPage>1){
    const pb=document.createElement('div');
    pb.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px';
    pb.innerHTML=`
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3)">Showing ${start+1}–${Math.min(start+_actPerPage,total)} of ${total} transactions</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">
        <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_actPage--;renderActivity()" ${_actPage===1?'disabled':''}>
          <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span>
        </button>
        <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Page ${_actPage} of ${maxPage}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_actPage++;renderActivity()" ${_actPage===maxPage?'disabled':''}>
          <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
    td.appendChild(pb);
  }

  // Table header
  const table=document.createElement('table');
  table.style.cssText='width:100%;border-collapse:collapse';
  table.innerHTML=`
    <thead>
      <tr style="background:var(--s1);border-bottom:2px solid var(--bdr)">
        <th style="padding:10px 12px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">TXN ID</th>
        <th style="padding:10px 12px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Product(s)</th>
        <th style="padding:10px 12px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Date & Time</th>
        <th style="padding:10px 12px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Payment</th>
        <th style="padding:10px 12px;text-align:right;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Amount</th>
        <th style="padding:10px 12px;text-align:center;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Print</th>
      </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody id="actTBody"></tbody>
  `;
  td.appendChild(table);

  const tbody=table.querySelector('#actTBody');
  pageItems.forEach(t=>{
    const tDate=new Date(t.time||t.date||0);
    const timeStr=isNaN(tDate)?'—':tDate.toLocaleTimeString('en-NG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
    const dateStr=isNaN(tDate)?'—':tDate.toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'});
    const names=t.items?t.items.map(i=>esc(i.name)).join(', ').substring(0,60):'—';
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    tr.style.cssText='border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr);transition:background .15s;cursor:pointer';
    tr.onmouseover=()=>tr.style.background='var(--s1)';
    tr.onmouseout=()=>tr.style.background='';
    tr.innerHTML=`
      <td style="padding:11px 12px;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:11px;color:var(--b1);font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap">${t.id||'—'}</td>
      <td style="padding:11px 12px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1);max-width:200px">
        <div style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${names}</div>
        ${t.items?.length>1?`<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:2px">${t.items.length} items</div>`:''}
      </td>
      <td style="padding:11px 12px;font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);white-space:nowrap">${dateStr} <span style="font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--t4)">${timeStr}</span></td>
      <td style="padding:11px 12px;font-size:11px;color:var(--t2)">${t.payment||'—'}</td>
      <td style="padding:11px 12px;text-align:right;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1);white-space:nowrap">₦${(t.total||0).toLocaleString()}</td>
      <td style="padding:11px 12px;text-align:center">
        <button class="ib" title="Reprint receipt" style="width:30px;height:30px">
          <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:16px">print</span>
        </button>
      </td>
    `;
    tr.querySelector('.ib').onclick=e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); showReceipt(t.items||[],t.customer||'',t.payment||'Cash',t.discount||0,t.id,t.waNum||''); };
    tr.onclick=()=>showReceipt(t.items||[],t.customer||'',t.payment||'Cash',t.discount||0,t.id,t.waNum||'');
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });

  // Pagination bar bottom
  if(maxPage>1){
    const pb2=document.createElement('div');
    pb2.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin-top:16px;gap:6px';
    pb2.innerHTML=`
      <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_actPage--;renderActivity()" ${_actPage===1?'disabled':''}>
        <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span> Prev
      </button>
      <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1);padding:0 8px">Page ${_actPage} of ${maxPage}</span>
      <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_actPage++;renderActivity()" ${_actPage===maxPage?'disabled':''}>
        Next <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
      </button>
    `;
    td.appendChild(pb2);
  }
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   DASHBOARD
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
let _dashData=null;
let _dashDataStore=null;
let _dashTab='executive';

function loadDash(){
  _invMvLoaded=false;
  _archLoaded=false;
  if(!$('dashFrom').value||!$('dashTo').value){
    const iso=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    const d30=new Date(); d30.setDate(d30.getDate()-29);
    $('dashFrom').value=d30.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    $('dashTo').value=iso;
  }
  const from=$('dashFrom').value, to=$('dashTo').value;
  if(!from||!to) return toast('Select a date range first','wa');
  if(_dashDataCache&&from===_dashCacheFrom&&to===_dashCacheTo){
    _dashData=_dashDataCache;
    $('dashSub').innerText=`${from} → ${to}`;
    renderDash(_dashDataCache,from,to);
    return;
  }
  $('dashSub').innerText='Loading data…';
  $('dashBd').innerHTML='<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:200px;gap:12px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:36px;opacity:.3">sync</span><span style="font-size:13px">Loading dashboard…</span></div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(d=>{ _dashData=d; _dashDataStore=null; renderDash(d,from,to); })
    .withFailureHandler(()=>{ $('dashBd').innerHTML='<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:200px;color:var(--t3);gap:12px"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:40px;opacity:.3">wifi_off</span><span style="font-size:13px">No connection — click Refresh when back online</span></div>'; })
    .getDashboardData({from,to,store:null,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function onDashStoreChange(){
  if(_dashData){
    // Data already loaded — just re-render with new store filter
    const from=$('dashFrom').value, to=$('dashTo').value;
    renderDash(_dashData,from,to);
  } else {
    loadDash();
  }
}

function populateDashStoreFilter(){
  const sel=$('dashStoreFilter');
  if(!sel||!user.availableStores) return;
  if(sel.options.length>1) return;
  user.availableStores.forEach(s=>sel.add(new Option(s,s)));
}

function switchDashTab(tab){
  _dashTab=tab;
  document.querySelectorAll('.dash-tab').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('on'));
  const dtab=$('dtab-'+tab); if(dtab) dtab.classList.add('on');
  // Show print button only on inventory tab
  const invPrintBtn=$('dashInvPrintBtn');
  if(invPrintBtn) invPrintBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', tab!=='inventory');
  if(tab==='expiry'){ renderDashExpiry(); return; }
  if(_dashData){
    const from=$('dashFrom').value, to=$('dashTo').value;
    renderDash(_dashData,from,to);
  } else {
    loadDash();
  }
}

function renderDash(data,from,to){
  if(!data){ $('dashBd').innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)">No data available</div>'; return; }
  $('dashSub').innerText=`${from} → ${to}`;
  const tab=_dashTab||'executive';
  if(tab==='executive') renderDashExecutive(data,from,to);
  else if(tab==='sales') renderDashSales(data,from,to);
  else if(tab==='expense') renderDashExpense(data,from,to);
  else if(tab==='inventory') renderDashInventory(data,from,to);
  else if(tab==='movement') renderDashMovement(data,from,to);
  else if(tab==='archived') renderDashArchived(data,from,to);
  else if(tab==='expiry') renderDashExpiry();
}

function renderDashPageNav(currentTab){
  const tabs=['executive','sales','expense','inventory','movement','archived'];
  const labels={executive:'Executive Summary',sales:'Sales Overview',expense:'Expense Overview',inventory:'Inventory Overview',movement:'Inventory Movement',archived:'Archived'};
  const idx=tabs.indexOf(currentTab);
  const prev=idx>0?tabs[idx-1]:null;
  const next=idx<tabs.length-1?tabs[idx+1]:null;
  const el=document.createElement('div');
  el.style.cssText='display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:20px;padding-top:16px;border-top:1px solid var(--bdr)';
  el.innerHTML=`
    <div>
      ${prev?`<button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="switchDashTab('${prev}')">
        <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span>${labels[prev]}
      </button>`:''}
    </div>
    <div>
      ${next?`<button class="btn btn-p btn-sm" onclick="switchDashTab('${next}')">
        ${labels[next]}<span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
      </button>`:''}
    </div>
  `;
  const bd=$('dashBd');
  if(bd) bd.appendChild(el);
}

function renderDashExecutive(data,from,to){
  const fmt=n=>'₦'+(+n||0).toLocaleString();
  const can=p=>user&&user.permissions&&user.permissions.includes(p);
  const canFinance=can('dashboard_finance');

  const totalRev=data.totalRevenue||0;
  const totalProfit=data.totalProfit||0;
  const totalCost=totalRev-totalProfit;
  const totalExpense=data.totalExpenses||data.todayExpenses||0;
  const margin=totalRev>0?((totalProfit/totalRev)*100).toFixed(1):0;
  const revTrend=data.revTrend||data.monthlySales||[];

  $('dashBd').innerHTML=`
    <!-- KPI CARDS -->
    <div class="exec-kpis" id="execKpis"></div>

    <!-- LINE CHART -->
    <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t1)">Total Sales Over Time</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:2px">Revenue trend for selected period</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <canvas id="execLineChart" style="width:100%;height:200px"></canvas>
    </div>

    <!-- BOTTOM ROW -->
    <div class="dg2" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <!-- Payment breakdown -->
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Revenue by Payment Method</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Cash vs POS vs Transfer</div>
        <div class="donut-wrap" id="execPayDonut" style="margin-top:16px"></div>
      </div>
      <!-- Stock health -->
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Stock Health</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Current inventory status across all stores</div>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;margin-top:16px" id="execStockHealth"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- STORE BAR CHART -->
    <div class="cc" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="cc-h">Total Sales by Store</div>
      <div class="cc-s">Revenue breakdown per store</div>
      <div class="hbar mt8" id="execStoreChart"></div>
    </div>

    <!-- BEST DAY + TOP SELLERS + STOCK HEALTH -->
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:14px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Best Day of Week</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Revenue &amp; transactions by day</div>
        <div id="execBestDay" style="margin-top:10px"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Top Sellers</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Staff by revenue this period</div>
        <div id="execTopSellers" style="margin-top:10px"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Stock Health</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Inventory status across all stores</div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;margin-top:10px" id="execStockHealth2"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  // KPI CARDS
  const kpis=[
    {l:'Total Revenue',v:totalRev,pre:'₦',ico:'payments',color:'#2254e0',bg:'var(--bglow)'},
    {l:'Total Cost',v:totalCost,pre:'₦',ico:'production_quantity_limits',color:'var(--rd1)',bg:'var(--rglow)'},
    {l:'Total Profit',v:totalProfit,pre:'₦',ico:'trending_up',color:'var(--gn1)',bg:'var(--gglow)'},
    {l:'Total Expenses',v:totalExpense,pre:'₦',ico:'receipt_long',color:'var(--am1)',bg:'var(--aglow)'},
    {l:'Profit Margin',v:margin,pre:'',suf:'%',ico:'percent',color:'#8b5cf6',bg:'rgba(139,92,246,.1)'},
  ];

  const kd=$('execKpis');
  // Today's values for comparison
  const todayRev=data.todayRevenue||0;
  const todayProfit=data.todayProfit||0;
  const todayCost=todayRev-todayProfit;
  const todayExp=data.todayExpenses||0;
  const todayMargin=todayRev>0?((todayProfit/todayRev)*100).toFixed(1):0;
  const todayVals=[todayRev,todayCost,todayProfit,todayExp,todayMargin];

  kpis.forEach((k,i)=>{
    const tv=todayVals[i]||0;
    const tvStr=k.pre==='₦'?'₦'+Number(tv).toLocaleString():tv+(k.suf||'');
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.className='exec-kpi';
    el.style.animationDelay=`${i*0.08}s`;
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px">
        <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:10px;background:${k.bg};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">
          <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:19px;color:${k.color}">${k.ico}</span>
        </div>
        <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px">${k.l}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="exec-kpi-v" style="color:${k.color}">—</div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:8px">
        <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t4)">Today</span>
        <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;font-family:var(--f-m);color:${k.color}">${tvStr}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="height:2px;background:${k.bg};border-radius:2px;margin-top:8px">
        <div style="height:100%;width:60%;background:${k.color};border-radius:2px;transition:width .8s ease"></div>
      </div>
    `;
    kd.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(()=>countUp(el.querySelector('.exec-kpi-v'),k.v,k.pre,k.suf),i*120);
  });

  // LINE CHART
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const canvas=$('execLineChart');
    if(!canvas) return;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width=canvas.offsetWidth*2;
    canvas.height=400;
    canvas.style.height='200px';
    const pts=revTrend.length>=2?revTrend.map(m=>m.revenue||m.sales||0):[0,0];
    const labels=revTrend.map(m=>m.month||m.label||'');
    const W=canvas.width, H=canvas.height;
    const pad={t:30,r:20,b:40,l:60};
    const maxV=Math.max(...pts,1);
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);

    // Grid lines
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.08)';
    ctx.lineWidth=1;
    for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){
      const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-i/4);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pad.l,y); ctx.lineTo(W-pad.r,y); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.5)';
      ctx.font=`${24}px sans-serif`;
      ctx.textAlign='right';
      ctx.fillText('₦'+((maxV*i/4)/1000).toFixed(0)+'k',pad.l-8,y+8);
    }

    // X labels
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.6)';
    ctx.font='22px sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign='center';
    const step=(W-pad.l-pad.r)/(pts.length-1||1);
    pts.forEach((_,i)=>{
      const x=pad.l+i*step;
      ctx.fillText(labels[i]||'',x,H-8);
    });

    // Gradient fill
    const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,pad.t,0,H-pad.b);
    grad.addColorStop(0,'rgba(34,84,224,0.18)');
    grad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(34,84,224,0)');
    ctx.beginPath();
    pts.forEach((v,i)=>{
      const x=pad.l+i*step;
      const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV);
      i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);
    });
    ctx.lineTo(pad.l+(pts.length-1)*step,H-pad.b);
    ctx.lineTo(pad.l,H-pad.b);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle=grad;
    ctx.fill();

    // Line
    ctx.strokeStyle='#2254e0';
    ctx.lineWidth=3;
    ctx.lineJoin='round';
    ctx.beginPath();
    pts.forEach((v,i)=>{
      const x=pad.l+i*step;
      const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV);
      i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);
    });
    ctx.stroke();

    // Dots
    pts.forEach((v,i)=>{
      const x=pad.l+i*step;
      const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(x,y,6,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='#2254e0';
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(x,y,3,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
      ctx.fill();
    });
  },200);

  // PAYMENT METHOD DONUT
  const pm=data.paymentMethods||{Cash:0,POS:0,Transfer:0};
  const pmVals=[pm.Cash||0,pm.POS||0,pm.Transfer||0];
  const pmNames=['Cash','POS','Transfer'];
  const pmColors=['#2254e0','#0a7c5c','#c97c0a'];
  const pdWrap=$('execPayDonut');
  if(pdWrap){
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width=100; canvas.height=100;
    canvas.style.cssText='width:100px;height:100px;flex-shrink:0';
    const legend=document.createElement('div'); legend.className='donut-legend';
    const pmTotal=pmVals.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)||1;
    legend.innerHTML=pmVals.map((v,i)=>`
      <div class="donut-item">
        <div class="donut-dot" style="background:${pmColors[i]}"></div>
        <div class="donut-n">${pmNames[i]}</div>
        <div class="donut-v">${Math.round((v/pmTotal)*100)}% · ${fmt(v)}</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
    pdWrap.appendChild(canvas); pdWrap.appendChild(legend);
    setTimeout(()=>drawDonut(canvas,pmVals,pmColors),100);
  }

  // STOCK HEALTH
  const lowStock=data.lowStock||[];
  const shEl=$('execStockHealth');
  if(shEl){
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(inv=>{
        const allProds=[...(inv.electronics||inv.products||[]),...(inv.windowblind||[])];
        const total=allProds.length;
        const outOfStock=allProds.filter(p=>p.stock<=0).length;
        const lowStockCount=allProds.filter(p=>p.stock>0&&p.stock<=p.reorder).length;
        const healthy=total-outOfStock-lowStockCount;
        const healthPct=total>0?Math.round((healthy/total)*100):0;

        shEl.innerHTML=`
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px">
            <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t2)">Overall Health</span>
            <span style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:${healthPct>=70?'var(--gn1)':healthPct>=40?'var(--am1)':'var(--rd1)'}">${healthPct}%</span>
          </div>
          <div style="height:8px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:16px">
            <div style="height:100%;width:${healthPct}%;background:${healthPct>=70?'var(--gn1)':healthPct>=40?'var(--am1)':'var(--rd1)'};border-radius:4px;transition:width .8s ease"></div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:10px">
            <div style="text-align:center;padding:12px;background:var(--gglow);border-radius:var(--r2)">
              <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:var(--gn1);font-family:var(--f-m)">${healthy}</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1);margin-top:3px">Healthy</div>
            </div>
            <div style="text-align:center;padding:12px;background:var(--aglow);border-radius:var(--r2)">
              <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:var(--am1);font-family:var(--f-m)">${lowStockCount}</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--am1);margin-top:3px">Low Stock</div>
            </div>
            <div style="text-align:center;padding:12px;background:var(--rglow);border-radius:var(--r2)">
              <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:var(--rd1);font-family:var(--f-m)">${outOfStock}</div>
