              <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--rd1);margin-top:3px">Out of Stock</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          ${lowStock.length?`
          <div style="margin-top:14px;border-top:1px solid var(--bdr);padding-top:12px">
            <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px;margin-bottom:8px">Critical Items</div>
            ${lowStock.slice(0,4).map(p=>`
              <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr)">
                <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t1);font-weight:600">${esc(p.name)}</span>
                <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;font-family:var(--f-m);color:${p.stock<=0?'var(--rd1)':'var(--am1)'}">
                  ${p.stock<=0?'Out':''+p.stock+' left'}
                </span>
              </div>
            `).join('')}
          </div>`:''}
        `;
      })
      .getInventoryOverview({_token:_sessionToken});
  }

  // STORE CHART
  const stores=data.storeComparison||[];
  const maxSt=Math.max(...stores.map(s=>s.revenue||0),1);
  const stEl=$('execStoreChart');
  if(stEl) stEl.innerHTML=stores.map((s,i)=>`
    <div class="hbar-row">
      <div class="hbar-top">
        <span class="hbar-name">${s.store}</span>
        <span class="hbar-val">${fmt(s.revenue)}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="hbar-bg">
        <div class="hbar-fg" style="width:${Math.round(((s.revenue||0)/maxSt)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--gn),var(--gn1));animation-delay:${i*0.1}s"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('')||'<div style="color:var(--t3);font-size:13px;padding:10px">No data</div>';

// BEST DAY OF WEEK
  const bdEl=$('execBestDay');
  if(bdEl){
    const weekly=data.weeklySales||[];
    const maxRev=Math.max(...weekly.map(d=>d.revenue||0),1);
    const maxCnt=Math.max(...weekly.map(d=>d.count||0),1);
    bdEl.innerHTML=weekly.map(d=>`
      <div style="padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr)">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:4px">
          <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${d.day}</span>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px">
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--b1)">&#8358;${(d.revenue||0).toLocaleString()}</span>
            <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3)">${d.count||0} txns</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:4px">
          <div style="flex:1;height:4px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden">
            <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round(((d.revenue||0)/maxRev)*100)}%;background:var(--b1);border-radius:3px;transition:width .7s ease"></div>
          </div>
          <div style="flex:1;height:4px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden">
            <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round(((d.count||0)/maxCnt)*100)}%;background:var(--gn1);border-radius:3px;transition:width .7s ease"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
  }

  // TOP SELLERS
  const tsEl=$('execTopSellers');
  if(tsEl){
    const sellers=data.topSellers||[];
    if(!sellers.length){
      tsEl.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);padding:10px 0">No sales data</div>';
    } else {
      const maxS=Math.max(...sellers.map(s=>s.revenue||0),1);
      tsEl.innerHTML=sellers.slice(0,5).map((s,i)=>`
        <div style="padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr)">
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:4px">
            <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
              <div style="width:20px;height:20px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--bglow);color:var(--b1);font-size:10px;font-weight:800;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">${i+1}</div>
              <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${s.name}</span>
            </div>
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--gn1)">&#8358;${(s.revenue||0).toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
          <div style="height:4px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden">
            <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round(((s.revenue||0)/maxS)*100)}%;background:var(--gn1);border-radius:3px;transition:width .7s ease"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }
  }

  // STOCK HEALTH DONUT (new slot)
  const sh2El=$('execStockHealth2');
  if(sh2El){
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(inv=>{
        const allP=[...(inv.electronics||inv.products||[]),...(inv.windowblind||[])];
        const tot=allP.length||1;
        const out=allP.filter(p=>p.stock<=0).length;
        const low=allP.filter(p=>p.stock>0&&p.stock<=p.reorder).length;
        const healthy=tot-out-low;
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width=90; canvas.height=90;
        canvas.style.cssText='width:90px;height:90px;flex-shrink:0';
        const legend=document.createElement('div');
        legend.style.cssText='flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px';
        legend.innerHTML=[
          {l:'Healthy',v:healthy,c:'var(--gn1)'},
          {l:'Low Stock',v:low,c:'var(--am1)'},
          {l:'Out of Stock',v:out,c:'var(--rd1)'}
        ].map(x=>`
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12px">
            <div style="width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:${x.c};flex-shrink:0"></div>
            <span style="flex:1;color:var(--t2);font-weight:600">${x.l}</span>
            <span style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${x.v}</span>
          </div>
        `).join('');
        sh2El.appendChild(canvas);
        sh2El.appendChild(legend);
        setTimeout(()=>drawDonut(canvas,[healthy,low,out],['#0f9f76','#e8930f','#e02424']),150);
      })
      .getInventoryOverview({_token:_sessionToken});
  }
}

function renderDashSales(data,from,to){
  const fmt=n=>'₦'+(+n||0).toLocaleString();
  const totalRev=data.totalRevenue||0;
  const totalProfit=data.totalProfit||0;
  const totalCost=totalRev-totalProfit;
  const avgSale=data.avgSaleValue||0;
  const dailyTrend=data.dailyTrend||[];
  const topProds=data.topProducts||[];
  const bottomProds=data.bottomProducts||[];
  const allProds=data.allProducts||[];

  $('dashBd').innerHTML=`
    <!-- KPI CARDS -->
    <div class="exec-kpis" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);margin-bottom:20px" id="salesKpis"></div>

    <!-- DAILY TREND LINE CHART -->
    <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:16px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t1)">Trend of Sales by Day</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:2px">Daily revenue for selected period</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <canvas id="salesDailyChart" style="width:100%;height:200px"></canvas>
    </div>

    <!-- TOP & BOTTOM PRODUCTS -->
    <div class="dg2" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Top 5 Selling Products</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Best performing by revenue</div>
        <div class="hbar mt8" id="salesTopChart"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Bottom 5 Selling Products</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Lowest performing — consider promotion</div>
        <div class="hbar mt8" id="salesBotChart"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- TRANSACTION COUNT BY PRODUCT -->
    <div class="cc" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="cc-h">Transaction Count by Product</div>
      <div class="cc-s">How many times each product was sold</div>
      <div class="hbar mt8" id="salesTxnChart"></div>
    </div>

    <!-- HEATMAP + BEST DAY -->
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:2fr 1fr;gap:14px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Hourly Sales Heatmap</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Revenue intensity by hour of day</div>
        <div id="salesHeatmap" style="margin-top:12px;overflow-x:auto"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Best Day of Week</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Revenue &amp; transactions by day</div>
        <div id="salesBestDay" style="margin-top:10px"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  // KPI CARDS
  const todayRevS=data.todayRevenue||0;
  const todayProfitS=data.todayProfit||0;
  const todayCostS=todayRevS-todayProfitS;
  const todayAvgS=data.todaySales>0?Math.round(todayRevS/data.todaySales):0;

  const kpis=[
    {l:'Total Sales',v:totalRev,pre:'₦',ico:'payments',color:'#2254e0',bg:'var(--bglow)',today:todayRevS},
    {l:'Total Profit',v:totalProfit,pre:'₦',ico:'trending_up',color:'var(--gn1)',bg:'var(--gglow)',today:todayProfitS},
    {l:'Total Cost',v:totalCost,pre:'₦',ico:'production_quantity_limits',color:'var(--rd1)',bg:'var(--rglow)',today:todayCostS},
    {l:'Avg Sale Value',v:avgSale,pre:'₦',ico:'calculate',color:'var(--am1)',bg:'var(--aglow)',today:todayAvgS},
  ];
  const kd=$('salesKpis');
  kpis.forEach((k,i)=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.className='exec-kpi';
    el.style.animationDelay=`${i*0.08}s`;
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px">
        <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:10px;background:${k.bg};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">
          <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:19px;color:${k.color}">${k.ico}</span>
        </div>
        <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px">${k.l}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="exec-kpi-v" style="color:${k.color}">—</div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:8px">
        <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t4)">Today</span>
        <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;font-family:var(--f-m);color:${k.color}">&#8358;${Number(k.today||0).toLocaleString()}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="height:2px;background:${k.bg};border-radius:2px;margin-top:8px">
        <div style="height:100%;width:${k.v>0?Math.min(100,Math.round((k.today/k.v)*100)):0}%;background:${k.color};border-radius:2px;transition:width .8s ease"></div>
      </div>
    `;
    kd.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(()=>countUp(el.querySelector('.exec-kpi-v'),k.v,k.pre),i*120);
  });

  // DAILY LINE CHART
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const canvas=$('salesDailyChart');
    if(!canvas||!dailyTrend.length) return;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width=canvas.offsetWidth*2;
    canvas.height=400;
    canvas.style.height='200px';
    const pts=dailyTrend.map(d=>d.revenue||0);
    const labels=dailyTrend.map(d=>{
      const dt=new Date(d.date);
      return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{day:'numeric',month:'short'});
    });
    const W=canvas.width,H=canvas.height;
    const pad={t:30,r:20,b:40,l:70};
    const maxV=Math.max(...pts,1);
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);

    // Grid
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.08)'; ctx.lineWidth=1;
    for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){
      const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-i/4);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pad.l,y); ctx.lineTo(W-pad.r,y); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.5)'; ctx.font='22px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign='right';
      ctx.fillText('₦'+((maxV*i/4)/1000).toFixed(0)+'k',pad.l-8,y+8);
    }

    // Show max 15 labels
    const step=(W-pad.l-pad.r)/(pts.length-1||1);
    const showEvery=Math.ceil(pts.length/15);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.6)'; ctx.font='20px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign='center';
    pts.forEach((_,i)=>{ if(i%showEvery===0) ctx.fillText(labels[i]||'',pad.l+i*step,H-8); });

    // Gradient fill
    const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,pad.t,0,H-pad.b);
    grad.addColorStop(0,'rgba(34,84,224,0.18)'); grad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(34,84,224,0)');
    ctx.beginPath();
    pts.forEach((v,i)=>{ const x=pad.l+i*step; const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV); i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y); });
    ctx.lineTo(pad.l+(pts.length-1)*step,H-pad.b); ctx.lineTo(pad.l,H-pad.b);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle=grad; ctx.fill();

    // Line
    ctx.strokeStyle='#2254e0'; ctx.lineWidth=3; ctx.lineJoin='round';
    ctx.beginPath();
    pts.forEach((v,i)=>{ const x=pad.l+i*step; const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV); i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y); });
    ctx.stroke();

    // Dots — only if few points
    if(pts.length<=30){
      pts.forEach((v,i)=>{
        const x=pad.l+i*step; const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,5,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fillStyle='#2254e0'; ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,3,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; ctx.fill();
      });
    }
  },200);

  // TOP 5
  const maxT=Math.max(...topProds.map(p=>p.revenue||0),1);
  $('salesTopChart').innerHTML=topProds.map((p,i)=>`
    <div class="hbar-row">
      <div class="hbar-top"><span class="hbar-name">${i+1}. ${esc(p.name)}</span><span class="hbar-val">${fmt(p.revenue)}</span></div>
      <div class="hbar-bg"><div class="hbar-fg" style="width:${Math.round(((p.revenue||0)/maxT)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--b),var(--b2));animation-delay:${i*0.1}s"></div></div>
    </div>
  `).join('')||'<div style="color:var(--t3);font-size:13px;padding:10px">No data</div>';

  // BOTTOM 5
  const maxB=Math.max(...bottomProds.map(p=>p.revenue||0),1);
  $('salesBotChart').innerHTML=bottomProds.map((p,i)=>`
    <div class="hbar-row">
      <div class="hbar-top"><span class="hbar-name">${i+1}. ${esc(p.name)}</span><span class="hbar-val" style="color:var(--rd1)">${fmt(p.revenue)}</span></div>
      <div class="hbar-bg"><div class="hbar-fg" style="width:${Math.round(((p.revenue||0)/maxB)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--rd1),#f87171);animation-delay:${i*0.1}s"></div></div>
    </div>
  `).join('')||'<div style="color:var(--t3);font-size:13px;padding:10px">No data</div>';

  // TRANSACTION COUNT
  const sorted=allProds.slice(0,10);
  const maxC=Math.max(...sorted.map(p=>p.count||0),1);
  $('salesTxnChart').innerHTML=sorted.map((p,i)=>`
    <div class="hbar-row">
      <div class="hbar-top"><span class="hbar-name">${esc(p.name)}</span><span class="hbar-val" style="color:var(--b1)">${p.count||0} txns</span></div>
      <div class="hbar-bg"><div class="hbar-fg" style="width:${Math.round(((p.count||0)/maxC)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,#8b5cf6,#a78bfa);animation-delay:${i*0.1}s"></div></div>
    </div>
  `).join('')||'<div style="color:var(--t3);font-size:13px;padding:10px">No data</div>';

  // HOURLY HEATMAP
  const hmEl=$('salesHeatmap');
  if(hmEl){
    const hourly=data.hourlyData||Array.from({length:24},(_,h)=>({hour:h,revenue:0,count:0}));
    const maxHR=Math.max(...hourly.map(h=>h.revenue||0),1);
    const hours=Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>i);
    hmEl.innerHTML=`
      <div style="display:flex;gap:3px;margin-bottom:4px;padding-left:36px">
        ${hours.map(h=>`<div style="flex:1;text-align:center;font-size:8px;color:var(--t4);font-weight:700">${h%3===0?h+'h':''}</div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:3px;align-items:center">
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--t4);font-weight:700;width:32px;flex-shrink:0;text-align:right;padding-right:4px">Sales</div>
        ${hourly.map(h=>{
          const alpha=0.08+((h.revenue||0)/maxHR)*0.85;
          const rev=(h.revenue||0);
          const cnt=(h.count||0);
          return '<div style="flex:1;height:28px;border-radius:3px;background:rgba(34,84,224,'+alpha.toFixed(2)+');cursor:default;position:relative;transition:transform .1s" title="'+h.hour+':00 - Revenue: \u20a6'+rev.toLocaleString()+' | '+cnt+' sales" onmouseover="this.style.transform=\'scale(1.15)\';this.style.zIndex=\'10\'" onmouseout="this.style.transform=\'scale(1)\';this.style.zIndex=\'1\'"></div>';
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:3px;align-items:center;margin-top:3px">
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--t4);font-weight:700;width:32px;flex-shrink:0;text-align:right;padding-right:4px">Txns</div>
        ${hourly.map(h=>{
          const maxC2=Math.max(...hourly.map(x=>x.count||0),1);
          const alpha=0.08+((h.count||0)/maxC2)*0.85;
          return '<div style="flex:1;height:28px;border-radius:3px;background:rgba(15,159,118,'+alpha.toFixed(2)+');cursor:default" title="'+h.hour+':00 - '+h.count+' transactions" onmouseover="this.style.transform=\'scale(1.15)\';this.style.zIndex=\'10\'" onmouseout="this.style.transform=\'scale(1)\';this.style.zIndex=\'1\'"></div>';
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:8px;padding-top:6px;border-top:1px solid var(--bdr)">
        <span style="font-size:9px;color:var(--t4)">Low activity</span>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:2px">
          ${[0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9].map(a=>'<div style="width:16px;height:8px;border-radius:2px;background:rgba(34,84,224,'+a+')"></div>').join('')}
        </div>
        <span style="font-size:9px;color:var(--t4)">High activity</span>
      </div>
    `;
    // Peak hour callout
    const peakHour=hourly.reduce((a,b)=>(b.revenue||0)>(a.revenue||0)?b:a,hourly[0]);
    if(peakHour&&peakHour.revenue>0){
      hmEl.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
        <div style="margin-top:10px;padding:8px 10px;background:var(--bglow2);border:1px solid rgba(34,84,224,.15);border-radius:var(--r4);font-size:11px;color:var(--b1);font-weight:700;display:flex;justify-content:space-between">
          <span>Peak Hour: ${peakHour.hour}:00 – ${(peakHour.hour+1)}:00</span>
          <span style="font-family:var(--f-m)">&#8358;${(peakHour.revenue||0).toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>
      `);
    }
  }

  // BEST DAY
  const bdEl2=$('salesBestDay');
  if(bdEl2){
    const weekly=data.weeklySales||[];
    const maxR=Math.max(...weekly.map(d=>d.revenue||0),1);
    const maxC3=Math.max(...weekly.map(d=>d.count||0),1);
    bdEl2.innerHTML=weekly.map(d=>`
      <div style="padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr)">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:5px">
          <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${d.day}</span>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center">
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--b1)">&#8358;${(d.revenue||0).toLocaleString()}</span>
            <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3);background:var(--s2);padding:1px 6px;border-radius:10px">${d.count||0}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:4px">
          <div style="flex:1;height:4px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden" title="Revenue">
            <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round(((d.revenue||0)/maxR)*100)}%;background:var(--b1);border-radius:3px;transition:width .7s ease"></div>
          </div>
          <div style="flex:1;height:4px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden" title="Transactions">
            <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round(((d.count||0)/maxC3)*100)}%;background:var(--gn1);border-radius:3px;transition:width .7s ease"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
  }

  renderDashPageNav('sales');
}

function renderDashExpense(data,from,to){
  const fmt=n=>'₦'+(+n||0).toLocaleString();
  const totalExp=data.totalExpenses||0;
  const totalRev=data.totalRevenue||0;
  const totalProfit=data.totalProfit||0;
  const margin=totalRev>0?((totalProfit/totalRev)*100).toFixed(1):0;
  const expBreak=data.expenseBreakdown||[];
  const avgSale=data.avgSaleValue||0;
  const expCount=expBreak.length;
  const dailyTrend=data.dailyTrend||[];

  $('dashBd').innerHTML=`
    <div class="exec-kpis" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);margin-bottom:20px" id="expKpis"></div>

    <div class="dg2" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <!-- Expenses by type -->
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Expenses by Type</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Breakdown by expense category</div>
        <div class="hbar mt8" id="expTypeChart"></div>
      </div>
      <!-- Expense donut -->
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Expense % of Total</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Each category's share of expenses</div>
        <div class="donut-wrap" id="expDonut" style="margin-top:16px"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Sales vs Expenses comparison -->
    <div class="cc" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="cc-h">Total Sales vs Total Expenses</div>
      <div class="cc-s">Profitability analysis</div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px;margin-top:16px" id="expVsRev"></div>
    </div>

    <!-- Daily expense trend -->
    <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="margin-bottom:16px">
        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t1)">Revenue vs Expenses Trend</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:2px">Daily comparison for selected period</div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:16px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:11px;color:var(--t3)">
          <div style="width:12px;height:3px;background:var(--b1);border-radius:3px"></div>Revenue
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:11px;color:var(--t3)">
          <div style="width:12px;height:3px;background:var(--rd1);border-radius:3px;border-style:dashed"></div>Expenses
        </div>
      </div>
      <canvas id="expTrendChart" style="width:100%;height:200px"></canvas>
    </div>

    <!-- EXPENSE TREND + BEST DAY -->
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px;margin-bottom:16px">
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Best Day of Week</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Revenue &amp; transactions by day</div>
        <div id="expBestDay" style="margin-top:10px"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="cc">
        <div class="cc-h">Expense Breakdown Summary</div>
        <div class="cc-s">Top categories at a glance</div>
        <div id="expSummary" style="margin-top:10px"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  // KPI CARDS
  const todayExpE=data.todayExpenses||0;
  const todayRevE=data.todayRevenue||0;
  const todayProfitE=data.todayProfit||0;
  const todayMarginE=todayRevE>0?((todayProfitE/todayRevE)*100).toFixed(1):0;
  const todayAvgE=data.todaySales>0?Math.round(todayRevE/data.todaySales):0;

  const kpis=[
    {l:'Total Expenses',v:totalExp,pre:'₦',ico:'receipt_long',color:'var(--rd1)',bg:'var(--rglow)',today:todayExpE},
    {l:'Profit Margin',v:margin,pre:'',suf:'%',ico:'percent',color:'#8b5cf6',bg:'rgba(139,92,246,.1)',today:todayMarginE,todayLabel:todayMarginE+'%'},
    {l:'Expense Categories',v:expCount,pre:'',ico:'category',color:'var(--am1)',bg:'var(--aglow)',today:null},
    {l:'Avg Sale Value',v:avgSale,pre:'₦',ico:'calculate',color:'var(--b1)',bg:'var(--bglow)',today:todayAvgE},
  ];
  const kd=$('expKpis');
  kpis.forEach((k,i)=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.className='exec-kpi';
    el.style.animationDelay=`${i*0.08}s`;
    const todayStr=k.today===null?'—':(k.todayLabel||('&#8358;'+Number(k.today||0).toLocaleString()));
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px">
        <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:10px;background:${k.bg};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">
          <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:19px;color:${k.color}">${k.ico}</span>
        </div>
        <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px">${k.l}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="exec-kpi-v" style="color:${k.color}">—</div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:8px">
        <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t4)">Today</span>
        <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;font-family:var(--f-m);color:${k.color}">${todayStr}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="height:2px;background:${k.bg};border-radius:2px;margin-top:8px">
        <div style="height:100%;width:${k.today&&k.v>0?Math.min(100,Math.round((k.today/k.v)*100)):0}%;background:${k.color};border-radius:2px;transition:width .8s ease"></div>
      </div>
    `;
    kd.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(()=>countUp(el.querySelector('.exec-kpi-v'),k.v,k.pre,k.suf),i*120);
  });

  // EXPENSES BY TYPE
  const maxE=Math.max(...expBreak.map(e=>e.amount||0),1);
  $('expTypeChart').innerHTML=expBreak.slice(0,8).map((e,i)=>`
    <div class="hbar-row">
      <div class="hbar-top"><span class="hbar-name">${e.type}</span><span class="hbar-val" style="color:var(--rd1)">${fmt(e.amount)}</span></div>
      <div class="hbar-bg"><div class="hbar-fg" style="width:${Math.round(((e.amount||0)/maxE)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--rd1),#f87171);animation-delay:${i*0.1}s"></div></div>
    </div>
  `).join('')||'<div style="color:var(--t3);font-size:13px;padding:10px">No expenses recorded</div>';

  // EXPENSE DONUT
  const expColors=['#2254e0','#0a7c5c','#c97c0a','#e02424','#8b5cf6','#ec4899','#14b8a6','#f97316'];
  const expVals=expBreak.slice(0,8).map(e=>e.amount||0);
  const expLabels=expBreak.slice(0,8).map(e=>e.type);
  const pdWrap=$('expDonut');
  const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width=100; canvas.height=100;
  canvas.style.cssText='width:100px;height:100px;flex-shrink:0';
  const legend=document.createElement('div'); legend.className='donut-legend';
  const totalE=expVals.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)||1;
  legend.innerHTML=expVals.map((v,i)=>`
    <div class="donut-item">
      <div class="donut-dot" style="background:${expColors[i]}"></div>
      <div class="donut-n">${expLabels[i]}</div>
      <div class="donut-v">${Math.round((v/totalE)*100)}%</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
  pdWrap.appendChild(canvas); pdWrap.appendChild(legend);
  setTimeout(()=>drawDonut(canvas,expVals,expColors),100);

  // SALES VS EXPENSES
  const vsEl=$('expVsRev');
  const maxVs=Math.max(totalRev,totalExp,1);
  vsEl.innerHTML=`
    <div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:6px">
        <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Total Revenue</span>
        <span style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1)">${fmt(totalRev)}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="height:10px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:5px;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round((totalRev/maxVs)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--gn),var(--gn1));border-radius:5px;transition:width .8s ease"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:6px">
        <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Total Expenses</span>
        <span style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--rd1)">${fmt(totalExp)}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="height:10px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:5px;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round((totalExp/maxVs)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--rd1),#f87171);border-radius:5px;transition:width .8s ease"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:6px">
        <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Net Profit</span>
        <span style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--b1)">${fmt(totalProfit)}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="height:10px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:5px;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round((totalProfit/maxVs)*100)}%;background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--b),var(--b2));border-radius:5px;transition:width .8s ease"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  // DAILY TREND
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const canvas=$('expTrendChart');
    if(!canvas||!dailyTrend.length) return;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width=canvas.offsetWidth*2;
    canvas.height=400; canvas.style.height='200px';
    const pts=dailyTrend.map(d=>d.revenue||0);
    const labels=dailyTrend.map(d=>{ const dt=new Date(d.date); return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{day:'numeric',month:'short'}); });
    const W=canvas.width,H=canvas.height;
    const pad={t:30,r:20,b:40,l:70};
    const maxV=Math.max(...pts,1);
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.08)'; ctx.lineWidth=1;
    for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){
      const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-i/4);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pad.l,y); ctx.lineTo(W-pad.r,y); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.5)'; ctx.font='22px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign='right';
      ctx.fillText('₦'+((maxV*i/4)/1000).toFixed(0)+'k',pad.l-8,y+8);
    }
    const step=(W-pad.l-pad.r)/(pts.length-1||1);
    const showEvery=Math.ceil(pts.length/15);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(100,120,180,0.6)'; ctx.font='20px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign='center';
    pts.forEach((_,i)=>{ if(i%showEvery===0) ctx.fillText(labels[i]||'',pad.l+i*step,H-8); });
    const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,pad.t,0,H-pad.b);
    grad.addColorStop(0,'rgba(224,36,36,0.15)'); grad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(224,36,36,0)');
    ctx.beginPath();
    pts.forEach((v,i)=>{ const x=pad.l+i*step; const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV); i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y); });
    ctx.lineTo(pad.l+(pts.length-1)*step,H-pad.b); ctx.lineTo(pad.l,H-pad.b);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle=grad; ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='var(--rd1)'; ctx.lineWidth=3; ctx.lineJoin='round';
    ctx.beginPath();
    pts.forEach((v,i)=>{ const x=pad.l+i*step; const y=pad.t+(H-pad.t-pad.b)*(1-v/maxV); i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y); });
    ctx.stroke();
  },200);
// BEST DAY
  const expBdEl=$('expBestDay');
  if(expBdEl){
    const weekly=data.weeklySales||[];
    const maxR=Math.max(...weekly.map(d=>d.revenue||0),1);
    const maxC=Math.max(...weekly.map(d=>d.count||0),1);
    expBdEl.innerHTML=weekly.map(d=>`
      <div style="padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr)">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:5px">
          <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${d.day}</span>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center">
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--b1)">&#8358;${(d.revenue||0).toLocaleString()}</span>
            <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3);background:var(--s2);padding:1px 6px;border-radius:10px">${d.count||0}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="height:4px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden">
          <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round(((d.revenue||0)/maxR)*100)}%;background:var(--b1);border-radius:3px;transition:width .7s ease"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
  }

  // EXPENSE SUMMARY
  const expSumEl=$('expSummary');
  if(expSumEl){
    const top5=expBreak.slice(0,5);
    const totalE=top5.reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.amount||0),0)||1;
    expSumEl.innerHTML=top5.map(e=>`
      <div style="padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr)">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:4px">
          <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:var(--t1)">${e.type}</span>
          <span style="font-size:11px;font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--rd1)">&#8358;${(e.amount||0).toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
          <div style="flex:1;height:4px;background:var(--s2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden">
            <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.round(((e.amount||0)/totalE)*100)}%;background:var(--rd1);border-radius:3px;transition:width .7s ease"></div>
          </div>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3);white-space:nowrap">${Math.round(((e.amount||0)/totalE)*100)}%</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
  }
  

  renderDashPageNav('expense');
}
function renderDashInventory(data,from,to){
  const lowStock=data.lowStock||[];

  $('dashBd').innerHTML=`
    <!-- KPI CARDS -->
    <div class="exec-kpis" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);margin-bottom:20px" id="invKpis"></div>

   

      <!-- INVENTORY TABS -->
    <div class="cc" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;padding:4px;background:var(--s1);border-radius:var(--r3);border:1px solid var(--bdr)">
          <button class="att-tab on" id="invTabEl" onclick="switchInvTab('el')" style="white-space:nowrap;padding:8px 16px">
            <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:14px">inventory_2</span>
            All Products
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="sw" style="width:200px">
          <span class="material-icons-round ico" style="font-size:16px">search</span>
          <input class="sin" id="invSearch" placeholder="Search product…" oninput="filterInvTable(_invActiveTab)" style="padding-left:34px;font-size:12px;height:34px">
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Electronics table -->
      <div id="invElPanel">
        <div style="overflow-x:auto;max-height:420px;overflow-y:auto">
          <table class="staff-table" id="invElTable">
            <thead style="position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--s0);z-index:1">
              <tr>
                <th>Product Name</th>
                <th>Category</th>
                <th style="text-align:center">Stock</th>
                <th style="text-align:center">Reorder</th>
                <th style="text-align:center">Status</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody id="invElBody">
              <tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">
                <span class="material-icons-round spin">sync</span>
              </td></tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  // KPI CARDS — use cache or load from server
  const _storeFilter=$('dashStoreFilter')?$('dashStoreFilter').value||'':'';
  const _renderInvKPIs=inv=>{
      const elProds=inv.electronics||inv.products||[];
      const wbProds=inv.windowblind||[];
      const allProds=[...elProds,...wbProds];
      const totalIn=allProds.reduce((s,p)=>s+(p.stock||0),0);
      const outOfStock=allProds.filter(p=>p.stock<=0).length;
      const lowStockCount=allProds.filter(p=>p.stock>0&&p.stock<=p.reorder).length;
      const turnover=allProds.length>0?(outOfStock/allProds.length*100).toFixed(1):0;
      const totalStockValue=allProds.reduce((s,p)=>s+(p.stock||0)*((p.cost||0)+(p.expenseOnCost||0)),0);

      const kpis=[
        {l:'Total Stock Units',v:totalIn,pre:'',ico:'inventory_2',color:'var(--b1)',bg:'var(--bglow)'},
        {l:'Total Stock Value',v:totalStockValue,pre:'₦',ico:'payments',color:'var(--gn1)',bg:'var(--gglow)'},
        {l:'Out of Stock',v:outOfStock,pre:'',ico:'remove_shopping_cart',color:'var(--rd1)',bg:'var(--rglow)'},
        {l:'Low Stock Items',v:lowStockCount,pre:'',ico:'warning',color:'var(--am1)',bg:'var(--aglow)'},
        {l:'Stock Out Rate',v:turnover,pre:'',suf:'%',ico:'percent',color:'#8b5cf6',bg:'rgba(139,92,246,.1)'},
      ];
      const kd=$('invKpis');
      if(!kd) return;
      kd.innerHTML='';
      kpis.forEach((k,i)=>{
        const el=document.createElement('div');
        el.className='exec-kpi';
        el.style.animationDelay=`${i*0.08}s`;
        el.innerHTML=`
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px">
            <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:10px;background:${k.bg};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">
              <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:19px;color:${k.color}">${k.ico}</span>
            </div>
            <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px">${k.l}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="exec-kpi-v" style="color:${k.color}">—</div>
        `;
        kd.appendChild(el);
        setTimeout(()=>countUp(el.querySelector('.exec-kpi-v'),k.v,k.pre,k.suf),i*120);
      });

      // ELECTRONICS TABLE
      _invElData=[...elProds,...wbProds];
      _invWbData=[];
      renderInvTable('el',_invElData);
  };
  if(_invOverviewCache&&!_storeFilter){
    _renderInvKPIs(_invOverviewCache);
  } else {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(inv=>{ if(!_storeFilter) _invOverviewCache=inv; _renderInvKPIs(inv); })
      .getInventoryOverview({store:_storeFilter||null,_token:_sessionToken});
  }
}

let _invElData=[], _invWbData=[];

function filterInvTable(type){
  const q=($('invSearch')?$('invSearch').value||'':'').toLowerCase();
  const data=type==='el'?_invElData:_invWbData;
  renderInvTable(type, data.filter(p=>(p.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(p.category||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
}

let _invActiveTab='el';

function switchInvTab(tab){
  _invActiveTab='el';
  const s=$('invSearch');
  if(s) s.value='';
  renderInvTable('el', _invElData);
}

function renderInvTable(type, prods){
  const tbody=$(type==='el'?'invElBody':'invWbBody');
  if(!tbody) return;
  if(!prods.length){ tbody.innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--t3)">No products found</td></tr>`; return; }
  const statusBadge=(stock,reorder)=>{
    if(stock<=0) return `<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--rglow);color:var(--rd1)">Out of Stock</span>`;
    if(stock<=reorder) return `<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--aglow);color:var(--am1)">Need to Reorder</span>`;
    return `<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--gglow);color:var(--gn1)">Available</span>`;
  };
  if(type==='el'){
    tbody.innerHTML=prods.map(p=>`
      <tr>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(p.name)}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">${esc(p.category||'—')}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:${p.stock<=0?'var(--rd1)':p.stock<=p.reorder?'var(--am1)':'var(--t1)'}">${p.stock}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;color:var(--t3)">${p.reorder}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center">${statusBadge(p.stock,p.reorder)}</td>
      </tr>
    `).join('');
  } else {
    tbody.innerHTML=prods.map(p=>`
      <tr>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(p.name)}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:${p.stock<=0?'var(--rd1)':p.stock<=p.reorder?'var(--am1)':'var(--t1)'}">${p.stock}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;color:var(--t3)">${p.unit||'—'}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;color:var(--t3)">${p.reorder}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center">${statusBadge(p.stock,p.reorder)}</td>
      </tr>
    `).join('');
  }
}


function renderDashMovement(data,from,to){
  $('dashBd').innerHTML=`
    <div class="cc" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px">
        <div>
          <div class="cc-h" style="margin:0">Inventory Movement</div>
          <div class="cc-s">All stock in and out transactions</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          <div class="sw" style="width:180px">
            <span class="material-icons-round ico" style="font-size:16px">search</span>
            <input class="sin" id="invMvSearch" placeholder="Search product…" oninput="filterInvMovement()" style="padding-left:34px;font-size:12px;height:34px">
          </div>
          <select class="fc" id="invMvStore" onchange="filterInvMovement()" style="font-size:12px;height:34px;width:160px">
            <option value="">All Stores</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto;max-height:500px;overflow-y:auto">
        <table class="staff-table">
          <thead style="position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--s0);z-index:1">
            <tr>
              <th>Product</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Action</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Qty</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Balance After</th>
              <th>Performed By</th>
              <th>Date</th>
              <th>Time</th>
              <th>Store</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="invMvBody">
            <tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">
              <span class="material-icons-round spin">sync</span>
            </td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      <div id="invMvPager" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;margin-top:14px"></div>
    </div>
  `;

  // Populate store filter dynamically
  const invMvSel=$('invMvStore');
  if(invMvSel&&user.availableStores){
    invMvSel.innerHTML='<option value="">All Stores</option>';
    user.availableStores.forEach(s=>invMvSel.add(new Option(s,s)));
  }
  if(_invMvLoaded && _invMvData.length){
    _invMvPage=1; renderInvMovement();
  } else {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(rows=>{
        _invMvData=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];
        _invMvLoaded=true;
        _invMvPage=1;
        renderInvMovement();
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('invMvBody').innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--rd1)">${e.message}</td></tr>`; })
      .getInventoryMovement({from,to,days:14,_token:_sessionToken});
  }
  renderDashPageNav('movement');
}

let _invMvData=[], _invMvPage=1;
const _invMvPerPage=20;

function filterInvMovement(){
  _invMvPage=1;
  renderInvMovement();
}

function renderInvMovement(){
  const q=($('invMvSearch')?$('invMvSearch').value||'':'').toLowerCase();
  const store=($('invMvStore')?$('invMvStore').value||'':'').toLowerCase();
  let rows=_invMvData.filter(r=>{
    if(q&&!(r.product||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
    if(store&&!(r.store||'').toLowerCase().includes(store)) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const total=rows.length;
  const maxPage=Math.ceil(total/_invMvPerPage)||1;
  _invMvPage=Math.max(1,Math.min(_invMvPage,maxPage));
  const start=(_invMvPage-1)*_invMvPerPage;
  const page=rows.slice(start,start+_invMvPerPage);

  const tbody=$('invMvBody');
  if(!tbody) return;

  if(!page.length){
    tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">No records found</td></tr>';
    $('invMvPager').innerHTML='';
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML=page.map(r=>{
    const isOut=(r.action||'').toUpperCase().includes('OUT')||(r.action||'').toUpperCase().includes('SALE');
    return `
      <tr>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(r.product)}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center">
          <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;
            background:${isOut?'var(--rglow)':'var(--gglow)'};
            color:${isOut?'var(--rd1)':'var(--gn1)'}">
            ${r.action||'—'}
          </span>
        </td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700">${r.qty}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--b1);font-weight:700">${r.balance}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t2)">${r.by||'—'}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">${r.date}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:12px">${r.time}</td>
        <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-size:11px">${r.store}</td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');

  // Pagination
  const pager=$('invMvPager');
  if(!pager) return;
  if(maxPage<=1){ pager.innerHTML=''; return; }
  pager.innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_invMvPage--;renderInvMovement()" ${_invMvPage===1?'disabled':''}>
      <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span>
    </button>
    <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Page ${_invMvPage} of ${maxPage} · ${total} records</span>
    <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_invMvPage++;renderInvMovement()" ${_invMvPage===maxPage?'disabled':''}>
      <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
    </button>
  `;
}

function renderDashArchived(data,from,to){
  $('dashBd').innerHTML=`
    <div class="cc" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px">
        <div>
          <div class="cc-h" style="margin:0">Archived Sales</div>
          <div class="cc-s">Reversed and undone transactions · Audit trail</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          <div class="sw" style="width:180px">
            <span class="material-icons-round ico" style="font-size:16px">search</span>
            <input class="sin" id="archSearch" placeholder="Search…" oninput="filterArchived()" style="padding-left:34px;font-size:12px;height:34px">
          </div>
          <select class="fc" id="archStore" onchange="filterArchived()" style="font-size:12px;height:34px;width:160px">
            <option value="">All Stores</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto;max-height:500px;overflow-y:auto">
        <table class="staff-table">
          <thead style="position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--s0);z-index:1">
            <tr>
              <th>Sale ID</th>
              <th>Product</th>
              <th>Sold By</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Qty</th>
              <th style="text-align:right">Amount</th>
              <th>Payment</th>
              <th>Original Date</th>
              <th>Undo Date</th>
              <th>Undone By</th>
              <th>Store</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="archBody">
            <tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">
              <span class="material-icons-round spin">sync</span>
            </td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      <div id="archPager" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;margin-top:14px"></div>
    </div>
  `;

  const archSel=$('archStore');
  if(archSel&&user.availableStores){
    archSel.innerHTML='<option value="">All Stores</option>';
    user.availableStores.forEach(s=>archSel.add(new Option(s,s)));
  }
  if(_archLoaded && _archData.length){
    _archPage=1; renderArchived();
  } else {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(rows=>{
        _archData=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];
        _archLoaded=true;
        _archPage=1;
        renderArchived();
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('archBody').innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--rd1)">${e.message}</td></tr>`; })
      .getArchivedSales({from,to,_token:_sessionToken});
  }
  renderDashPageNav('archived');
}

let _archData=[], _archPage=1;
const _archPerPage=20;

function filterArchived(){
  _archPage=1;
  renderArchived();
}

function renderArchived(){
  const q=($('archSearch')?$('archSearch').value||'':'').toLowerCase();
  const store=($('archStore')?$('archStore').value||'':'').toLowerCase();
  let rows=_archData.filter(r=>{
    if(q&&!(r.product||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)&&!(r.saleId||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)&&!(r.soldBy||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
    if(store&&!(r.store||'').toLowerCase().includes(store)) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const total=rows.length;
  const maxPage=Math.ceil(total/_archPerPage)||1;
  _archPage=Math.max(1,Math.min(_archPage,maxPage));
  const start=(_archPage-1)*_archPerPage;
  const page=rows.slice(start,start+_archPerPage);

  const tbody=$('archBody');
  if(!tbody) return;

  if(!page.length){
    tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">No archived records found</td></tr>';
    $('archPager').innerHTML='';
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML=page.map(r=>`
    <tr>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:11px;color:var(--b1);font-weight:700">${r.saleId}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(r.product)}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t2)">${r.soldBy}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700">${r.qty}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;text-align:right;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:var(--rd1)">₦${Number(r.total).toLocaleString()}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">${r.payment}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">${r.originalDate} <span style="font-family:var(--f-m)">${r.originalTime}</span></td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">${r.undoDate||'—'}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t2);font-size:12px">${r.undoneBy||'—'}</td>
      <td style="padding:10px 14px;color:var(--t3);font-size:11px">${r.store}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');

  // Pagination
  const pager=$('archPager');
  if(!pager) return;
  if(maxPage<=1){ pager.innerHTML=''; return; }
  pager.innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_archPage--;renderArchived()" ${_archPage===1?'disabled':''}>
      <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span>
    </button>
    <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Page ${_archPage} of ${maxPage} · ${total} records</span>
    <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_archPage++;renderArchived()" ${_archPage===maxPage?'disabled':''}>
      <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
    </button>
  `;
}

function renderDashExpiry(){
  $('dashBd').innerHTML=`
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:13px;margin-bottom:20px" id="expiryKpis"></div>
    <div class="cc" style="margin-bottom:16px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px">
        <div>
          <div class="cc-h" style="margin:0">Product Expiry Status</div>
          <div class="cc-s">All products with expiry dates across all stores</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px">
          <button class="chip on" id="efAll" onclick="setExpiryFilter('all',this)">All</button>
          <button class="chip" id="efExpired" onclick="setExpiryFilter('expired',this)" style="color:var(--rd1)">🔴 Expired</button>
          <button class="chip" id="efCritical" onclick="setExpiryFilter('critical',this)" style="color:var(--rd1)">Critical (≤7d)</button>
          <button class="chip" id="efWarning" onclick="setExpiryFilter('warning',this)" style="color:var(--am1)">Warning (≤14d)</button>
          <button class="chip" id="efSoon" onclick="setExpiryFilter('soon',this)" style="color:var(--b1)">Soon (≤60d)</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto">
        <table class="staff-table" id="expiryTable">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Product</th>
              <th>Store</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Stock</th>
              <th>Expiry Date</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Days Left</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Status</th>
              <th style="text-align:center">Action</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="expiryBody">
            <tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)">
              <span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3">sync</span>
            </td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(alerts){
      if(!alerts||!Array.isArray(alerts)){
        $('expiryBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">No expiry data found</td></tr>';
        return;
      }
      window._expiryAlerts=alerts;
      // KPIs
      const expired=alerts.filter(a=>a.status==='expired');
      const critical=alerts.filter(a=>a.status==='critical');
      const warning=alerts.filter(a=>a.status==='warning');
      const soon=alerts.filter(a=>a.status==='soon');
      const kd=$('expiryKpis');
      kd.innerHTML='';
      [{l:'Expired — In Stock',v:expired.length,color:'var(--rd1)',bg:'var(--rglow)',ico:'error'},
       {l:'Critical (≤7 days)',v:critical.length,color:'var(--rd1)',bg:'var(--rglow)',ico:'warning'},
       {l:'Expiring Soon',v:warning.length+soon.length,color:'var(--am1)',bg:'var(--aglow)',ico:'schedule'}
      ].forEach(function(k){
        const el=document.createElement('div');
        el.className='exec-kpi';
        el.innerHTML=`
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px">
            <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:10px;background:${k.bg};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">
              <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:19px;color:${k.color}">${k.ico}</span>
            </div>
            <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px">${k.l}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="exec-kpi-v" style="color:${k.color}">${k.v}</div>
        `;
        kd.appendChild(el);
      });
      renderExpiryTable(alerts);
    })
    .getExpiryAlerts({_token:_sessionToken});
}

window._expiryAlerts=[];
window._expiryFilter='all';

function setExpiryFilter(filter,el){
  window._expiryFilter=filter;
  document.querySelectorAll('#dtab-expiry ~ * .chip, #dashBd .chip').forEach(function(c){ c.classList.remove('on'); });
  if(el) el.classList.add('on');
  const filtered=filter==='all'?window._expiryAlerts:window._expiryAlerts.filter(function(a){ return a.status===filter; });
  renderExpiryTable(filtered);
}

function renderExpiryTable(alerts){
  const tbody=$('expiryBody');
  if(!tbody) return;
  if(!alerts.length){
    tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">No products match this filter</td></tr>';
    return;
  }
  const statusBadge=function(a){
    if(a.status==='expired') return '<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--rglow);color:var(--rd1)">Expired</span>';
    if(a.status==='critical') return '<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--rglow);color:var(--rd1)">Critical</span>';
    if(a.status==='warning') return '<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--aglow);color:var(--am1)">Warning</span>';
    return '<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--bglow);color:var(--b1)">Soon</span>';
  };
  tbody.innerHTML=alerts.map(function(a){
    const daysColor=a.daysToExpiry<0?'var(--rd1)':a.daysToExpiry<=7?'var(--rd1)':a.daysToExpiry<=14?'var(--am1)':'var(--b1)';
    const daysLabel=a.daysToExpiry<0?Math.abs(a.daysToExpiry)+'d ago':a.daysToExpiry+'d left';
    return '<tr>'+
      '<td style="padding:12px 14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">'+a.name+'</td>'+
      '<td style="padding:12px 14px;color:var(--t2);font-size:12px">'+a.store+'</td>'+
      '<td style="padding:12px 14px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700">'+a.stock+'</td>'+
      '<td style="padding:12px 14px;color:var(--t2);font-size:12px;font-family:var(--f-m)">'+a.expiryDate+'</td>'+
      '<td style="padding:12px 14px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:'+daysColor+'">'+daysLabel+'</td>'+
      '<td style="padding:12px 14px;text-align:center">'+statusBadge(a)+'</td>'+
      '<td style="padding:12px 14px;text-align:center">'+
        '<button onclick="handleExpiryProduct(\''+a.productID+'\',\''+a.store+'\',\''+a.name+'\')" '+
        'style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:8px;border:1.5px solid var(--gn1);background:var(--gglow);color:var(--gn1);cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center" title="Mark as Handled">'+
        '<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:16px">check</span></button>'+
      '</td>'+
      '</tr>';
  }).join('');
}

function handleExpiryProduct(productID, store, name){
  showModal('Mark as Handled',`
    <div style="text-align:center;padding:10px 0">
      <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:40px;color:var(--gn1);display:block;margin-bottom:12px">check_circle</span>
      <p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--t2);margin:0">Mark <strong>${esc(name)}</strong> (${esc(store)}) as handled?</p>
      <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:8px">This confirms you have checked and cleared the expired/expiring stock from the shelf. The product will be removed from the expiry tracker until the next stock-in with a new expiry date.</p>
    </div>
  `,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:15px">check_circle</span> Mark as Handled',c:'btn-ok',fn:()=>{
      closeModal();
      showLoading('Updating…');
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
          hideLoading();
          if(r&&r.success){
            toast(name+' marked as handled','ok');
            window._expiryAlerts=window._expiryAlerts.filter(a=>!(a.productID===productID&&a.store===store));
            setExpiryFilter(window._expiryFilter||'all', $('ef'+(window._expiryFilter||'All').charAt(0).toUpperCase()+(window._expiryFilter||'all').slice(1)));
            renderDashExpiry();
          } else {
            toast('Could not update — try again','er');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
        .clearProductExpiry({productID,store,_token:_sessionToken});
    }}
  ]);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   UPDATE PRICE
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function initPrice(){
  $('pSearch').value=''; $('pSR').classList.remove('on'); $('elPForm').classList.add('hidden'); selElP=null;
  $('elPCard').classList.remove('hidden');
}
function selElPrice(p){
  selElP=p; $('pSearch').value=p.name;
  $('curCost').innerText='₦'+(p.cost||0).toLocaleString();
  $('curExp').innerText='₦'+(p.expenseOnCost||0).toLocaleString();
  $('curPrice').innerText='₦'+p.price.toLocaleString();
  $('nCost').value=p.cost||0; $('nExp').value=p.expenseOnCost||0; $('nPrice').value=p.price||0;
  $('elPForm').classList.remove('hidden');
}
function doElPrice(){
  if(!selElP) return toast('Select a product first','er');
  const nc=+$('nCost').value, ne=+$('nExp').value, np=+$('nPrice').value;
  if(np<=0) return toast('Price must be greater than 0','er');
  showLoading('Updating price…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
      hideLoading(); toast(r.msg,'ok');
      const i=products.findIndex(p=>p.id===selElP.id);
      if(i!==-1){ products[i].cost=nc; products[i].expenseOnCost=ne; products[i].price=np; }
      $('elPForm').classList.add('hidden'); $('pSearch').value=''; selElP=null;
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .updateProductPrice({productID:selElP.id,store:user.store,cost:nc,expenseOnCost:ne,listPrice:np,_token:_sessionToken});
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   NEW PRODUCT
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
function openNewProd(){ openOV('newProdOV'); }
function doNewProd(){
  const name=$('npName').value.trim(), price=+$('npPrice').value, cat=$('npCat').value.trim();
  const sub=$('npSub').value.trim(), stock=+($('npStock').value||0), cost=+($('npCost').value||0), exp=+($('npExp').value||0);
  const reorder=+($('npReorder').value||5);
  if(!name||!price||!cat) return toast('Fill name, price and category','er');
  const _npExp=$('npExpiry').value||null;
  const _npAlrt=[60,30,14,7].filter(v=>$('npAlert'+v)&&$('npAlert'+v).checked).join(',')||null;
  closeOV('newProdOV');
  showLoading('Creating product…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
      hideLoading(); toast(r.msg,'ok');
      $('npExpiry').value='';
      $('npAlertWrap').classList.add('hidden');
      [60,30,14,7].forEach(v=>{const el=$('npAlert'+v);if(el)el.checked=(v===14);});
      loadProds();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .sellNewProduct({productName:name,unitSoldPrice:price,category:cat,subCategory:sub,initialStock:stock,qty:0,cost,expenseOnCost:exp,reorderLevel:reorder,store:user.store,soldBy:user.name,expiryDate:_npExp,alertDays:_npAlrt,_token:_sessionToken});
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   
/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   BARCODE GENERATOR
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
let _bcSelected=[];
let _bcScript=false;
let _bcAllLabels=[];
let _actPage=1;
const _actPerPage=20;
let _retAppPage=1, _retAllPage=1;
const _retPerPage=15;

function openBcSettings(){
  openOV('bcSettingsOV');
}

let _bcPage=1;
const _bcPerPage=24;

function bcChangePage(dir){
  const total=_bcAllLabels?_bcAllLabels.length:0;
  const maxPage=Math.ceil(total/_bcPerPage);
  _bcPage=Math.max(1,Math.min(_bcPage+dir,maxPage));
  renderBcPage();
}

function renderBcPage(){
  const total=_bcAllLabels?_bcAllLabels.length:0;
  const maxPage=Math.ceil(total/_bcPerPage)||1;
  const start=(_bcPage-1)*_bcPerPage;
  const end=start+_bcPerPage;
  const pageLabels=_bcAllLabels.slice(start,end);

  const grid=$('bcGrid');
  grid.innerHTML='';
  pageLabels.forEach(el=>grid.appendChild(el));

  // Pagination controls
  $('bcPageInfo').innerText=`Page ${_bcPage} of ${maxPage}`;
  $('bcPageInfo').style.display=total>_bcPerPage?'inline':'none';
  $('bcPrevBtn').style.display=total>_bcPerPage?'inline-flex':'none';
  $('bcNextBtn').style.display=total>_bcPerPage?'inline-flex':'none';
  $('bcPrevBtn').disabled=_bcPage===1;
  $('bcNextBtn').disabled=_bcPage===maxPage;
}

function initBarcode(){
  _bcSelected=[];
  renderBcSelected();
  $('bcGrid').innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:60px 20px;color:var(--t3);background:var(--s0);border-radius:var(--r2)"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:48px;opacity:.15">qr_code_2</span><p style="font-size:13px;margin-top:12px;font-weight:700">No barcodes yet</p><p style="font-size:12px;margin-top:4px">Add products and click Generate</p></div>';
  $('bcLabelCount').innerText='0 labels';
  buildBcCatBtns();
  if(!_bcScript){
    const s=document.createElement('script');
    s.src='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/jsbarcode/3.11.6/JsBarcode.all.min.js';
    s.onload=()=>{ _bcScript=true; };
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }
}

function buildBcCatBtns(){
  const box=$('bcCatBtns');
  if(!box||!products.length) return;
  const cats=[...new Set(products.map(p=>p.category).filter(Boolean))];
  box.innerHTML='';
  cats.slice(0,10).forEach(cat=>{
    const btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.className='btn btn-s btn-sm';
    btn.style.cssText='font-size:10px;padding:4px 9px;border-radius:20px';
    btn.innerText=cat;
    btn.onclick=()=>{
      const catProds=products.filter(p=>p.category===cat);
      let added=0;
      catProds.forEach(p=>{
        if(!_bcSelected.find(x=>x.id===p.id)){
          _bcSelected.push({id:String(p.id),name:p.name,price:p.price,qty:1});
          added++;
        }
      });
      renderBcSelected();
      toast(added+' '+cat+' products added','ok');
    };
    box.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

function bcSearchProd(){
  const q=$('bcSearch').value.toLowerCase().trim(), box=$('bcSR');
  if(!q){ box.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  const m=products.filter(p=>p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)||String(p.id).toLowerCase().includes(q)).slice(0,12);
  box.innerHTML='';
  if(!m.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="sri" style="color:var(--t3)">No results</div>'; }
  m.forEach(p=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='sri';
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="sri-n">${esc(p.name)}</div><div class="sri-m">${esc(p.id)} · ₦${Number(p.price).toLocaleString()} · Stock: ${p.stock??'—'}</div>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{ bcAddProduct(p); box.classList.remove('on'); $('bcSearch').value=''; };
    box.appendChild(d);
  });
  box.classList.add('on');
}

function bcAddProduct(p){
  if(_bcSelected.find(x=>x.id===p.id)) return toast(p.name+' already added','in');
  _bcSelected.push({id:String(p.id),name:p.name,price:p.price,qty:1});
  renderBcSelected();
  toast(p.name+' added','ok');
}

function bcAddManual(){
  const name=$('bcManName').value.trim();
  const price=+($('bcManPrice').value||0);
  const id=$('bcManId').value.trim()||('M-'+Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase().slice(-5));
  if(!name) return toast('Enter product name','er');
  _bcSelected.push({id,name,price,qty:1});
  $('bcManName').value=''; $('bcManPrice').value=''; $('bcManId').value='';
  renderBcSelected();
  toast('Manual label added','ok');
}

function bcRemove(id){
  _bcSelected=_bcSelected.filter(x=>x.id!==id);
  renderBcSelected();
}

function bcUpdateQty(id,val){
  const p=_bcSelected.find(x=>x.id===id);
  if(p) p.qty=Math.max(1,+val||1);
}

function bcSetBulkQty(){
  const qty=+($('bcBulkQty').value||1);
  if(!qty||qty<1) return toast('Enter a valid quantity','er');
  _bcSelected.forEach(p=>p.qty=qty);
  renderBcSelected();
  toast('All quantities set to '+qty,'ok');
}

function renderBcSelected(){
  const list=$('bcSelList');
  if(!_bcSelected.length){
    list.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:16px;color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">No products added yet</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML='';
  _bcSelected.forEach(p=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.style.cssText='background:var(--s1);border:1px solid var(--bdr);border-radius:var(--r2);padding:8px 10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px';
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
        <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${esc(p.name)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3);font-family:var(--f-m)">${p.id} · ₦${Number(p.price).toLocaleString()}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;flex-shrink:0">
        <label style="font-size:9px;color:var(--t3);font-weight:800">QTY</label>
        <input type="number" value="${p.qty}" min="1" max="999"
          style="width:48px;padding:4px 6px;border:1px solid var(--bdr2);border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;font-family:var(--f-m);background:var(--s0);color:var(--t1);text-align:center"
          onchange="bcUpdateQty('${p.id}',this.value)">
        <button onclick="bcRemove('${p.id}')" style="color:var(--t4);background:none;border:none;cursor:pointer;font-size:15px;padding:2px;border-radius:4px;display:flex;align-items:center" onmouseover="this.style.color='var(--rd1)'" onmouseout="this.style.color='var(--t4)'">✕</button>
      </div>
    `;
    list.appendChild(el);
  });
}

function generateBarcodes(){
  if(!_bcSelected.length) return toast('Add products first','er');
  if(!_bcScript) return toast('Barcode engine loading — try again in a moment','wa');

  const grid=$('bcGrid');
  const showName=$('bcShowName').checked;
  const showPrice=$('bcShowPrice').checked;
  const showId=$('bcShowId').checked;
  const showStore=$('bcShowStore').checked;
  const showCutLines=$('bcShowCutLines').checked;
  const customText=$('bcCustomText').value.trim();
  const size=$('bcSize').value;
  const format=$('bcFormat').value;
  const colsOverride=$('bcCols').value;

  const colsMap={small:'repeat(4,1fr)',medium:'repeat(3,1fr)',large:'repeat(2,1fr)',sheet:'repeat(5,1fr)'};
  const heightMap={small:38,medium:55,large:80,sheet:32};
  const h=heightMap[size]||55;

  let cols=colsMap[size]||'repeat(3,1fr)';
  if(colsOverride!=='auto') cols='repeat('+colsOverride+',1fr)';

  grid.style.gridTemplateColumns=cols;
  grid.style.gap=showCutLines?'0':'8px';
  grid.style.background=showCutLines?'var(--s2)':'transparent';
  grid.style.padding=showCutLines?'2px':'0';
  grid.innerHTML='';

  let total=0;
  _bcSelected.forEach(p=>{
    for(let i=0;i<p.qty;i++){
      const wrap=document.createElement('div');
      wrap.className='bc-label';
      wrap.style.cssText=`background:#fff;${showCutLines?'border:1px dashed #c8d3e8;':'border:1px solid #e8eef8;border-radius:8px;'}padding:6px;text-align:center;position:relative;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;overflow:hidden`;
      if(!showCutLines) wrap.style.borderRadius='8px';

      const svg=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','svg');
svg.style.cssText='width:100%;height:auto;display:block;max-width:100%';
      const bcVal=String(p.id).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9\-\.\ \$\/\+\%]/g,'').slice(0,20)||'NOCODE';
      try{
        JsBarcode(svg,bcVal,{format,width:1.8,height:h,displayValue:showId,margin:2,background:'#ffffff',lineColor:'#0a1628',fontSize:8,fontOptions:'bold'});
      }catch(e){
        try{ JsBarcode(svg,bcVal,{format:'CODE128',width:1.8,height:h,displayValue:showId,margin:2,background:'#ffffff',lineColor:'#0a1628',fontSize:8}); }
        catch(e2){ svg.innerHTML='<text x="5" y="20" style="font-size:9px;fill:red">Invalid</text>'; }
      }
      wrap.appendChild(svg);

      if(showName){ const n=document.createElement('div'); n.style.cssText='font-family:var(--f-d);font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;color:#0a1628;margin-top:4px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;line-height:1.3'; n.innerText=p.name; wrap.appendChild(n); }
      if(showPrice&&p.price){ const pr=document.createElement('div'); pr.style.cssText='font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;color:#2254e0;margin-top:2px'; pr.innerText='₦'+Number(p.price).toLocaleString(); wrap.appendChild(pr); }
      if(showStore&&user&&user.store){ const st=document.createElement('div'); st.style.cssText='font-size:8px;color:#6b84af;margin-top:1px'; st.innerText=user.store; wrap.appendChild(st); }
      if(customText){ const ct=document.createElement('div'); ct.style.cssText='font-size:8px;color:#a8bcda;margin-top:1px'; ct.innerText=customText; wrap.appendChild(ct); }

      grid.appendChild(wrap);
      total++;
    }
  });

  $('bcLabelCount').innerText=total+' label'+(total!==1?'s':'')+' generated';
  toast(total+' label'+(total!==1?'s':'')+' generated','ok');
}

function printBarcodes(){
  if(!$('bcGrid').children.length||$('bcGrid').querySelector('[style*="padding:60px"]'))
    return toast('Generate barcodes first','er');

  const size=$('bcSize').value;
  const colsOverride=$('bcCols').value;
  const colsMap={small:'repeat(4,1fr)',medium:'repeat(3,1fr)',large:'repeat(2,1fr)',sheet:'repeat(5,1fr)'};
  let cols=colsMap[size]||'repeat(3,1fr)';
  if(colsOverride!=='auto') cols='repeat('+colsOverride+',1fr)';
  const showCutLines=$('bcShowCutLines').checked;

  const pw=window.open('','_blank','width=950,height=750');
  if(!pw){ toast('Allow popups to print','wa'); return; }
  pw.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8"><title>StarTrack Labels</title>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Outfit:wght@400;700;800;900&family=Fira+Code:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
      *{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}
      body{font-family:Outfit,sans-serif;background:#fff;padding:12px}
      .nop{display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:center;padding:10px;margin-bottom:14px;background:#f8fafd;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e7f0}
      .nop button{padding:8px 18px;border:none;border-radius:7px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;font-family:Outfit,sans-serif}
      .pb{background:#2254e0;color:#fff}.gb{background:#059669;color:#fff}.cb{background:#6b7280;color:#fff}
      .grid{display:grid;width:100%;grid-template-columns:${cols};gap:${showCutLines?'0':'6px'};background:${showCutLines?'#e0e7f0':'#fff'};padding:${showCutLines?'1px':'0'}}
.bc-label{background:#fff;${showCutLines?'border:1px dashed #c8d3e8;':'border:1px solid #e8eef8;border-radius:7px;'}padding:8px 6px 6px;text-align:center;page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid;overflow:hidden;width:100%;min-width:0}
.bc-label svg{width:100%!important;height:auto!important;max-width:100%!important;display:block;overflow:hidden}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
.bc-name{font-family:Outfit,sans-serif;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;color:#0a1628;margin-top:4px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.bc-price{font-family:'Fira Code',monospace;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;color:#2254e0;margin-top:2px}
.bc-sub{font-size:8px;color:#a8bcda;margin-top:1px}
@media print{.nop{display:none!important}@page{size:A4;margin:6mm}body{padding:0}.grid{display:grid!important;width:100%!important;grid-template-columns:${cols}!important}.bc-label{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid}}
    </style>
  </head><body>
    <div class="nop">
      <button class="pb" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ Print</button>
      <button class="gb" onclick="window.print()">💾 Save as PDF</button>
      <button class="cb" onclick="window.close()">✕ Close</button>
    </div>
    <div class="grid">${Array.from($('bcGrid').children).map(el=>'<div class="bc-label">'+el.outerHTML+'</div>').join('')}</div>
    <script>window.onload=()=>setTimeout(()=>window.print(),800);<\/script>
  </body></html>`);
  pw.document.close();
}

function bcClearAll(){
  _bcSelected=[];
  renderBcSelected();
  $('bcGrid').innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:60px 20px;color:var(--t3);background:var(--s0);border-radius:var(--r2)"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:48px;opacity:.15">qr_code_2</span><p style="font-size:13px;margin-top:12px;font-weight:700">No barcodes yet</p><p style="font-size:12px;margin-top:4px">Add products and click Generate</p></div>';
  $('bcLabelCount').innerText='0 labels';
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  LOGOUT
───────────────────────────────────────────── */

function doLogout(){
  const msg=_clockIn
    ?'<p style="color:var(--t2);font-size:14px">Sign out of Tado Foods POS?</p><div style="margin-top:10px;padding:10px 12px;background:var(--aglow);border-left:3px solid var(--am1);border-radius:var(--r4);font-size:12px;color:var(--am);font-weight:700">⚠️ You are still clocked in — your session will remain open until you clock out.</div>'
    :'<p style="color:var(--t2);font-size:14px">Sign out of Tado Foods POS?</p>';
  showModal('Sign Out',msg,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'Sign Out',c:'btn-d',fn:()=>{
      closeModal();
      if(_sessionToken){ google.script.run.invalidateSession(_sessionToken); _sessionToken=null; }
      // Stop timer display but DO NOT clock out
      if(_clockTimer){ clearInterval(_clockTimer); _clockTimer=null; }
      _clockIn=null;
      if(_notifTimer){ clearInterval(_notifTimer); _notifTimer=null; }
      _notifs=[];
      user={}; products=[]; wbProds=[]; cart=[]; myTxns=[];
      sessionStorage.clear();
      $('appShell').classList.add('hidden');
      $('loginScreen').classList.remove('hidden');
      $('lgCode').value=''; $('sbAv').innerText='?';
      ['nav-dashboard','nav-updatePrice','nav-records'].forEach(id=>$(id).classList.add('hidden'));
    }}
  ]);
}
/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   CLOCK IN / OUT
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
let _clockIn=null, _clockTimer=null;

function doClockIn(){
  if(_clockIn) return toast('Already clocked in','wa');
  _clockIn=new Date();
  localStorage.setItem('clockIn_'+user.name, _clockIn.toISOString());
  $('clockStatus').innerText='Clocked In';
  $('clockStatus').style.color='var(--gn1)';
  $('clockInfo').innerText='Since '+_clockIn.toLocaleTimeString('en-NG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  $('clockInBtn').classList.add('hidden');
  $('clockOutBtn').classList.remove('hidden');
  // Start live timer
  _clockTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    const diff=Date.now()-_clockIn.getTime();
    const h=Math.floor(diff/3600000);
    const m=Math.floor((diff%3600000)/60000);
    const s=Math.floor((diff%60000)/1000);
    $('clockTimer').innerText=(h?h+'h ':'')+m+'m '+s+'s';
  },1000);
  // Save to backend
  google.script.run
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast('Clock in could not be saved — no connection','wa'))
    .recordClockIn({name:user.name,store:user.store,time:_clockIn.toISOString(),_token:_sessionToken});
  toast('Clocked in at '+_clockIn.toLocaleTimeString('en-NG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),'ok');
}

function cfmClockOut(){
  if(!_clockIn) return;
  const diff=Date.now()-_clockIn.getTime();
  const h=Math.floor(diff/3600000);
  const m=Math.floor((diff%3600000)/60000);
  showModal('Clock Out',`
    <div style="text-align:center;padding:10px 0">
      <div style="font-family:var(--f-d);font-size:28px;font-weight:900;color:var(--b1)">${h}h ${m}m</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:4px">Time worked today</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t2);margin-top:12px">Clock out of <strong>${user.store}</strong>?</div>
    </div>
  `,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:15px">logout</span> Clock Out',c:'btn-d',fn:()=>{
      closeModal(); doClockOut();
    }}
  ]);
}

function doClockOut(){
  const out=new Date();
  const diff=out.getTime()-_clockIn.getTime();
  const h=Math.floor(diff/3600000);
  const m=Math.floor((diff%3600000)/60000);
  clearInterval(_clockTimer);
  $('clockStatus').innerText='Clocked Out';
  $('clockStatus').style.color='var(--rd1)';
  $('clockInfo').innerText='Worked '+h+'h '+m+'m · Out at '+out.toLocaleTimeString('en-NG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  $('clockTimer').innerText=h+'h '+m+'m';
  $('clockOutBtn').classList.add('hidden');
  $('clockInBtn').classList.remove('hidden');
  // Save to backend
  google.script.run
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast('Clock out could not be saved — no connection','wa'))
    .recordClockOut({name:user.name,store:user.store,clockIn:_clockIn.toISOString(),clockOut:out.toISOString(),hoursWorked:parseFloat((diff/3600000).toFixed(2)),_token:_sessionToken});
  toast('Clocked out · '+h+'h '+m+'m worked','ok');
  _clockIn=null;
