      $('clockStatus').innerText='Not Clocked In';
      $('clockStatus').style.color='';
      $('clockInfo').innerText='—';
      $('clockTimer').innerText='—';
      $('clockInBtn').classList.remove('hidden');
      $('clockOutBtn').classList.add('hidden');
  localStorage.removeItem('clockIn_'+user.name);
}
/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   OFFLINE MODE
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
let _isOnline=navigator.onLine;

window.addEventListener('online',()=>{
  // Wait 2 seconds to confirm connection is stable before syncing
  setTimeout(()=>{
    if(!navigator.onLine) return;
    _isOnline=true;
    $('offlineBanner').classList.add('hidden');
    toast('Back online · Syncing pending sales…','ok');
    syncPending();
  },2000);
});
window.addEventListener('offline',()=>{
  _isOnline=false;
  $('offlineBanner').classList.remove('hidden');
  toast('You are offline · Sales will be saved locally','wa');
});

// Check on load
if(!navigator.onLine){
  _isOnline=false;
  $('offlineBanner')?.classList.remove('hidden');
}

function savePendingSale(payload){
  const pending=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pendingSales')||'[]');
  payload._localId='PS-'+Date.now();
  payload._savedAt=new Date().toISOString();
  pending.push(payload);
  localStorage.setItem('pendingSales',JSON.stringify(pending));
  const count=pending.length;
  $('offlineMsg').innerText=count+' sale'+(count>1?'s':'')+' pending sync — connect to internet to sync';
  toast('Sale saved offline · Will sync when online','wa');
}

function syncPending(){
  const pending=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pendingSales')||'[]');
  if(!pending.length){ toast('No pending sales to sync','in'); return; }
  if(!navigator.onLine){ toast('Still offline · Cannot sync yet','er'); return; }
  
  toast('Syncing '+pending.length+' pending sale(s)…','in');
  let done=0, errors=0;
  const syncedIds = []; // Track which IDs successfully synced
  
  pending.forEach(payload=>{
    payload._token=_sessionToken; // attach the CURRENT session at sync time
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler((r)=>{
        // Only count as synced if the backend actually recorded it (success returns a saleID).
        // An unauthorized or business-rule failure comes back through the success handler too,
        // so those must stay in the queue rather than be silently dropped.
        if(r && r.saleID){
          done++;
          syncedIds.push(payload._localId);
        } else {
          errors++;
          console.error('Sync rejected for', payload._localId, r);
        }
        if(done+errors===pending.length){
          const remaining=pending.filter(p=>!syncedIds.includes(p._localId));
          localStorage.setItem('pendingSales',JSON.stringify(remaining));
          if(errors) toast(done+' synced, '+errors+' failed · will retry when possible','wa');
          else toast(done+' sale(s) synced successfully','ok');
          if(!remaining.length) $('offlineBanner').classList.add('hidden');
          loadProds();
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler((e)=>{
        errors++;
        console.error('Sync failed for', payload._localId, e);
        if(done+errors===pending.length){
          // Keep failed ones in pending for retry
          const remaining=pending.filter(p=>!syncedIds.includes(p._localId));
          localStorage.setItem('pendingSales',JSON.stringify(remaining));
          toast(done+' synced, '+errors+' failed · Retrying later','wa');
        }
      })
      .recordSale(payload);
  });
}

function openSettings(){
  $('pinCur').value=''; $('pinNew').value=''; $('pinCfm').value='';
  $('pinBtn').disabled=false;
  $('pinBtn').innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round">lock_reset</span>Change PIN';
  const dark=document.body.classList.contains('dark');
  $('thIco').innerText=dark?'light_mode':'dark_mode';
  $('thLbl').innerText=dark?'Disable':'Enable';
  openOV('settingsOV');
  // Show admin section only for admin
  const sec=$('adminSettingsSection');
  if(sec){
    if(user&&(user.role==='admin'||user.role==='manager')){
      sec.classList.remove('hidden');
      loadHubSettings();
    } else {
      sec.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }
}

function openPINForm(){
  $('pinCur').value=''; $('pinNew').value=''; $('pinCfm').value='';
  $('pinBtn').disabled=false;
  $('pinBtn').innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round">lock_reset</span>Change PIN';
  closeOV('settingsOV');
  openOV('pinOV');
}

function doChangePIN(){
  const cur=$('pinCur').value.trim();
  const nw=$('pinNew').value.trim();
  const cfm=$('pinCfm').value.trim();
  if(!cur||!nw||!cfm) return toast('Fill all fields','er');
  if(nw!==cfm) return toast('New PINs do not match','er');
  if(nw.length<4) return toast('PIN must be at least 4 characters','er');
  if(nw===cur) return toast('New PIN must be different from current','er');
  const btn=$('pinBtn');
  btn.disabled=true;
  btn.innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:15px">sync</span> Saving…';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
      btn.disabled=false;
      btn.innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round">lock_reset</span>Change PIN';
      if(r.success){
        closeOV('pinOV');
        openOV('settingsOV');
        toast('PIN changed successfully','ok');
      } else {
        toast(r.msg||'Incorrect current PIN','er');
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{
      btn.disabled=false;
      btn.innerHTML='<span class="material-icons-round">lock_reset</span>Change PIN';
      toast('Error: '+e.message,'er');
    })
    .changePIN({name:user.name,currentPIN:cur,newPIN:nw,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function showLoading(msg){
  const ov=$('loadingOV');
  $('loadingMsg').innerText=msg||'Processing…';
  ov.style.display='flex';
  // Auto-hide after 30 seconds to prevent stuck loading
  if(window._loadingTimeout) clearTimeout(window._loadingTimeout);
  window._loadingTimeout=setTimeout(()=>{
    hideLoading();
    toast('This is taking longer than expected — check your connection','wa');
  },30000);
}

function hideLoading(){
  $('loadingOV').style.display='none';
  if(window._loadingTimeout) clearTimeout(window._loadingTimeout);
}


function initAttendancePage(){
  const can=p=>user&&user.permissions&&user.permissions.includes(p);
  const canManage=can('staff_mgmt');
  const canAdmin=user&&user.role==='admin';

  // Show/hide tabs based on permissions
  if(canManage||canAdmin){
    const staffTab=$('attTab-staff');
    if(staffTab) staffTab.classList.remove('hidden');
    const histTab=$('attTab-history');
    if(histTab) histTab.classList.remove('hidden');
    const addBtn=$('attAddStaffBtn');
    if(addBtn) addBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
  }
  if(canAdmin){
    // Shifts tab visibility controlled by settings
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(settings=>{
        const shiftsEnabled=String(settings.shifts_enabled).toLowerCase()==='true';
        const shiftsTab=$('attTab-shifts');
        if(shiftsTab){
          shiftsEnabled?shiftsTab.classList.remove('hidden'):shiftsTab.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      })
      .getHubSettings(_sessionToken);
  }

  $('attSub').innerText='Today — '+new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long'});
  switchAttTab('overview');
}

function switchAttTab(tab){
  ['overview','staff','shifts','history','hubsettings'].forEach(t=>{
    const ap=$('attPane-'+t); if(ap) ap.classList.add('hidden');
    const at=$('attTab-'+t); if(at) at.classList.remove('on');
  });
  const ap2=$('attPane-'+tab); if(ap2) ap2.classList.remove('hidden');
  const at2=$('attTab-'+tab); if(at2) at2.classList.add('on');
  if(tab==='overview') loadAttOverview();
  if(tab==='staff') loadStaffTab();
  if(tab==='shifts') loadShiftsTab();
  if(tab==='hubsettings') loadHubSettings();
  if(tab==='history'){
    const iso=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    if(!$('attHistFrom').value) $('attHistFrom').value=iso;
    if(!$('attHistTo').value) $('attHistTo').value=iso;
    loadAttHistory();
  }
}

function loadAttOverview(){
  $('attTodayList').innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3">sync</span></div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(renderAttOverview)
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('attTodayList').innerHTML=`<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--rd1)">${e.message}</div>`; })
    .getAttendanceOverview(_sessionToken);
}

function renderAttOverview(d){
  $('attCntPresent').innerText=d.present||0;
  $('attCntLate').innerText=d.late||0;
  $('attCntAbsent').innerText=d.absent||0;
  $('attCntTotal').innerText=d.total||0;

  if(!d.records||!d.records.length){
    $('attTodayList').innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)">No attendance records for today</div>';
    return;
  }

  $('attTodayList').innerHTML=`
    <table class="staff-table">
      <thead><tr><th>Staff</th><th>Store</th><th>Clock In</th><th>Clock Out</th><th>Hours</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>
        ${d.records.map(r=>`
          <tr>
            <td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
              <div class="staff-av" style="background:${r.status==='Present'?'var(--gn1)':r.status==='Late'?'var(--am1)':'var(--rd1)'}">
                ${r.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
              </div>
              <div>
                <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:var(--t1)">${r.name}</div>
                <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3)">${r.role}</div>
              </div>
            </div></td>
            <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t2)">${r.store}</td>
            <td style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px">${r.clockIn||'—'}</td>
            <td style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px">${r.clockOut||'<span style="color:var(--gn1);font-size:11px">Still in</span>'}</td>
            <td style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700">${r.hours||'—'}</td>
            <td><span class="bdg ${r.status==='Present'?'bdg-ok':r.status==='Late'?'bdg-am':'bdg-r'}">${r.status}</span></td>
          </tr>
        `).join('')}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  `;
}

let _allStaffData=[];
function loadStaffTab(){
  $('staffTableBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.2">sync</span></td></tr>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(d=>{
      _allStaffData=d;
      renderStaffTable(d);
      // populate history dropdown
      const sel=$('attHistStaff');
      sel.innerHTML='<option value="">All Staff</option>';
      d.forEach(s=>{ const o=new Option(s.name,s.id); sel.add(o); });
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('staffTableBody').innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--rd1)">${e.message}</td></tr>`; })
    .getAllStaff(_sessionToken);
}

function renderStaffTable(data){
  const permLabels={
    stock:'Stock In',expenses:'Expenses',returns:'Returns',
    barcode:'Barcodes',activity:'Activity',prices:'Update Price',
    attendance_view:'Attendance View',approve_returns:'Approve Returns',customers:'Customer Tracker'
  };
  const roleColors={admin:'bdg-r',manager:'bdg-am',staff:'bdg-b'};

  $('staffTableBody').innerHTML=data.map(s=>`
    <tr>
      <td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
        <div class="staff-av" style="background:var(--b1);font-size:13px">${esc(s.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase())}</div>
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:var(--t1)">${esc(s.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3)">${esc(s.store)}</div>
        </div>
      </div></td>
      <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t2)">${s.store}</td>
      <td>
        <select class="fc fc-m" style="width:110px;font-size:12px" onchange="changeStaffRole('${s.id}',this.value)">
          <option value="Staff" ${s.role==='staff'?'selected':''}>Staff</option>
          <option value="Manager" ${s.role==='manager'?'selected':''}>Manager</option>
          <option value="Admin" ${s.role==='admin'?'selected':''}>Admin</option>
        </select>
      </td>
      <td>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;max-width:280px">
          ${(s.extraPerms||[]).map(p=>`
            <span class="perm-chip active">${permLabels[p]||p}
              <button onclick="removeStaffPerm('${s.id}','${p}')">×</button>
            </span>
          `).join('')}
          <button class="perm-chip" onclick="openAssignPerm('${s.id}')">
            <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:12px">add</span> Assign
          </button>
        </div>
      </td>
      <td><span class="bdg ${s.status==='Active'?'bdg-ok':'bdg-r'}">${s.status}</span></td>
      <td>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="toggleStaffStatus('${s.id}','${s.status}')">
            ${s.status==='Active'?'Deactivate':'Activate'}
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-p btn-sm" onclick="openResetPIN('${s.id}')">
            <span class="material-icons-round">lock_reset</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
}

function filterStaffList(){
  const q=($('staffSearch').value||'').toLowerCase();
  const role=($('staffRoleFilter').value||'').toLowerCase();
  const filtered=_allStaffData.filter(s=>{
    const matchQ=!q||s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)||s.store.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    const matchR=!role||s.role===role;
    return matchQ&&matchR;
  });
  renderStaffTable(filtered);
}

function changeStaffRole(id,role){
  const _sn=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  openOV('genOV');
  $('gmTit').innerText='Change Role';
  $('gmBd').innerHTML=`<p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--t2)">Change <strong>${esc(_sn)}</strong>'s role to <strong>${esc(role)}</strong>?</p>`;
  $('gmFt').innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-s" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
    <button class="btn btn-p" onclick="confirmChangeRole('${id}','${esc(role)}')">
      <span class="material-icons-round">check</span>Confirm
    </button>
  `;
}

function confirmChangeRole(id,role){
  const _sn=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  closeModal();
  showLoading('Updating role…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast(`${_sn} is now ${role}`,'ok'); loadStaffTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .setStaffRole({rowId:id,role,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function openAssignPerm(id){
  const name=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  const permOptions={
    stock:'Stock In',expenses:'Expenses',returns:'Returns',
    barcode:'Barcodes',activity:'Activity',prices:'Update Price',
    attendance_view:'Attendance View',approve_returns:'Approve Returns',
    customers:'Customer Tracker'
  };
  openOV('genOV');
  $('gmTit').innerText='Assign Permission — '+name;
  $('gmBd').innerHTML=`
    <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t3);margin-bottom:16px">Select a permission to temporarily assign to this staff member. They will see it on next login.</p>
    <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
      ${Object.entries(permOptions).map(([k,v])=>`
        <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:var(--r2);border:1px solid var(--bdr);cursor:pointer;transition:all .15s" 
               onmouseover="this.style.borderColor='var(--b1)'" onmouseout="this.style.borderColor='var(--bdr)'">
          <input type="radio" name="permPick" value="${k}" style="accent-color:var(--b1)">
          <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--t1)">${v}</span>
        </label>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
  `;
  $('gmFt').innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-s" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
    <button class="btn btn-p" onclick="doAssignPerm('${id}')">
      <span class="material-icons-round">check</span>Assign
    </button>
  `;
}

function doAssignPerm(id){
  const name=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  const picked=document.querySelector('input[name="permPick"]:checked');
  if(!picked) return toast('Select a permission','wa');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{
      closeModal();
      toast(`${picked.value} assigned to ${name}`,'ok');
      loadStaffTab();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e.message,'er'))
    .assignStaffPerm({rowId:id,perm:picked.value,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function removeStaffPerm(id,perm){
  const name=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  openOV('genOV');
  $('gmTit').innerText='Remove Permission';
  $('gmBd').innerHTML=`<p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--t2)">Remove <strong>${esc(perm)}</strong> permission from <strong>${esc(name)}</strong>?</p>`;
  $('gmFt').innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-s" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
    <button class="btn btn-d" onclick="closeModal();confirmRemovePerm('${id}','${esc(perm)}')">
      <span class="material-icons-round">remove_circle</span>Remove
    </button>
  `;
}

function confirmRemovePerm(id,perm){
  const name=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  showLoading('Removing permission…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast(`Permission removed from ${name}`,'ok'); loadStaffTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .removeStaffPerm({rowId:id,perm,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function loadShiftsTab(){
  $('shiftList').innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:28px;opacity:.2">sync</span></div>';
  // Load staff first so dropdown is populated, then load shifts
  if(!_allStaffData.length){
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(d=>{
        _allStaffData=d;
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(renderShiftList)
          .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('shiftList').innerHTML=`<div style="color:var(--rd1);padding:20px">${e.message}</div>`; })
          .getShifts(_sessionToken);
      })
      .getAllStaff(_sessionToken);
  } else {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(renderShiftList)
      .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('shiftList').innerHTML=`<div style="color:var(--rd1);padding:20px">${e.message}</div>`; })
      .getShifts(_sessionToken);
  }
}

function renderShiftList(shifts){
  if(!shifts||!shifts.length){
    $('shiftList').innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">No shifts created yet</div>';
    return;
  }
  $('shiftList').innerHTML='';
  shifts.forEach(s=>{
    // Get assigned staff names
    const assignedIds=String(s.staffId||'').split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const assignedNames=assignedIds.map(id=>{
      const st=_allStaffData.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
      return st?st.name:id;
    }).filter(Boolean);

    const card=document.createElement('div');
    card.className='shift-card';
    card.style.flexDirection='column';
    card.style.alignItems='flex-start';
    card.innerHTML=`
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;width:100%">
        <div style="width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:var(--r2);background:var(--bglow);color:var(--b1);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0">
          <span class="material-icons-round">schedule</span>
        </div>
        <div style="flex:1">
          <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:14px;color:var(--t1)">${esc(s.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:3px">${esc(s.start)} — ${esc(s.end)} · Grace: ${s.grace} mins</div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" style="color:var(--rd1)" onclick="deleteShift('${s.id}')">
          <span class="material-icons-round">delete</span>
        </button>
      </div>
      <div style="margin-top:12px;width:100%;border-top:1px solid var(--bdr);padding-top:12px">
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);letter-spacing:.7px;text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:8px">Assigned Staff</div>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px" id="shiftStaff-${s.id}">
          ${assignedNames.length
            ? assignedNames.map((n,i)=>`
                <span class="bdg bdg-b" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px">
                  ${esc(n)}
                  <button onclick="removeStaffFromShift('${s.id}','${assignedIds[i]}')" style="background:none;border:none;cursor:pointer;color:inherit;font-size:13px;padding:0;line-height:1">×</button>
                </span>`)
              .join('')
            : '<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t4)">No staff assigned</span>'
          }
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">
          <select class="fc fc-m" style="font-size:12px;flex:1" id="shiftSel-${s.id}">
            <option value="">— Add staff to shift —</option>
            ${_allStaffData
              .filter(st=>!assignedIds.includes(String(st.id)))
              .map(st=>`<option value="${st.id}">${st.name}</option>`)
              .join('')}
          </select>
          <button class="btn btn-p btn-sm" onclick="addStaffToShift('${s.id}')">
            <span class="material-icons-round">add</span>Add
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    $('shiftList').appendChild(card);
  });
}

function addStaffToShift(shiftId){
  const sel=$('shiftSel-'+shiftId);
  if(!sel||!sel.value) return toast('Select a staff member first','wa');
  showLoading('Assigning…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Staff assigned','ok'); loadShiftsTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .assignStaffToShift({shiftId,staffId:sel.value,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function removeStaffFromShift(shiftId, staffId){
  showLoading('Removing…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Staff removed','ok'); loadShiftsTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .assignStaffToShift({shiftId,staffId,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function doCreateShift(){
  const name=$('shiftName').value.trim();
  const start=$('shiftStart').value;
  const end=$('shiftEnd').value;
  const grace=parseInt($('shiftGrace').value)||5;
  if(!name) return toast('Enter a shift name','wa');
  if(!start||!end) return toast('Set start and end times','wa');
  showLoading('Creating shift…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Shift created','ok'); $('shiftName').value=''; $('shiftStart').value=''; $('shiftEnd').value=''; $('shiftGrace').value=''; loadShiftsTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .createShift({name,start,end,grace,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function assignStaffToShift(shiftId, staffId){
  if(!staffId) return;
  showLoading('Assigning shift…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Shift assigned','ok'); loadShiftsTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .assignStaffToShift(shiftId, staffId);
}

function deleteShift(id){
  if(!confirm('Delete this shift?')) return;
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ toast('Shift deleted','ok'); loadShiftsTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e.message,'er'))
    .deleteShift({rowId:id,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function loadAttHistory(){
  const from=$('attHistFrom').value, to=$('attHistTo').value, staff=$('attHistStaff').value;
  if(!from||!to) return toast('Select a date range','wa');
  $('attHistBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:28px;opacity:.2">sync</span></td></tr>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(renderAttHistory)
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('attHistBody').innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--rd1)">${e.message}</td></tr>`; })
    .getAttendanceHistory({from,to,staffId:staff||null,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function renderAttHistory(rows){
  if(!rows||!rows.length){
    $('attHistBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)">No records found</td></tr>';
    return;
  }
  $('attHistBody').innerHTML=rows.map(r=>`
    <tr class="history-row-${(r.status||'').toLowerCase()}">
      <td><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:var(--t1)">${r.name}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3)">${r.store}</div></td>
      <td style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t2)">${r.date}</td>
      <td style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px">${r.clockIn||'—'}</td>
      <td style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px">${r.clockOut||'—'}</td>
      <td style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:var(--b1)">${r.hours||'—'}</td>
      <td><span class="bdg ${r.status==='Present'?'bdg-ok':r.status==='Late'?'bdg-am':'bdg-r'}">${r.status||'—'}</span></td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
}

function toggleStaffStatus(id,currentStatus){
  const name=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  const newStatus=currentStatus==='Active'?'Inactive':'Active';
  if(!confirm(`${newStatus==='Inactive'?'Deactivate':'Activate'} ${name}?`)) return;
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ toast(`${name} ${newStatus==='Active'?'activated':'deactivated'}`,'ok'); loadStaffTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e.message,'er'))
    .setStaffStatus({rowId:id,status:newStatus,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function openResetPIN(id){
  const name=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  openOV('genOV');
  $('gmTit').innerText='Reset PIN — '+name;
  $('gmBd').innerHTML=`
    <div class="fg"><label class="fl">New PIN</label><input class="fc fc-m" id="rstPin" type="password" placeholder="Enter new PIN" style="letter-spacing:6px"></div>
    <div class="fg"><label class="fl">Confirm PIN</label><input class="fc fc-m" id="rstPin2" type="password" placeholder="Confirm new PIN" style="letter-spacing:6px"></div>
  `;
  $('gmFt').innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-s" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
    <button class="btn btn-p" onclick="doResetPIN('${id}')"><span class="material-icons-round">lock_reset</span>Reset PIN</button>
  `;
}

function doResetPIN(id){
  const name=(_allStaffData.find(x=>x.id===String(id))||{name:'Staff'}).name;
  const p=$('rstPin').value.trim(),p2=$('rstPin2').value.trim();
  if(!p) return toast('Enter a PIN','wa');
  if(p!==p2) return toast('PINs do not match','wa');
  showLoading('Resetting PIN…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); closeModal(); toast(`PIN reset for ${name}`,'ok'); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .adminResetPIN({rowId:id,newPin:p,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function openNewStaff(){
  openOV('genOV');
  $('gmTit').innerText='Add New Staff';
  $('gmBd').innerHTML=`
    <div class="fg"><label class="fl">Full Name</label><input class="fc" id="nsName" placeholder="e.g. Amaka Johnson"></div>
    <div class="fg"><label class="fl">PIN</label><input class="fc fc-m" id="nsPin" type="password" placeholder="Set login PIN" style="letter-spacing:6px"></div>
    <div class="fg"><label class="fl">Role</label>
      <select class="fc" id="nsRole">
        <option value="Staff">Staff</option>
        <option value="Manager">Manager</option>
        <option value="Admin">Admin</option>
      </select>
    </div>
    <div class="fg"><label class="fl">Store</label>
      <select class="fc" id="nsStore">
        ${(user.availableStores||[user.store]).map(s=>`<option value="${s}">${s}</option>`).join('')}
      </select>
    </div>
  `;
  $('gmFt').innerHTML=`
    <button class="btn btn-s" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
    <button class="btn btn-p" onclick="doNewStaff()"><span class="material-icons-round">person_add</span>Add Staff</button>
  `;
}

function doNewStaff(){
  const name=$('nsName').value.trim();
  const pin=$('nsPin').value.trim();
  const role=$('nsRole').value;
  const store=$('nsStore').value;
  if(!name) return toast('Enter a name','wa');
  if(!pin) return toast('Enter a PIN','wa');
  showLoading('Adding staff…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); closeModal(); toast(`${name} added successfully`,'ok'); loadStaffTab(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .createStaff({name,pin,role,store,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function loadHubSettings(){
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(settings=>{
      // Shifts toggle
      const shiftsOn=settings.shifts_enabled==='true'||settings.shifts_enabled===true;
      $('shiftsToggle').checked=shiftsOn;
      $('shiftsToggleTrack').style.background=shiftsOn?'var(--b1)':'var(--bdr2)';
      $('shiftsToggleThumb').style.left=shiftsOn?'23px':'3px';

      // Show/hide shifts tab based on setting
      const shiftsTab=$('attTab-shifts');
      if(shiftsTab){
        if(shiftsOn) shiftsTab.classList.remove('hidden');
        else shiftsTab.classList.add('hidden');
      }

      // Grace period
      if(settings.default_grace) $('defaultGrace').value=settings.default_grace;

      // Notification prefs
      $('notifLowStock').checked=settings.notif_low_stock==='true'||settings.notif_low_stock===true;
      $('notifOutStock').checked=settings.notif_out_stock==='true'||settings.notif_out_stock===true;
      $('notifReturns').checked=settings.notif_returns==='true'||settings.notif_returns===true;
      if($('notifExpiry')) $('notifExpiry').checked=settings.notif_expiry==='true'||settings.notif_expiry===true;

      // VAT
      const vatOn=settings.vat_enabled==='true'||settings.vat_enabled===true;
      $('vatToggle').checked=vatOn;
      $('vatToggleTrack').style.background=vatOn?'var(--b1)':'var(--bdr2)';
      $('vatToggleThumb').style.left=vatOn?'23px':'3px';
      if(settings.vat_rate) $('vatRate').value=settings.vat_rate;
      if(settings.vat_base) $('vatBase').value=settings.vat_base;
      sessionStorage.setItem('hubSettings_'+user.name, JSON.stringify(settings));

      // Emails
      const emails=settings.alert_emails?settings.alert_emails.split(',').map(e=>e.trim()).filter(Boolean):[];
      renderEmailList(emails);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e.message,'er'))
    .getHubSettings(_sessionToken);
}

function renderEmailList(emails){
  const el=$('emailList');
  if(!emails.length){
    el.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);padding:8px 0">No alert emails added yet</div>';
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML=emails.map((e,i)=>`
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px 12px;background:var(--s1);border-radius:var(--r2);border:1px solid var(--bdr)">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
        <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:15px;color:var(--b1)">email</span>
        <span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t1)">${e}</span>
      </div>
      <button onclick="removeAlertEmail(${i})" style="background:none;border:none;cursor:pointer;color:var(--rd1);display:flex;align-items:center">
        <span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:17px">close</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function addAlertEmail(){
  const email=$('newAlertEmail').value.trim();
  if(!email) return toast('Enter an email address','wa');
  if(!email.includes('@')) return toast('Enter a valid email','wa');
  showLoading('Saving email…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); $('newAlertEmail').value=''; toast('Email added','ok'); loadHubSettings(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .addAlertEmail({email,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function removeAlertEmail(idx){
  showLoading('Removing email…');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Email removed','ok'); loadHubSettings(); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .removeAlertEmail({idx,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function saveHubSetting(key,value){
  if(key==='vat_enabled'){
    const on=value===true||value==='true';
    $('vatToggleTrack').style.background=on?'var(--b1)':'var(--bdr2)';
    $('vatToggleThumb').style.left=on?'23px':'3px';
  }
  if(key==='vat_enabled'||key==='vat_rate'||key==='vat_base'){
    // Update localStorage cache immediately so cart reflects change
    try{
      const s=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('hubSettings_'+user.name)||'{}');
      s[key]=String(value);
      sessionStorage.setItem('hubSettings_'+user.name,JSON.stringify(s));
      updTotals();
    }catch(e){}
  }
  if(key==='shifts_enabled'){
    const on=value===true||value==='true';
    const label=on?'enable':'disable';
    closeModal();
    setTimeout(()=>{
    openOV('genOV');
    $('gmTit').innerText=(on?'Enable':'Disable')+' Shifts';
    $('gmBd').innerHTML=`<p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--t2)">Are you sure you want to <strong>${label}</strong> the Shifts feature?</p>`;
    $('gmFt').innerHTML=`
      <button class="btn btn-s" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn btn-p" onclick="closeModal();confirmSaveHubSetting('${key}','${value}')">
        <span class="material-icons-round">check</span>Confirm
      </button>
    `;
    },200);
    return;
  }
  confirmSaveHubSetting(key,value);
}

function confirmSaveHubSetting(key,value){
  showLoading('Saving setting…');
  if(key==='shifts_enabled'){
    const on=value===true||value==='true'||value==='true';
    $('shiftsToggleTrack').style.background=on?'var(--b1)':'var(--bdr2)';
    $('shiftsToggleThumb').style.left=on?'23px':'3px';
    const shiftsTab=$('attTab-shifts');
    if(shiftsTab) on?shiftsTab.classList.remove('hidden'):shiftsTab.classList.add('hidden');
  }
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(()=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Saved','ok'); })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast(e.message,'er'); })
    .saveHubSetting({key,value:String(value),_token:_sessionToken});
}

function initStaffPage(){}
function initSettingsPage(){}
/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   RETURNS
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
let retPayMethod='Cash', retTxnData=null;

function initReturns(){
  $('retDate').value=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  $('retTxnId').value='';
  $('retLookupBox').classList.add('hidden');
  $('retNotFound').classList.add('hidden');
  $('refundBox').classList.add('hidden');
  retTxnData=null;
  if(user.isAdmin) loadPendingReturns();
}


function loadPendingReturns(){
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      const card=$('pendingReturnsCard');
      const list=$('pendingReturnsList');
      const bdg=$('retBdg');
      
      if(!data||!data.length){
        if(card) card.classList.add('hidden');
        if(bdg) bdg.classList.add('hidden');
        updateNotifBadge();
        return;
      }
      // Show card and badge
      if(card) card.classList.remove('hidden');
      if(bdg){ bdg.innerText=data.length; bdg.classList.remove('hidden'); }

      // Add to notifications
      const pendingOnly = data.filter(r=>r.status==='Pending'||!r.status);
      pendingOnly.forEach(r=>{
        const nid='PENDING-'+r.returnId;
        if(_notifs.some(n=>n.id===nid)) return;
        _notifs.unshift({
          id:nid,
          title:'🔄 Pending Return Request',
          msg:r.productName+' × '+r.qty+' · ₦'+Number(r.refundAmount).toLocaleString()+' · By: '+r.returnedBy,
          type:'wa',
          action:()=>goPage('returns'),
          time:new Date(r.date).toISOString(),
          read:false
        });
      });
      saveNotifs();
      updateNotifBadge();
      
      if(!list) return;
      list.innerHTML='';
      window._pendingReturnTxnIds = {};
      pendingOnly.forEach(r=>{
        window._pendingReturnTxnIds[r.returnId] = r.originalTxnId;
        const el=document.createElement('div');
        el.style.cssText='background:var(--s1);border:1px solid var(--bdr);border-radius:var(--r2);padding:13px;margin-bottom:8px';
        el.innerHTML=`
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:8px">
            <div>
              <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(r.productName)}</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:2px">${esc(r.returnId)} · Qty: ${r.qty} · By: ${esc(r.returnedBy)}</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:1px">Reason: ${esc(r.reason)} · Via: ${esc(r.refundMethod)}</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:1px">Txn: ${esc(r.originalTxnId)}</div>
            </div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1);white-space:nowrap">₦${Number(r.refundAmount).toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
            <button class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" style="flex:1" onclick="handleReturn('${r.returnId}','${r.productID}',${r.qty},${r.refundAmount},'approve',${r.isWB||false})">
              <span class="material-icons-round">check_circle</span>Approve
            </button>
            <button class="btn btn-d btn-sm" style="flex:1" onclick="handleReturn('${r.returnId}','${r.productID}',${r.qty},${r.refundAmount},'reject',${r.isWB||false})">
              <span class="material-icons-round">cancel</span>Reject
            </button>
          </div>
        `;
        list.appendChild(el);
      });
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast('Error loading pending returns: '+e.message,'er'))
    .getPendingReturns(_sessionToken);
}

function handleReturn(returnId,productID,qty,refundAmount,action,isWB){
  const label=action==='approve'?'Approve':'Reject';
  const cls=action==='approve'?'btn-ok':'btn-d';
  const msg=action==='approve'
    ?`<p style="color:var(--t2);font-size:14px">Approve this return?<br><strong>Stock will be restored</strong> and refund of <strong>₦${Number(refundAmount).toLocaleString()}</strong> will be recorded.</p>`
    :`<p style="color:var(--t2);font-size:14px">Reject this return request?<br>Stock will <strong>not</strong> be restored.</p>`;
  showModal(label+' Return',msg,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:label,c:cls,fn:()=>{
      closeModal();
      toast(label+'ing return…','in');
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
          toast(r.msg,r.success?'ok':'er');
          loadPendingReturns();
          if(action==='approve') loadProds();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e&&e.message?'Error: '+e.message:'Connection error — please try again','er'))
        .approveReturn({returnId,productID,qty,refundAmount,action,isWB,approvedBy:user.name,originalTxnId:window._pendingReturnTxnIds&&window._pendingReturnTxnIds[returnId]||'',_token:_sessionToken});
    }}
  ]);
}

function setRefundPM(el,m){
  retPayMethod=m;
  document.querySelectorAll('#refundPayRow .pm').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('on'));
  el.classList.add('on');
}

function lookupReturn(){
  const id=$('retTxnId').value.trim();
  if(!id) return toast('Enter a transaction ID','er');
  $('retLookupBox').classList.add('hidden');
  $('retNotFound').classList.add('hidden');
  retTxnData=null;

  // First check session cache
  const cached=myTxns.find(t=>t.id===id);
  if(cached){
    retTxnData=cached;
    populateReturnForm(cached);
    return;
  }

  // Otherwise look up from backend
  toast('Looking up transaction…','in');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
      if(r&&r.id){
        retTxnData=r;
        populateReturnForm(r);
      } else {
        $('retNotFound').classList.remove('hidden');
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e&&e.message?'Error: '+e.message:'Connection error — please try again','er'))
    .getTransactionById({id,store:user.store,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function populateReturnForm(txn){
  // Look up customer history if we have a name
  const custName = txn.customer || '';
  let custTag = custName
    ? `<span style="cursor:pointer;color:var(--b1);font-weight:700;text-decoration:underline" onclick="lookupReturnCustomer('${custName.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">👤 ${custName}</span>`
    : 'No customer name';

  $('retTxnInfo').innerHTML=
    '🧾 <strong>'+txn.id+'</strong> · '+custTag+
    ' · <strong>₦'+(txn.total||0).toLocaleString()+'</strong> · '+(txn.payment||'');
  const sel=$('retItem'); sel.innerHTML='';
  (txn.items||[]).forEach((item,i)=>{
    sel.add(new Option(item.name+' — ₦'+item.unitPrice.toLocaleString()+' × '+(item.qty||1),i));
  });
  $('retQty').value=1;
  $('retLookupBox').classList.remove('hidden');
  $('retNotFound').classList.add('hidden');
  calcRefund();
}

function calcRefund(){
  if(!retTxnData) return;
  const idx=+$('retItem').value;
  const item=retTxnData.items[idx];
  if(!item) return;
  const qty=Math.min(+($('retQty').value||1), item.qty||1);
  const refund=item.unitPrice*qty;
  $('refundAmt').innerText='₦'+refund.toLocaleString();
  $('refundBox').classList.remove('hidden');
}

function processReturn(){
  if(!retTxnData) return toast('Look up a transaction first','er');
  const idx=+$('retItem').value;
  const item=retTxnData.items[idx];
  const qty=+($('retQty').value||1);
  const reason=$('retReason').value;
  if(!reason) return toast('Select a reason for return','er');
  if(!qty||qty<1) return toast('Enter valid quantity','er');
  if(qty>(item.qty||1)) return toast('Cannot return more than was sold','er');
  const refund=item.unitPrice*qty;

  showModal('Confirm Return',`
    <div style="background:var(--gglow);border:1px solid rgba(15,159,118,.2);border-radius:var(--r4);padding:14px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:12px">
      <div style="font-family:var(--f-d);font-size:24px;font-weight:900;color:var(--gn1)">₦${refund.toLocaleString()}</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gn1);margin-top:3px">Refund via ${retPayMethod}</div>
    </div>
    <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--t2)">Return <strong>${qty}× ${item.name}</strong><br>Reason: <strong>${reason}</strong><br>Stock will be restored.</p>
  `,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:15px">check_circle</span> Confirm Return',c:'btn-ok',fn:()=>{
      closeModal();
      showLoading('Processing return…');
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
          hideLoading();
          toast(r.msg,'ok');
          initReturns();
          loadProds();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e=>{
          hideLoading();
          toast('Error: '+e.message,'er');
        })
        .processReturn({
          originalTxnId:retTxnData.id,
          productID:item.id,
          productName:item.name,
          qty,
          unitPrice:item.unitPrice,
          refundAmount:refund,
          refundMethod:retPayMethod,
          reason,
          returnedBy:user.name,
          store:user.store,
          date:$('retDate').value,
          isWB:item.isWB||false,
          isAdmin:user.isAdmin||false,
          _token:_sessionToken
        });
    }}
  ]);
}

function toggleNoReceipt(){
  const panel=$('noReceiptPanel');
  panel.classList.toggle('hidden');
  if(!panel.classList.contains('hidden')){
    $('nrDateFrom').value='';
    $('nrDateTo').value='';
    $('nrResults').classList.add('hidden');
    $('nrNoResults').classList.add('hidden');
  }
}

function nrQuickDate(range){
  const today=new Date();
  const fmt=d=>d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
  if(range==='today'){
    $('nrDateFrom').value=fmt(today);
    $('nrDateTo').value=fmt(today);
  } else if(range==='yesterday'){
    const y=new Date(today); y.setDate(y.getDate()-1);
    $('nrDateFrom').value=fmt(y);
    $('nrDateTo').value=fmt(y);
  } else if(range==='week'){
    const w=new Date(today); w.setDate(w.getDate()-7);
    $('nrDateFrom').value=fmt(w);
    $('nrDateTo').value=fmt(today);
  } else if(range==='month'){
    const m=new Date(today); m.setDate(1);
    $('nrDateFrom').value=fmt(m);
    $('nrDateTo').value=fmt(today);
  }
}

function searchNoReceipt(){
  const productName=$('nrProduct').value.trim();
  const dateFrom=$('nrDateFrom').value;
  const dateTo=$('nrDateTo').value;
  const amount=$('nrAmount').value;
  const paymentType=$('nrPayment').value;
  const soldBy=$('nrSoldBy').value.trim();

  if(!productName&&!dateFrom&&!amount&&paymentType==='any'&&!soldBy)
    return toast('Enter at least one search detail','er');

  $('nrLoading').classList.remove('hidden');
  $('nrResults').classList.add('hidden');
  $('nrNoResults').classList.add('hidden');

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      $('nrLoading').classList.add('hidden');
      if(!data||data.error||!data.length){
        $('nrNoResults').classList.remove('hidden');
        return;
      }
      const list=$('nrResultsList');
      list.innerHTML='';
      data.forEach(r=>{
        const el=document.createElement('div');
        el.style.cssText='background:var(--s0);border:1px solid var(--bdr);border-radius:var(--r2);padding:10px;cursor:pointer;transition:border-color .15s';
        el.onmouseover=()=>el.style.borderColor='var(--b1)';
        el.onmouseout=()=>el.style.borderColor='var(--bdr)';
        el.innerHTML=`
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:6px">
            <div>
              <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(r.productName)}</div>
              <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3);font-family:var(--f-m);margin-top:1px">${r.saleId}</div>
            </div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1)">₦${Number(r.totalAmount).toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--t3);display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <span>📅 ${new Date(r.date).toLocaleDateString('en-NG')}</span>
            <span>🧾 ${r.paymentType}</span>
            <span>👤 ${r.soldBy}</span>
            <span>📦 Qty: ${r.qty}</span>
          </div>
          <button class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" style="width:100%;margin-top:8px" onclick="useNoReceiptTxn('${r.saleId}')">
            <span class="material-icons-round">check_circle</span>Use This Transaction
          </button>
        `;
        list.appendChild(el);
      });
      $('nrResults').classList.remove('hidden');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ $('nrLoading').classList.add('hidden'); toast('Search error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .searchSalesByDetails({productName,dateFrom,dateTo,amount,paymentType,soldBy,store:user.store,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function useNoReceiptTxn(saleId){
  $('retTxnId').value=saleId;
  $('noReceiptPanel').classList.add('hidden');
  lookupReturn();
}

function lookupReturnCustomer(name){
  toast('Looking up customer history…','in');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(c=>{
      if(!c){ toast('No customer record found for '+name,'wa'); return; }
      showModal('Customer History — '+c.name,`
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-bottom:16px">
          <div class="cust-dk"><div class="cust-dk-v" style="color:var(--gn1)">${c.visitCount}</div><div class="cust-dk-l">Total Visits</div></div>
          <div class="cust-dk"><div class="cust-dk-v" style="color:var(--b1)">₦${(c.totalSpend||0).toLocaleString()}</div><div class="cust-dk-l">Total Spend</div></div>
          <div class="cust-dk"><div class="cust-dk-v" style="color:var(--am1);font-size:13px">${c.lastProduct||'—'}</div><div class="cust-dk-l">Last Purchase</div></div>
        </div>
        ${c.whatsapp?`<div style="padding:10px 12px;background:var(--gglow);border-radius:var(--r4);font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1);margin-bottom:8px">📱 ${c.whatsapp}</div>`:''}
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3)">Last receipt: <span style="font-family:var(--f-m);color:var(--t1)">${c.lastSaleID||'—'}</span></div>
      `,[{label:'Close',fn:closeModal}]);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'))
    .getCustomerByName({name,_token:_sessionToken});
}
/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   NOTIFICATIONS
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
let _notifs=[];
let _notifTimer=null;

function openNotifPanel(){
  renderNotifPanel();
  openOV('notifOV');
  // Mark all as read
  _notifs.forEach(n=>n.read=true);
  saveNotifs();
  updateNotifBadge();
}

function saveNotifs(){
  try{
    localStorage.setItem('notifs_'+user.name, JSON.stringify(_notifs));
  }catch(e){}
}

function loadNotifs(){
  try{
    const saved=localStorage.getItem('notifs_'+user.name);
    if(saved) _notifs=JSON.parse(saved);
    // Clear notifs older than 30 days
    const cutoff=Date.now()-(30*24*60*60*1000);
    _notifs=_notifs.filter(n=>new Date(n.time).getTime()>cutoff);
    saveNotifs();
  }catch(e){ _notifs=[]; }
}

function addNotif(title,msg,type='in',action=null){
  _notifs.unshift({
    id:'N-'+Date.now(),
    title,msg,type,action,
    time:new Date().toISOString(),
    read:false
  });
  // Keep max 50 notifs
  if(_notifs.length>50) _notifs=_notifs.slice(0,50);
  saveNotifs();
  updateNotifBadge();
}

function updateNotifBadge(){
  const unread=_notifs.filter(n=>!n.read).length;
  const total=unread;
  document.querySelectorAll('.notif-badge').forEach(b=>{
    b.innerText=total>99?'99+':total;
    b.style.display=total===0?'none':'flex';
    b.classList.toggle('hidden',total===0);
  });
}

function clearAllNotifs(){
  // Save dismissed IDs so they don't reappear
  const dismissed=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotifs_'+user.name)||'[]');
  _notifs.forEach(n=>{ if(!dismissed.includes(n.id)) dismissed.push(n.id); });
  localStorage.setItem('dismissedNotifs_'+user.name, JSON.stringify(dismissed));
  _notifs=[];
  saveNotifs();
  updateNotifBadge();
  renderNotifPanel();
}

function renderNotifPanel(){
  const list=$('notifList');
  if(!_notifs.length){
    list.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:36px;opacity:.2">notifications_none</span><p style="font-size:13px;margin-top:8px">No notifications</p></div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML='';
  _notifs.forEach(n=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.className='notif-item'+(n.read?'':' unread');
    const icoMap={ok:'check_circle',er:'error',in:'info',wa:'warning'};
    const icoClsMap={ok:'notif-ico-ok',er:'notif-ico-er',in:'notif-ico-in',wa:'notif-ico-wa'};
    const timeStr=new Date(n.time).toLocaleString('en-NG',{day:'numeric',month:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div class="notif-ico ${icoClsMap[n.type]||'notif-ico-in'}">
        <span class="material-icons-round">${icoMap[n.type]||'info'}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="notif-body">
        <div class="notif-title">${esc(n.title)}</div>
        <div class="notif-msg">${esc(n.msg)}</div>
        <div class="notif-time">${timeStr}</div>
      </div>
    `;
    if(n.action) el.onclick=()=>{ closeOV('notifOV'); n.action(); };
    list.appendChild(el);
  });
}

function pollNotifications(){
  const can=p=>user&&user.permissions&&user.permissions.includes(p);
  const isAdmin=user.role==='admin';
  const isManager=user.role==='manager';

  if(isAdmin||(isManager&&can('approve_returns'))){
    loadPendingReturns();
  }
  if(!isAdmin&&!isManager){
    pollStaffNotifications();
  }
  if(can('stock')||isAdmin){
    pollStockNotifications();
  }
  if(!isAdmin){
    pollPermNotifications();
  }
}

function pollStockNotifications(){
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(alerts=>{
      if(!alerts||!alerts.length) return;
      const existingIds=new Set(_notifs.map(n=>n.id));
      const dismissed=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotifs_'+user.name)||'[]');
      alerts.forEach(a=>{
        const nid='STOCK-'+a.type.toUpperCase()+'-'+a.id;
        if(dismissed.includes(nid)) return;
        if(existingIds.has(nid)) return;
        addNotif(
          a.type==='out'?'🔴 Out of Stock':'🟡 Low Stock',
          a.name+' · '+a.store+' · '+a.stock+' units remaining',
          a.type==='out'?'er':'wa',
          ()=>goPage('stock')
        );
        _notifs[0].id=nid;
        saveNotifs();
        updateNotifBadge();
      });
    })
    .getStockAlerts(_sessionToken);
}

function pollStaffNotifications(){
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      if(!data||!data.length) return;
      const existingIds=new Set(_notifs.map(n=>n.id));
      const dismissed=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotifs_'+user.name)||'[]');
      data.forEach(r=>{
        const nid='RET-UPDATE-'+r.returnId+'-'+r.status;
        if(dismissed.includes(nid)) return;
        if(!existingIds.has(nid) && (r.status==='Approved'||r.status==='Rejected')){
          const isApproved=r.status==='Approved';
          addNotif(
            isApproved?'✅ Return Approved':'❌ Return Rejected',
            r.productName+' × '+r.qty+' · ₦'+Number(r.refundAmount).toLocaleString()+
            (isApproved?' · Stock restored':' · Contact admin for details'),
            isApproved?'ok':'er',
            ()=>goPage('returns')
          );
          _notifs[0].id=nid;
          saveNotifs();
          updateNotifBadge();
        }
      });
    })
    .getMyReturns({returnedBy:user.name,store:user.store,_token:_sessionToken});

  }

function pollPermNotifications(){
  const permLabels={
    stock:'Stock Management',
    expenses:'Expenses',
    prices:'Price Updates',
    approve_returns:'Approve Returns',
    staff_mgmt:'Staff Management',
    barcode:'Barcode Generator',
    activity:'Activity View',
    dashboard_ops:'Dashboard',
    dashboard_finance:'Financial Dashboard',
    attendance_view:'Attendance View',
  };

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(notifs=>{
      if(!notifs||!notifs.length) return;
      notifs.forEach(n=>{
        const isAdd=n.type==='PERM_ADDED';
        const label=permLabels[n.perm]||n.perm;
        addNotif(
          isAdd?'✅ New Access Granted':'🚫 Access Removed',
          isAdd?`You now have access to: ${label}`:`Your access to ${label} has been removed`,
          isAdd?'ok':'er',
          null
        );
        saveNotifs();
        updateNotifBadge();
        google.script.run.clearPermNotif({row:n.row,_token:_sessionToken});
      });
    })
    .getPermNotifs({staffName:user.name,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function switchRetTab(tab){
  ['pending','approved','all'].forEach(t=>{
    const rp=$('retPanel'+t.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+t.slice(1)); if(rp) rp.classList.toggle('hidden', t!==tab);
    const rt=$('retTab'+t.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+t.slice(1)); if(rt) rt.classList.toggle('on', t===tab);
  });
  if(tab==='approved') loadApprovedReturns();
  if(tab==='all') loadAllReturns();
}

function loadApprovedReturns(){
  const list=$('approvedReturnsList');
  const empty=$('retNoApproved');
  list.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:28px;animation:spin .8s linear infinite">refresh</span><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:8px">Loading approved returns…</div></div>';
  if(empty) empty.classList.add('hidden');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      if(!data||!data.length){ list.innerHTML=''; if(empty) empty.classList.remove('hidden'); return; }
      if(empty) empty.classList.add('hidden');
      _retAppPage=1;
      renderApprovedReturns(data);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ list.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--rd1);font-size:12px">Failed to load</div>'; toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .getReturnsByStatus({status:'Approved',_token:_sessionToken});
}

function renderApprovedReturns(data){
  const list=$('approvedReturnsList');
  const total=data.length;
  const maxPage=Math.ceil(total/_retPerPage);
  _retAppPage=Math.max(1,Math.min(_retAppPage,maxPage));
  const start=(_retAppPage-1)*_retPerPage;
  const items=data.slice(start,start+_retPerPage);
  list.innerHTML='';

  if(maxPage>1){
    const pb=document.createElement('div');
    pb.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px';
    pb.innerHTML=`
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3)">Showing ${start+1}–${Math.min(start+_retPerPage,total)} of ${total}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">
        <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_retAppPage--;loadApprovedReturns()" ${_retAppPage===1?'disabled':''}>
          <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span>
        </button>
        <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Page ${_retAppPage} of ${maxPage}</span>
        <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_retAppPage++;loadApprovedReturns()" ${_retAppPage===maxPage?'disabled':''}>
          <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
    list.appendChild(pb);
  }

  items.forEach(r=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.style.cssText='background:var(--s1);border:1px solid var(--bdr);border-radius:var(--r2);padding:11px 13px;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center';
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(r.productName)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:2px">${esc(r.returnId)} · Txn: ${esc(r.originalTxnId)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3)">By: ${esc(r.returnedBy)} · ${new Date(r.date).toLocaleDateString('en-NG')}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="text-align:right">
        <div style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1)">₦${Number(r.refundAmount).toLocaleString()}</div>
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--gn1);font-weight:700;margin-top:3px">✅ Approved</div>
      </div>
    `;
    list.appendChild(el);
  });
}

function loadAllReturns(){
  const tbody=$('allReturnsTable');
  const empty=$('retNoAll');
  tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:28px;animation:spin .8s linear infinite">refresh</span><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:8px">Loading all returns…</div></td></tr>';
  if(empty) empty.classList.add('hidden');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      if(!data||!data.length){ tbody.innerHTML=''; if(empty) empty.classList.remove('hidden'); return; }
      if(empty) empty.classList.add('hidden');
      _retAllPage=1;
      renderAllReturns(data);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--rd1);font-size:12px">Failed to load</td></tr>'; toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .getAllReturns(_sessionToken);
}

function renderAllReturns(data){
  const tbody=$('allReturnsTable');
  const total=data.length;
  const maxPage=Math.ceil(total/_retPerPage);
  _retAllPage=Math.max(1,Math.min(_retAllPage,maxPage));
  const start=(_retAllPage-1)*_retPerPage;
  const items=data.slice(start,start+_retPerPage);
  const statusColor={Pending:'var(--am1)',Approved:'var(--gn1)',Rejected:'var(--rd1)'};

  tbody.innerHTML=items.map(r=>`
    <tr style="border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr);transition:background .15s" onmouseover="this.style.background='var(--s1)'" onmouseout="this.style.background=''">
      <td style="padding:8px;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--t2)">${r.returnId}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(r.productName)}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--t3)">${r.originalTxnId}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;text-align:center;color:var(--t2)">${r.qty}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;text-align:right;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1)">₦${Number(r.refundAmount).toLocaleString()}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;text-align:center"><span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:${statusColor[r.status]||'var(--t4)'}22;color:${statusColor[r.status]||'var(--t4)'}">${r.status}</span></td>
      <td style="padding:8px;color:var(--t3);font-size:11px">${esc(r.returnedBy)}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');

  // Add pagination row at bottom of table
  if(maxPage>1){
    tbody.innerHTML+=`
      <tr>
        <td colspan="7" style="padding:12px;text-align:center;border-top:1px solid var(--bdr)">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px">
            <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_retAllPage--;loadAllReturns()" ${_retAllPage===1?'disabled':''}>
              <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span> Prev
            </button>
            <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Page ${_retAllPage} of ${maxPage} · ${total} total</span>
            <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_retAllPage++;loadAllReturns()" ${_retAllPage===maxPage?'disabled':''}>
              Next <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }
}

function filterReturns(){
  const q=$('retSearch').value.toLowerCase().trim();
  if(!q) return;
  switchRetTab('all');
  const tbody=$('allReturnsTable');
  const empty=$('retNoAll');
  tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:28px;animation:spin .8s linear infinite">refresh</span><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:8px">Searching…</div></td></tr>';
  if(empty) empty.classList.add('hidden');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      const filtered=(data||[]).filter(r=>
        r.returnId.toLowerCase().includes(q)||
        r.originalTxnId.toLowerCase().includes(q)||
        r.productName.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
      if(!filtered.length){ tbody.innerHTML=''; if(empty) empty.classList.remove('hidden'); return; }
      if(empty) empty.classList.add('hidden');
      const statusColor={Pending:'var(--am1)',Approved:'var(--gn1)',Rejected:'var(--rd1)'};
      tbody.innerHTML=filtered.map(r=>`
        <tr style="border-bottom:1px solid var(--bdr)">
          <td style="padding:8px;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--t2)">${r.returnId}</td>
          <td style="padding:8px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${esc(r.productName)}</td>
          <td style="padding:8px;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--t3)">${r.originalTxnId}</td>
          <td style="padding:8px;text-align:center;color:var(--t2)">${r.qty}</td>
          <td style="padding:8px;text-align:right;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:var(--gn1)">₦${Number(r.refundAmount).toLocaleString()}</td>
          <td style="padding:8px;text-align:center"><span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:${statusColor[r.status]||'var(--t4)'}22;color:${statusColor[r.status]||'var(--t4)'}">${r.status}</span></td>
          <td style="padding:8px;color:var(--t3);font-size:11px">${esc(r.returnedBy)}</td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{ tbody.innerHTML=''; toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
    .getAllReturns(_sessionToken);
}

function sendWAReceipt(waNum,id,tot,pay){
  const num='234'+String(waNum).replace(/^0/,'');
  
  // Build items list from receipt content
  const items=[];
  document.querySelectorAll('#rcptCnt [data-item]').forEach(el=>{
    items.push(el.getAttribute('data-item'));
  });

  // Get store and cashier from user
  const store=user.store||'StarTrack';
  const cashier=user.name||'';
  const date=new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
  const time=new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-NG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});

  // Build items text from cart snapshot stored in receipt
  let itemsText='';
  if(window._lastReceiptItems&&window._lastReceiptItems.length){
    window._lastReceiptItems.forEach((item,i)=>{
      const itemTotal=item.unitPrice*(item.qty||1);
      itemsText+='  '+(i+1)+'. '+item.name+'\n';
      itemsText+='     '+(item.isWB?'1':item.qty)+' × ₦'+Number(item.unitPrice).toLocaleString()+' = ₦'+itemTotal.toLocaleString()+'\n';
    });
  }

  const sub=window._lastReceiptSub||Number(tot);
  const disc=window._lastReceiptDisc||0;

  const msg=encodeURIComponent(
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'+
    '🏪 *Tado Foods POS*\n'+
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'+
    '📍 '+store+'\n'+
    '📅 '+date+'\n'+
    '🕐 '+time+'\n'+
    '🧾 Receipt: *'+id+'*\n'+
    '👤 Cashier: '+cashier+'\n'+
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'+
    '*ITEMS PURCHASED*\n'+
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'+
    itemsText+
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'+
    (disc>0?'Subtotal:  ₦'+Number(sub).toLocaleString()+'\nDiscount:  -₦'+Number(disc).toLocaleString()+'\n':'')+
    '💰 *TOTAL: ₦'+Number(tot).toLocaleString()+'*\n'+
    '💳 Payment: *'+pay+'*\n'+
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'+
    '✅ *PAYMENT CONFIRMED*\n'+
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'+
    'Thank you for shopping\n'+
    'with us! 🙏\n\n'+
    '_Tado Foods POS_'
  );
  window.open('https://wa.me/'+num+'?text='+msg,'_blank');
}
function loadActivityMonth(){
  const val=$('actMonth').value;
  if(val===''){
    // Reset to default 14 days
    myTxns=[];
    $('actDate').value='';
    $('actTxns').innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3">sync</span><p style="margin-top:10px;font-size:13px">Loading…</p></div>';
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
        myTxns=[];
        if(data&&data.transactions) data.transactions.forEach(t=>{ if(!t.time&&t.date) t.time=t.date; myTxns.push(t); });
        _actPage=1;
        renderActivity();
      })
      .getSalespersonActivity({soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:null,days:14,_token:_sessionToken});
    return;
  }

  // Load specific month
  const monthsAgo=Number(val);
  const d=new Date();
  d.setMonth(d.getMonth()-monthsAgo);
  const year=d.getFullYear();
  const month=d.getMonth();
  const monthName=d.toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{month:'long',year:'numeric'});

  $('actTxns').innerHTML=`<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--t3)"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3">sync</span><p style="margin-top:10px;font-size:13px">Loading ${monthName}…</p></div>`;

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data=>{
      myTxns=[];
      if(data&&data.transactions) data.transactions.forEach(t=>{ if(!t.time&&t.date) t.time=t.date; myTxns.push(t); });
      _actPage=1;
      $('actDate').value='';
      renderActivity();
    })
    .getSalespersonActivity({soldBy:user.name,store:user.store,date:null,days:null,month,year,_token:_sessionToken});
}

let _invExportFmt='pdf';
function setInvFmt(el,fmt){
  _invExportFmt=fmt;
  document.querySelectorAll('#invFmtPDF,#invFmtExcel').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('on'));
  el.classList.add('on');
}

function runInvExport(){
  if(_invExportFmt==='excel') exportInventoryExcel();
  else printInventory();
}

function exportInventoryExcel(){
  const store=$('dashStoreFilter')?$('dashStoreFilter').value||null:null;
  toast('Preparing Excel export…','in');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(inv=>{
      let rows=[];
      (inv.electronics||[]).forEach(p=>rows.push({...p,_type:'Electronics'}));
      (inv.windowblind||[]).forEach(p=>rows.push({...p,_type:'Window Blind'}));
      if(store) rows=rows.filter(r=>String(r.store||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'')=== store.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,''));

      // Build CSV content
      const headers=['#','Product Name','Category','Type','Stock','List Price','Cost','Expense on Cost','Total Stock Value','Reorder Level','Status','Store'];
      const csvRows=[headers.join(',')];
      rows.forEach((r,i)=>{
        const tsv=((r.cost||0)+(r.expenseOnCost||0))*(r.stock||0);
        const status=r.stock<=0?'Out of Stock':r.stock<=r.reorder?'Low Stock':'Available';
        const row=[
          i+1,
          '"'+(r.name||'').replace(/"/g,'""')+'"',
          '"'+(r.category||'').replace(/"/g,'""')+'"',
          r._type,
          r.stock||0,
          r.price||0,
          r.cost||0,
          r.expenseOnCost||0,
          tsv,
          r.reorder||0,
          status,
          '"'+(r.store||'').replace(/"/g,'""')+'"'
        ];
        csvRows.push(row.join(','));
      });

      // Add totals row
      const totalStock=rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.stock||0),0);
      const totalValue=rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+((r.cost||0)+(r.expenseOnCost||0))*(r.stock||0),0);
      csvRows.push(['','TOTAL','','',totalStock,'','','',totalValue,'','',''].join(','));

      const csv=csvRows.join('\n');
      const blob=new Blob(['\uFEFF'+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'});
      const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a=document.createElement('a');
      const now=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
      a.href=url;
      a.download='Inventory_'+(store||'AllStores')+'_'+now+'.csv';
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      document.body.removeChild(a);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      toast('Excel file downloaded','ok');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast('Export error: '+e.message,'er'))
    .getInventoryOverview({store:store||null,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function openInvPrintModal(){
  const sel=$('invPrintStore');
  if(sel&&user.availableStores){
    sel.innerHTML='<option value="">All Stores</option>';
    user.availableStores.forEach(s=>sel.add(new Option(s,s)));
    // Pre-select current dashboard store filter if set
    const cur=$('dashStoreFilter')?$('dashStoreFilter').value:'';
    if(cur) sel.value=cur;
  }
  openOV('invPrintOV');
}

function printInventory(){
  const store=$('invPrintStore').value;
  const type=$('invPrintType').value;
  const excludeZero=$('invExcludeZero').checked;
  const cols={
    cat:$('invColCat').checked,
    stock:$('invColStock').checked,
    price:$('invColPrice').checked,
    cost:$('invColCost').checked,
    exp:$('invColExp').checked,
    tsv:$('invColTSV').checked,
    store:$('invColStore').checked,
    status:$('invColStatus').checked
  };
  toast('Preparing inventory…','in');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(inv=>{
      let rows=[];
      if(type==='electronics'||type==='both'){
        (inv.electronics||[]).forEach(p=>rows.push({...p,_type:'Electronics'}));
      }
      if(type==='windowblind'||type==='both'){
        (inv.windowblind||[]).forEach(p=>rows.push({...p,_type:'Window Blind'}));
      }
      if(store) rows=rows.filter(r=>String(r.store||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'')=== store.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,''));
      if(excludeZero) rows=rows.filter(r=>r.stock>0);

      const now=new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-NG',{day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
      const storeLabel=store||'All Stores';

      // Build header row
      let headers='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:left;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">#</th>';
      headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:left;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Product Name</th>';
      if(cols.cat) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:left;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Category</th>';
      if(cols.stock) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Stock</th>';
      if(cols.price) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">List Price</th>';
      if(cols.cost) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Cost</th>';
      if(cols.exp) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Exp. on Cost</th>';
      if(cols.tsv) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Stock Value</th>';
      if(cols.store) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:left;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Store</th>';
      if(cols.status) headers+='<th style="padding:7px 10px;text-align:center;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#1a48cc;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px">Status</th>';

      const totalValue=cols.tsv?rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+((r.cost||0)+(r.expenseOnCost||0))*(r.stock||0),0):0;

      const bodyRows=rows.map((r,i)=>{
        const even=i%2===0;
        const bg=even?'#ffffff':'#f8fafd';
        const statusColor=r.stock<=0?'#dc2626':r.stock<=r.reorder?'#d97706':'#059669';
        const statusLabel=r.stock<=0?'Out of Stock':r.stock<=r.reorder?'Low Stock':'Available';
        let tr=`<tr style="background:${bg}">`;
        tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;color:#6b84af;font-family:'Courier New',monospace">${i+1}</td>`;
        tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#0a1628">${r.name}</td>`;
        if(cols.cat) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;color:#6b84af">${r.category||'—'}</td>`;
        if(cols.stock) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;text-align:right;color:${statusColor};font-family:'Courier New',monospace">${r.stock}</td>`;
        if(cols.price) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;text-align:right;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;color:#0a1628">₦${Number(r.price||0).toLocaleString()}</td>`;
        if(cols.cost) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;text-align:right;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;color:#0a1628">₦${Number(r.cost||0).toLocaleString()}</td>`;
        if(cols.exp) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;text-align:right;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;color:#0a1628">₦${Number(r.expenseOnCost||0).toLocaleString()}</td>`;
        if(cols.tsv) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;text-align:right;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;font-weight:700;color:#2254e0">₦${(((r.cost||0)+(r.expenseOnCost||0))*(r.stock||0)).toLocaleString()}</td>`;
        if(cols.store) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;color:#6b84af">${r.store||'—'}</td>`;
        if(cols.status) tr+=`<td style="padding:6px 10px;text-align:center"><span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:${statusColor}22;color:${statusColor}">${statusLabel}</span></td>`;
        tr+='</tr>';
        return tr;
      }).join('');

      const pw=window.open('','_blank','width=1000,height=750');
      if(!pw){ toast('Allow popups to print','wa'); return; }
      pw.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head>
        <meta charset="UTF-8"><title>Inventory Invoice — ${storeLabel}</title>
        <style>
          *{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}
          body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;background:#fff;padding:20px;color:#0a1628}
          .nop{display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:center;padding:10px;margin-bottom:16px;background:#f8fafd;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e7f0}
          .nop button{padding:8px 18px;border:none;border-radius:7px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer}
          .pb{background:#2254e0;color:#fff}.cb{background:#6b7280;color:#fff}
          table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px}
          @media print{.nop{display:none!important}@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:8mm}}
        </style>
      </head><body>
        <div class="nop">
          <button class="pb" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ Print / Save PDF</button>
          <button class="cb" onclick="window.close()">✕ Close</button>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:20px;padding-bottom:16px;border-bottom:2px solid #1a48cc">
          <div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:900;color:#1a48cc;letter-spacing:-.5px">Tado Foods POS</div>
          <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#0a1628;margin-top:4px">Inventory Invoice</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#6b84af;margin-top:3px">Store: ${storeLabel} &nbsp;·&nbsp; Generated: ${now} &nbsp;·&nbsp; By: ${user.name}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#6b84af;margin-top:1px">Total Items: ${rows.length}${excludeZero?' (zero-stock excluded)':''}${cols.tsv?' &nbsp;·&nbsp; Total Stock Value: ₦'+totalValue.toLocaleString():''}</div>
        </div>
        <table>
          <thead><tr>${headers}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>${bodyRows}</tbody>
        </table>
        <script>window.onload=function(){setTimeout(function(){window.print();},700);}<\/script>
      </body></html>`);
      pw.document.close();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast(e&&e.message?'Error: '+e.message:'Connection error — please try again','er'))
    .getInventoryOverview({store:store||null,_token:_sessionToken});
}
