/* ─── Customer module state ─────────────────────────────────────── */
var _custData=[], _custFilter='all', _custSort={key:'daysSinceLastVisit',dir:1}, _custPage=1;
var _custPP=20;
var _custPalette=['#2254e0','#0f9f76','#c97c0a','#e02424','#8b5cf6','#ec4899','#14b8a6','#f97316','#06b6d4','#22c55e'];
function _custAvatarColor(name){const p=['#2254e0','#0f9f76','#c97c0a','#e02424','#8b5cf6','#ec4899','#14b8a6','#f97316','#06b6d4','#22c55e'];const n=String(name||'?');let h=0;for(let i=0;i<n.length;i++)h=n.charCodeAt(i)+((h<<5)-h);return p[Math.abs(h)%p.length];}
function _custInitials(name){return(name||'?').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0,2).map(w=>w[0]).join('').toUpperCase()}

function initCustomers(){
  const pager=$('custPager');
  if(pager) pager.innerHTML='';
  _custFilter='all';
  _custPage=1;
  _custData=[];
  const sf=$('custStoreFilter');
  if(sf&&user.availableStores&&user.availableStores.length>1){
    sf.style.display='';
    sf.innerHTML='<option value="">All Stores</option>';
    user.availableStores.forEach(s=>sf.add(new Option(s,s)));
  }
  _custFilter='all';
  document.querySelectorAll('#page-customers .chip').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('on'));
  const cf=$('cfAll'); if(cf) cf.classList.add('on');
  loadCustomers();
}

function loadCustomers(){
  const pager=$('custPager');
  if(pager) pager.innerHTML='';
  const store=($('custStoreFilter')&&$('custStoreFilter').style.display!=='none')?($('custStoreFilter').value||null):null;
  if(_custDataCache&&!store){
    _custData=_custDataCache.customers||[];
    _renderKPIs(_custDataCache.stats||{});
    _renderSpotlight();
    _custPage=1;
    applyCustFilter();
    const bdg=$('custBdg');
    if(bdg){const rc=_custDataCache.stats.returning||0;bdg.innerText=rc;bdg.classList.toggle('hidden',rc===0);}
    return;
  }
  const tbody=$('custTableBody');
  if(tbody) tbody.innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="9" class="cust-empty-cell"><span class="material-icons-round spin" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.3;display:block;margin-bottom:8px">sync</span>Loading customers…</td></tr>`;
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(d=>{
      _custData=d.customers||[];
      _renderKPIs(d.stats||{});
      _renderSpotlight();
      _custPage=1;
      applyCustFilter();
      const bdg=$('custBdg');
      if(bdg){const rc=d.stats.returning||0;bdg.innerText=rc;bdg.classList.toggle('hidden',rc===0);}
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>{
      if(tbody) tbody.innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="9" class="cust-empty-cell" style="color:var(--rd1)"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:28px;display:block;margin-bottom:8px">wifi_off</span>${e.message||'Could not load customers'}</td></tr>`;
      toast('Customer data failed to load','er');
    })
    .getCustomerData({store,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function _renderKPIs(s){
  const set=(id,val,pre)=>{const el=$(id);if(el)countUp(el,val,pre,550)};
  set('ckTotal',s.total||0,''); set('ckReturn',s.returning||0,'');
  set('ckNew',s.newThisMonth||0,''); set('ckActive',s.activeThisWeek||0,'');
  set('ckSpend',s.totalSpend||0,'₦'); set('ckAvg',s.avgSpend||0,'₦');
  $('custSub').innerText=`${s.total||0} customers · ${s.returning||0} returning · ${s.newThisMonth||0} new this month`;
}

function _renderSpotlight(){
  const returning=_custData.filter(c=>c.isReturning).sort((a,b)=>b.visitCount-a.visitCount).slice(0,15);
  const sec=$('custSpotlight'),cnt=$('custReturnCount'),cards=$('custSpotlightCards');
  if(!sec) return;
  if(!returning.length){sec.classList.add('hidden');return;}
  sec.classList.remove('hidden');
  if(cnt) cnt.innerText=returning.length;
  if(!cards) return;
  cards.innerHTML='';
  returning.forEach(c=>{
    const card=document.createElement('div'); card.className='cust-ret-card';
    card.onclick=()=>openCustomerDetail(c);
    const d=c.daysSinceLastVisit;
    const lastStr=d===null?'':d===0?'Today':d===1?'Yesterday':`${d}d ago`;
    card.innerHTML=`<div class="cust-ret-name">${esc(c.name)}</div><div class="cust-ret-meta"><span class="material-icons-round">replay</span>${c.visitCount} visit${c.visitCount!==1?'s':''}</div><div class="cust-ret-spend">₦${(c.totalSpend||0).toLocaleString()}</div>${lastStr?`<div class="cust-ret-last">${lastStr}</div>`:''}`;
    cards.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function setCustFilter(filter,el){
  if(!_custData||!_custData.length) return;
  _custFilter=filter||'all';
  if(!_custSort) _custSort={key:'daysSinceLastVisit',dir:1};
  document.querySelectorAll('#page-customers .chip').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('on'));
  if(el) el.classList.add('on');
  _custPage=1; applyCustFilter();
}

function filterCustomers(){_custPage=1;applyCustFilter();}

function applyCustFilter(){
  if(!_custSort) _custSort={key:'daysSinceLastVisit',dir:1};
  if(!_custData) _custData=[];
  if(!_custFilter) _custFilter='all';
  const q=($('custSearch')?$('custSearch').value||'':'').toLowerCase().trim();
  let list=_custData.filter(c=>{
    if(q&&!c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)&&!(c.whatsapp||'').includes(q)&&!(c.favouriteProduct||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
    if(_custFilter==='returning'&&!c.isReturning) return false;
    if(_custFilter==='new'&&!c.isNewThisMonth) return false;
    if(_custFilter==='active'&&(c.daysSinceLastVisit===null||c.daysSinceLastVisit>7)) return false;
    if(_custFilter==='dormant'&&(c.daysSinceLastVisit===null||c.daysSinceLastVisit<=30)) return false;
    return true;
  });
  console.log('applyCustFilter: _custData='+_custData.length+' list='+list.length+' filter='+_custFilter);
  const k=_custSort.key,dir=_custSort.dir;
  list.sort((a,b)=>{
    let av=a[k]??(typeof a[k]==='number'?Infinity:'');
    let bv=b[k]??(typeof b[k]==='number'?Infinity:'');
    if(typeof av==='string') av=av.toLowerCase();
    if(typeof bv==='string') bv=bv.toLowerCase();
    return av<bv?-dir:av>bv?dir:0;
  });
  renderCustomersTable(list);
}

function sortCust(key){
  if(_custSort.key===key) _custSort.dir*=-1; else{_custSort.key=key;_custSort.dir=1;}
  document.querySelectorAll('.csico').forEach(el=>el.innerText='');
  const ico=$('csi-'+key); if(ico) ico.innerText=_custSort.dir===1?' ↑':' ↓';
  _custPage=1; applyCustFilter();
}

function renderCustomersTable(list){
  const tbody=$('custTableBody'),pager=$('custPager');
  if(!tbody) return;
  if(!list.length){
    tbody.innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="9" class="cust-empty-cell"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:32px;opacity:.15;display:block;margin-bottom:8px">person_search</span>No customers match this filter</td></tr>`;
    if(pager) pager.innerHTML=''; return;
  }
  const total=list.length;
  if(!total){
    tbody.innerHTML=`<tr><td colspan="9" class="cust-empty-cell">No customers match this filter</td></tr>`;
    if(pager) pager.innerHTML='';
    return;
  }
  const perPage=20;
  const maxPage=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/perPage));
  _custPage=Number.isFinite(_custPage)&&_custPage>0?Math.min(Math.floor(_custPage),maxPage):1;
  const start=(_custPage-1)*perPage;
  const page=list.slice(start,start+perPage);
  if(pager){
    if(total<=perPage){
      pager.innerHTML=`<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3)">${total} customer${total!==1?'s':''}</span>`;
    } else {
      pager.innerHTML=`
        <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_custPage--;applyCustFilter()" ${_custPage===1?'disabled':''}>
          <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_left</span>
        </button>
        <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">Page ${_custPage} of ${maxPage} · ${total} customers</span>
        <button class="btn btn-s btn-sm" onclick="_custPage++;applyCustFilter()" ${_custPage===maxPage?'disabled':''}>
          <span class="material-icons-round">chevron_right</span>
        </button>`;
    }
  }
  const WA_ICON=`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M17.472 14.382c-.297-.149-1.758-.867-2.03-.967-.273-.099-.471-.148-.67.15-.197.297-.767.966-.94 1.164-.173.199-.347.223-.644.075-.297-.15-1.255-.463-2.39-1.475-.883-.788-1.48-1.761-1.653-2.059-.173-.297-.018-.458.13-.606.134-.133.298-.347.446-.52.149-.174.198-.298.298-.497.099-.198.05-.371-.025-.52-.075-.149-.669-1.612-.916-2.207-.242-.579-.487-.5-.669-.51a12.8 12.8 0 0 0-.57-.01c-.198 0-.52.074-.792.372-.272.297-1.04 1.016-1.04 2.479 0 1.462 1.065 2.875 1.213 3.074.149.198 2.096 3.2 5.077 4.487.709.306 1.262.489 1.694.625.712.227 1.36.195 1.871.118.571-.085 1.758-.719 2.006-1.413.248-.694.248-1.289.173-1.413-.074-.124-.272-.198-.57-.347m-5.421 7.403h-.004a9.87 9.87 0 0 1-5.031-1.378l-.361-.214-3.741.982.998-3.648-.235-.374a9.86 9.86 0 0 1-1.51-5.26c.001-5.45 4.436-9.884 9.888-9.884 2.64 0 5.122 1.03 6.988 2.898a9.825 9.825 0 0 1 2.893 6.994c-.003 5.45-4.437 9.884-9.885 9.884m8.413-18.297A11.815 11.815 0 0 0 12.05 0C5.495 0 .16 5.335.157 11.892c0 2.096.547 4.142 1.588 5.945L.057 24l6.305-1.654a11.882 11.882 0 0 0 5.683 1.448h.005c6.554 0 11.89-5.335 11.893-11.893a11.821 11.821 0 0 0-3.48-8.413Z"/></svg>`;
  const _pageFiltered = page.filter(c=>c&&c.name);
  window._custPageMap = {};
  _pageFiltered.forEach((c, mapIdx) => {
    window._custPageMap[mapIdx] = _custData.indexOf(c);
  });
  tbody.innerHTML=_pageFiltered.map((c,idx)=>{
    const _ci = window._custPageMap[idx] !== -1 ? window._custPageMap[idx] : _custData.indexOf(c);
    const color=_custAvatarColor(c.name),init=_custInitials(c.name),d=c.daysSinceLastVisit;
    const vpColor=c.visitCount>=5?'var(--gn1)':c.visitCount>=2?'var(--am1)':'var(--t3)';
    const vpBg=c.visitCount>=5?'var(--gglow)':c.visitCount>=2?'var(--aglow)':'var(--s2)';
    const lvStr=d===null?'—':d===0?'Today':d===1?'Yesterday':`${d}d ago`;
    const lvColor=d===null?'var(--t3)':d<=3?'var(--gn1)':d<=14?'var(--t2)':d>30?'var(--t4)':'var(--t2)';
    const statusBadge=c.isReturning?`<span class="bdg bdg-g"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:11px">replay</span> Returning</span>`:c.isNewThisMonth?`<span class="bdg bdg-a">New</span>`:`<span class="bdg" style="background:var(--s2);color:var(--t3)">One-time</span>`;
    const waNum=(c.whatsapp||'').replace(/^0/,'').replace(/\D/g,'');
    const waBtn=waNum?`<a class="cust-wa" href="https://wa.me/234${waNum}" target="_blank" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">${WA_ICON}</a>`:`<span style="color:var(--t5);font-size:11px;display:block;text-align:center">—</span>`;
    return `<tr style="cursor:pointer" onclick="openCustomerDetail(_custData[${_ci}])">
      <td><div class="cust-name-wrap"><div class="cust-av" style="background:${color}">${esc(init)}</div><div><div class="cust-nm">${esc(c.name)}</div><div class="cust-st">${esc(c.store)}</div></div></div></td>
      <td style="text-align:center"><div class="cust-vis-pill" style="background:${vpBg};color:${vpColor}">${c.visitCount}</div></td>
      <td style="text-align:right;font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:var(--b1)">₦${(c.totalSpend||0).toLocaleString()}</td>
      <td style="text-align:right;font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:12px;color:var(--t2)">₦${(c.avgSpendPerVisit||0).toLocaleString()}</td>
      <td style="max-width:160px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--t2);font-size:12px">${esc(c.favouriteProduct||'—')}</td>
      <td style="text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:${lvColor};font-weight:600">${lvStr}</td>
      <td style="text-align:center">${statusBadge}</td>
      <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()">${waBtn}</td>
      <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()" style="text-align:center"><button class="cust-view-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openCustomerDetail(_custData[${_ci}])"><span class="material-icons-round">person</span></button></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}

function openCustomerDetail(cRaw){
  let c;
  try{
    if(typeof cRaw==='number'||!isNaN(cRaw)){
      c=_custData[cRaw];
    } else {
      c=typeof cRaw==='string'?JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(cRaw)):cRaw;
    }
  }catch(e){toast('Could not open customer detail','er');return;}
  if(!c){toast('Customer not found','er');return;}
  const color=_custAvatarColor(c.name),init=_custInitials(c.name),d=c.daysSinceLastVisit;
  const lastStr=d===null?'—':d===0?'Today':d===1?'Yesterday':`${d} days ago`;
  const lastColor=d===null?'var(--t3)':d<=7?'var(--gn1)':d>30?'var(--rd1)':'var(--t1)';
  const prods=(c.allProducts||[]).sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count);
  const maxProd=prods.length?prods[0].count:1;
  const waNum=(c.whatsapp||'').replace(/^0/,'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  $('custDetailHdr').innerHTML=`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px"><div class="cust-av" style="background:${color};width:42px;height:42px;font-size:16px">${init}</div><div><div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t1)">${esc(c.name)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:1px">${esc(c.store)}</div></div><div style="margin-left:8px;display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">${c.isReturning?`<span class="bdg bdg-g"><span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:11px">star</span> Returning</span>`:'<span class="bdg" style="background:var(--s2);color:var(--t3)">One-time</span>'}${c.isNewThisMonth?'<span class="bdg bdg-a">New</span>':''}${d!==null&&d>30?'<span class="bdg bdg-r">Dormant</span>':''}</div></div><button class="ib" onclick="closeOV('custDetailOV')"><span class="material-icons-round">close</span></button>`;
  $('custDetailBody').innerHTML=`<div class="cust-detail-kpis"><div class="cust-dk"><div class="cust-dk-v" style="color:var(--gn1)">${c.visitCount}</div><div class="cust-dk-l">Total Visits</div></div><div class="cust-dk"><div class="cust-dk-v" style="color:var(--b1)">₦${(c.totalSpend||0).toLocaleString()}</div><div class="cust-dk-l">Total Spend</div></div><div class="cust-dk"><div class="cust-dk-v" style="color:${lastColor};font-size:14px">${lastStr}</div><div class="cust-dk-l">Last Visit</div></div></div>
  <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-bottom:16px"><div class="cust-dk"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;margin-bottom:5px">First Visit</div><div style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${c.firstVisit||'—'}</div></div><div class="cust-dk"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--t3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;margin-bottom:5px">Avg Spend / Visit</div><div style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--am1)">₦${(c.avgSpendPerVisit||0).toLocaleString()}</div></div></div>
  ${prods.length?`<div style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="stit" style="margin-bottom:10px">What They Buy</div>${prods.map(p=>`<div class="cust-prod-row"><div class="cust-prod-name">${esc(p.name)}</div><div class="cust-prod-bar-wrap"><div class="cust-prod-bar-fill" style="width:${Math.round((p.count/maxProd)*100)}%"></div></div><div class="cust-prod-count">${p.count}×</div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}
  <div class="stit" style="margin-bottom:10px">Contact</div>
  ${waNum?`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:12px 14px;background:var(--s1);border:1px solid var(--bdr);border-radius:var(--r2);margin-bottom:16px"><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px"><span class="material-icons-round" style="color:#25D366;font-size:20px">phone</span><span style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-size:13px;color:var(--t1)">${c.whatsapp}</span></div><a href="https://wa.me/234${waNum}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-ok btn-sm" style="gap:6px"><span class="material-icons-round">chat</span>WhatsApp</a></div>`:`<div style="padding:14px;background:var(--s1);border-radius:var(--r2);font-size:12px;color:var(--t4);text-align:center;margin-bottom:16px">No WhatsApp number recorded</div>`}
  ${c.lastSaleID?`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:10px 12px;background:var(--bglow2);border-radius:var(--r4);font-size:12px"><span class="material-icons-round" style="color:var(--b1);font-size:16px">receipt</span><span style="color:var(--t3)">Last Receipt:</span><span style="font-family:var(--f-m);font-weight:700;color:var(--b1)">${c.lastSaleID}</span></div>`:''}`;
  openOV('custDetailOV');
}

function exportCustomerCSV(){
  if(!_custData.length) return toast('No customer data to export','wa');
  const headers=['Name','WhatsApp','Visits','Total Spend (₦)','Avg Spend / Visit (₦)','Favourite Product','First Visit','Last Visit','Days Since Last Visit','Store','Status'];
  const rows=_custData.map(c=>[`"${c.name.replace(/"/g,'""')}"`,c.whatsapp||'',c.visitCount,c.totalSpend,c.avgSpendPerVisit||0,`"${(c.favouriteProduct||'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`,c.firstVisit||'',c.lastVisit||'',c.daysSinceLastVisit!==null?c.daysSinceLastVisit:'',`"${(c.store||'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`,c.isReturning?'Returning':c.isNewThisMonth?'New':'One-time'].join(','));
  const csv=[headers.join(','),...rows].join('\n');
  const blob=new Blob(['\uFEFF'+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url; a.download=`StarTrack_Customers_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.csv`;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  toast(`${_custData.length} customers exported`,'ok');
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   TRANSFER STOCK
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
var _trFromProds=[], _trToProds=[];

function initTransfer(){
  const stores=user.availableStores||[user.store];
  const fromSel=$('trFromStore'), toSel=$('trToStore');
  fromSel.innerHTML=''; toSel.innerHTML='';
  stores.forEach(s=>fromSel.add(new Option(s,s)));
  stores.forEach(s=>toSel.add(new Option(s,s)));
  // Default to different stores if possible
  if(stores.length>=2){
    fromSel.value=stores[0];
    toSel.value=stores[1];
  }
  $('trFromProduct').innerHTML='<option value="">— Select product —</option>';
  $('trToProduct').innerHTML='<option value="">— Select product —</option>';
  $('trFromInfo').classList.add('hidden');
  $('trToInfo').classList.add('hidden');
  $('trFromQty').value='';
  $('trToQty').value='';
  loadTransferProducts('from');
  loadTransferProducts('to');
}

function loadTransferProducts(side){
  const storeSel=side==='from'?$('trFromStore'):$('trToStore');
  const prodSel=side==='from'?$('trFromProduct'):$('trToProduct');
  const store=storeSel.value;
  if(!store) return;
  const applyProds=prods=>{
    const list=Array.isArray(prods)?prods:[];
    if(side==='from') _trFromProds=list;
    else _trToProds=list;
    prodSel.innerHTML='<option value="">— Select product —</option>';
    list.forEach(p=>prodSel.add(new Option(p.name+' (Stock: '+p.stock+')',p.id)));
    onTransferProduct();
  };
  if(_allProdsCache&&_allProdsCache[store]&&_allProdsCache[store].length){
    applyProds(_allProdsCache[store]);
    return;
  }
  prodSel.innerHTML='<option value="">Loading…</option>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(prods=>{
      if(!prods||prods.unauthorized){toast('Session expired — please sign in again','er');return;}
      _allProdsCache[store]=Array.isArray(prods)?prods:[];
      applyProds(prods);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(e=>toast('Error loading products: '+(e&&e.message||'connection error'),'er'))
    .getProducts({store,_token:_sessionToken});
}

function onTransferProduct(){
  // Show current stock info for source
  const fromId=$('trFromProduct').value;
  const toId=$('trToProduct').value;
  const fromInfo=$('trFromInfo'), toInfo=$('trToInfo');
  if(fromId){
    const p=_trFromProds.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(fromId));
    if(p){ fromInfo.innerText='Current stock: '+p.stock+' · Price: ₦'+(p.price||0).toLocaleString(); fromInfo.classList.remove('hidden'); }
  } else { fromInfo.classList.add('hidden'); }
  if(toId){
    const p=_trToProds.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(toId));
    if(p){ toInfo.innerText='Current stock: '+p.stock+' · Price: ₦'+(p.price||0).toLocaleString(); toInfo.classList.remove('hidden'); }
  } else { toInfo.classList.add('hidden'); }
}

function doTransfer(){
  const fromStore=$('trFromStore').value;
  const fromProductID=$('trFromProduct').value;
  const fromQty=Number($('trFromQty').value)||0;
  const toStore=$('trToStore').value;
  const toProductID=$('trToProduct').value;
  const toQty=Number($('trToQty').value)||0;

  if(!fromStore||!toStore) return toast('Select both stores','er');
  if(fromStore===toStore) return toast('Source and destination stores must be different','er');
  if(!fromProductID) return toast('Select a source product','er');
  if(!toProductID) return toast('Select a destination product','er');
  if(fromQty<1) return toast('Enter quantity to deduct','er');
  if(toQty<1) return toast('Enter quantity to add','er');

  const fp=_trFromProds.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(fromProductID));
  if(fp&&fromQty>fp.stock) return toast('Cannot deduct more than available stock ('+fp.stock+')','er');

  const fromName=fp?fp.name:fromProductID;
  const toName=(_trToProds.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(toProductID))||{name:toProductID}).name;

  showModal('Confirm Transfer',`
    <div style="background:var(--rglow);border:1px solid rgba(224,36,36,.15);border-radius:var(--r2);padding:12px;margin-bottom:10px">
      <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--rd1);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px;margin-bottom:5px">Deduct from ${fromStore}</div>
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${fromQty} × ${fromName}</div>
    </div>
    <div style="text-align:center;color:var(--b1);margin:4px 0"><span class="material-icons-round">arrow_downward</span></div>
    <div style="background:var(--gglow);border:1px solid rgba(15,159,118,.15);border-radius:var(--r2);padding:12px">
      <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--gn1);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px;margin-bottom:5px">Add to ${toStore}</div>
      <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--t1)">${toQty} × ${toName}</div>
    </div>
  `,[
    {label:'Cancel',fn:closeModal},
    {label:'<span class="material-icons-round" style="font-size:15px">swap_horiz</span> Transfer',c:'btn-p',fn:()=>{
      closeModal();
      showLoading('Transferring stock…');
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r=>{
          hideLoading();
          if(r&&r.msg) toast(r.msg,'ok');
          $('trFromQty').value='';
          $('trToQty').value='';
          loadTransferProducts('from');
          loadTransferProducts('to');
          loadProds(); // refresh sales grid
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e=>{ hideLoading(); toast('Error: '+e.message,'er'); })
        .transferStock({
          fromStore, fromProductID, fromQty,
          toStore, toProductID, toQty,
          performedBy: user.name,
          _token: _sessionToken
        });
    }}
  ]);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   BARCODE SCANNER
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
var _scanStream = null;
var _scanAnimFrame = null;
var _scanCanvas = null;
var _scanCtx = null;
var _scanBuf = '';
var _scanBufTimer = null;

function openBarcodeScanner(){
  $('scannerInput').value = '';
  $('scanResult').classList.add('hidden');
  openOV('scannerOV');
  // Auto-focus the input for USB scanners
  setTimeout(() => $('scannerInput').focus(), 200);
  // Listen for keyboard scanner (fires rapid keystrokes)
  document.addEventListener('keydown', _kbScanCapture);
}

function closeScanner(){
  stopCamera();
  closeOV('scannerOV');
  document.removeEventListener('keydown', _kbScanCapture);
  _scanBuf = '';
  if(_scanBufTimer) clearTimeout(_scanBufTimer);
}

// Keyboard wedge scanner capture (USB/BT scanners simulate keyboard)
function _kbScanCapture(e){
  if(!$('scannerOV').classList.contains('on')) return;
  // Ignore if typing in a normal input
  if(document.activeElement === $('scannerInput')) return;
  if(e.key === 'Enter'){
    if(_scanBuf.length > 2) processScan(_scanBuf);
    _scanBuf = '';
    if(_scanBufTimer) clearTimeout(_scanBufTimer);
    return;
  }
  if(e.key.length === 1){
    _scanBuf += e.key;
    if(_scanBufTimer) clearTimeout(_scanBufTimer);
    // If no keydown within 100ms, treat as complete scan
    _scanBufTimer = setTimeout(()=>{
      if(_scanBuf.length > 2) processScan(_scanBuf);
      _scanBuf = '';
    }, 100);
  }
}

function onScannerInput(val){
  // Debounce — USB scanners paste full code instantly
  if(_scanBufTimer) clearTimeout(_scanBufTimer);
  _scanBufTimer = setTimeout(()=>{
    if(val && val.length > 2) processScan(val);
  }, 120);
}

function processScan(code){
  code = (code||'').trim();
  if(!code) return;
  if(_scanBufTimer){ clearTimeout(_scanBufTimer); _scanBufTimer=null; }
  $('scannerInput').value = '';
  // Find product by ID
  const allProds = [...products, ...(wbProds||[])];
  const found = allProds.find(p => 
    String(p.id).trim() === code || 
    String(p.id).trim().toUpperCase() === code.toUpperCase()
  );
  $('scanResultCode').innerText = code;
  $('scanResult').classList.remove('hidden');
  if(found){
    $('scanResultName').innerText = '✅ ' + found.name + ' — ₦' + Number(found.price||0).toLocaleString();
    $('scanResultName').style.color = 'var(--gn1)';
    if(found.stock <= 0){
      toast('⚠️ ' + found.name + ' is out of stock','wa');
    } else {
      // Add to cart and close
      setTimeout(()=>{
        if(!isWB()){
          addToCart(found);
          closeScanner();
          goPage('sales');
        }
      }, 600);
    }
  } else {
    $('scanResultName').innerText = '❌ No product found with ID: ' + code;
    $('scanResultName').style.color = 'var(--rd1)';
    toast('No product found for barcode: ' + code, 'er');
  }
}

async function startCamera(){
  toast('Camera scanning is blocked by Google Apps Script — use the USB/Bluetooth scanner input field above instead','wa');
  return;
  try {
    _scanStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
      video: { facingMode: 'environment', width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 720 } }
    });
    const video = $('scannerVideo');
    video.srcObject = _scanStream;
    video.style.display = 'block';
    await video.play();
    $('scannerCamPlaceholder').style.display = 'none';
    $('scanLine').classList.remove('hidden');
    $('camStartBtn').classList.add('hidden');
    $('camStopBtn').classList.remove('hidden');

    // Load ZXing barcode decoder from CDN
    if(!window.ZXing){
      await new Promise((res,rej)=>{
        const s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/zxing-js/0.20.0-beta.2/umd/index.min.js';
        s.onload = res; s.onerror = rej;
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
    }

    _scanCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    _scanCtx = _scanCanvas.getContext('2d');
    const hints = new Map();
    const formats = [
      ZXing.BarcodeFormat.CODE_128,
      ZXing.BarcodeFormat.CODE_39,
      ZXing.BarcodeFormat.EAN_13,
      ZXing.BarcodeFormat.QR_CODE
    ];
    hints.set(ZXing.DecodeHintType.POSSIBLE_FORMATS, formats);
    window._zxingReader = new ZXing.MultiFormatReader();
    window._zxingReader.setHints(hints);
    _scanLoop();
    toast('Camera started — point at barcode','ok');
  } catch(e){
    toast('Camera error: ' + e.message,'er');
  }
}

function _scanLoop(){
  const video = $('scannerVideo');
  if(!video || !_scanStream) return;
  if(video.readyState === video.HAVE_ENOUGH_DATA){
    _scanCanvas.width = video.videoWidth;
    _scanCanvas.height = video.videoHeight;
    _scanCtx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
    try {
      const imgData = _scanCtx.getImageData(0, 0, _scanCanvas.width, _scanCanvas.height);
      const lum = new ZXing.RGBLuminanceSource(imgData.data, _scanCanvas.width, _scanCanvas.height);
      const bin = new ZXing.HybridBinarizer(lum);
      const bitmap = new ZXing.BinaryBitmap(bin);
      const result = window._zxingReader.decode(bitmap);
      if(result && result.getText()){
        processScan(result.getText());
        stopCamera();
        return;
      }
    } catch(e){
      // DecodeError is normal when no barcode in frame — keep looping
    }
  }
  _scanAnimFrame = requestAnimationFrame(_scanLoop);
}

function stopCamera(){
  if(_scanAnimFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(_scanAnimFrame);
  if(_scanStream) _scanStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
  _scanStream = null;
  const video = $('scannerVideo');
  if(video){ video.srcObject = null; video.style.display = 'none'; }
  const ph = $('scannerCamPlaceholder');
  if(ph) ph.style.display = '';
  const sl = $('scanLine');
  if(sl) sl.classList.add('hidden');
  const cs = $('camStartBtn'); if(cs) cs.classList.remove('hidden');
  const cx = $('camStopBtn'); if(cx) cx.classList.add('hidden');
}

// Expose scanner functions
Object.assign(window, {
  openBarcodeScanner, closeScanner, startCamera, stopCamera,
  processScan, onScannerInput, _kbScanCapture
});

</script>
</body>
</html>
